plugging  $\sum_{i=3}^{k} \pi_{\theta}(i) = 1 - \pi_{\theta}(1) - \pi_{\theta}(2)$  into eq. (116) and rearranging the resulting expression we get

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta \left(a ^ {*}\right)} - \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta (a)} \geq \pi_ {\theta} (1) \cdot \left(1 + \frac {\delta}{k}\right) - \left(1 - \frac {\delta}{k}\right) + \pi_ {\theta} (2) \cdot \left(1 - \frac {\delta}{k}\right) (127) \\ \geq \pi_ {\theta} (2) \cdot \left(1 - \frac {\delta}{k}\right) \geq 0, \quad (\text {u s i n g} \theta \in \mathcal {n} _ {c}, \text {i . e . ,} \pi_ {\theta} (1) \geq c / (c + 1)) (128) \\ \end{array}
$$

which implies that  $\theta \in \mathcal{r}_1(a)$ , thus, finishing the proof.

claim c) we claim that  $\pi_{\theta_t}(a^*) \to 1$  as  $t \to \infty$ . for this, we wish to use the asymptotic convergence results of agarwal et al. (2019, theorem 5.1), which states this, but the stepsize there is  $\eta \leq 1/5$  while here we have  $\eta = 2/5$ . we claim that their asymptotic result still hold with the larger  $\eta$ . in fact, the restriction on  $\eta$  comes from that they can only prove the ascent lemma (lemma 18) for  $\eta \leq 1/5$ . other than this, their proof does not rely on the choice of  $\eta$ . since we can prove the ascent lemma with  $\eta \leq 2/5$  (and in particular with  $\eta = 2/5$ ), their result continues to hold even with  $\eta = 2/5$ .

thus,  $\pi_{\theta_t}(a^*) \to 1$  as  $t \to \infty$ . hence, there exists  $t_0 \geq 1$ , such that  $\pi_{\theta_{t_0}}(a^*) \geq \frac{c}{c + 1}$ , which means  $\theta_{t_0} \in \mathcal{n}_c \subset \mathcal{r}_1$ . according to the first part in our proof, i.e., once  $\theta_t$  is in  $\mathcal{r}_1$ , following gradient update  $\theta_{t+1}$  will be in  $\mathcal{r}_1$ , and  $\pi_{\theta_t}(a^*)$  is increasing in  $\mathcal{r}_1$ , we have  $\inf_t \pi_{\theta_t}(a^*) = \min_{1 \leq t \leq t_0} \pi_{\theta_t}(a^*)$ .  $t_0$  depends on initialization and  $c$ , which only depends on the problem.

proposition 2. for any initialization there exist  $t_0 \geq 1$  such that for any  $t \geq t_0$ ,  $t \mapsto \pi_{\theta_t}(a^*)$  is increasing. in particular, when  $\pi_{\theta_1}$  is the uniform distribution,  $t_0 = 1$ .

proof. we have  $t_0 = \min \{t \geq 1 : \pi_{\theta_t}(a^*) \geq \frac{c}{c + 1}\}$ , where  $c = \frac{k}{2\delta} \cdot \left(1 - \frac{\delta}{k}\right)$  in the proof for lemma 5 satisfies for any  $t \geq t_0$ ,  $t \mapsto \pi_{\theta_t}(a^*)$  is increasing.

now, let  $\theta_{1}$  be so that  $\pi_{\theta_1}$  is the uniform distribution. we show that  $t_0 = 1$ . recall from claim 1 that  $\mathcal{r}_2$  is the region where the probability of the optimal action exceeds that of the suboptimal ones and  $\mathcal{r}_1$  is the region where the gradient of the optimal action exceeds those of the suboptimal ones and that  $\mathcal{r}_2 \subset \mathcal{r}_1$ . clearly,  $\theta_{1} \in \mathcal{r}_{2}$  and hence also  $\theta_{1} \in \mathcal{r}_{1}$ . now, by part a) of claim 1,  $\mathcal{r}_1$  is invariant under the updates, showing that  $t_0 = 1$  holds as required.

theorem 2 (arbitrary initialization). using update 1 with  $\eta = 2/5$ , for all  $t \geq 1$ ,

$$
\left(\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) ^ {\top} r \leq 5 / \left(c ^ {2} \cdot t\right), \tag {129}
$$

where  $c = \inf_{t\geq 1}\pi_{\theta_t}(a^*) > 0$  is a constant that depends on  $r$  and  $\theta_{1}$ , but it does not depend on the time  $t$ .

proof. according to lemmas 4 and 5, the claim immediately holds, with  $c = \inf_{t\geq 1}\pi_{\theta_t}(a^*) > 0$ .

theorem 3 (uniform initialization). using update 1 with  $\eta = 2 / 5$  and  $\pi_{\theta_1}(a) = 1 / k, \forall a$ , for all  $t \geq 1$ ,

$$
\left(\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) ^ {\top} r \leq 5 k ^ {2} / t, \quad \text {a n d} \tag {130}
$$

$$
\sum_ {t = 1} ^ {t} \left(\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) ^ {\top} r \leq \min  \left\{k \sqrt {5 t}, 5 k ^ {2} \log t + 1 \right\}. \tag {131}
$$

proof. since the initial policy is uniform policy,  $\pi_{\theta_1}(a^*) \geq 1 / k$ . according to proposition 2, for all  $t \geq t_0 = 1$ ,  $t \mapsto \pi_{\theta_t}(a^*)$  is increasing. hence, we have  $\pi_{\theta_t}(a^*) \geq 1 / k$ ,  $\forall t \geq 1$ , and  $c_t = \min_{1 \leq s \leq t} \pi_{\theta_s}(a^*) \geq 1 / k$ . according to lemma 4,

$$
\left(\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) ^ {\top} r \leq \frac {5}{c _ {t} ^ {2}} \cdot \frac {1}{t}, \tag {132}
$$

we have  $(\pi^{*} - \pi_{\theta_{t}})^{\top}r\leq 5k^{2} / t,\forall t\geq 1$  . the remaining results follow from eq. (70) and  $c_{t}\geq 1 / k$

lemma 6. let  $r(1) > r(2) > r(3)$ . then,  $a^{*} = 1$  and  $\inf_{t\geq 1}\pi_{\theta_t}(1) = \min_{1\leq t\leq t_0}\pi_{\theta_t}(1)$ , where

$$
t _ {0} = \min  \left\{t \geq 1: \frac {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (1)}{\pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (3)} \geq \frac {r (2) - r (3)}{2 \cdot (r (1) - r (2))} \right\}. \tag {133}
$$

in general, for  $k$ -action bandit cases, let  $r(1) > r(2) > \dots > r(k)$ , we have,

$$
t _ {0} = \min  \left\{t \geq 1: \pi_ {\theta} (1) \geq \frac {\sum_ {j \neq 1 , j \neq i} \pi_ {\theta} (j) \cdot (r (i) - r (j))}{2 \cdot (r (1) - r (i))}, \text {f o r a l l} i \in \{2, 3, \dots k - 1 \} \right\}. \tag {134}
$$

proof. 3-action case. recall the definition of  $\mathcal{r}_1$  from the proof for lemma 5:

$$
\mathcal {r} _ {1} = \left\{\theta : \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta (a ^ {*})} \geq \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta (a)}, \forall a \neq a ^ {*} \right\}. \tag {135}
$$

by part a) of claim 1, it suffices to prove that  $\theta \in \mathcal{r}_1$ . thus, our goal is to show that any  $\theta$  such that  $\frac{\pi_{\theta}(1)}{\pi_{\theta}(3)} \geq \frac{r(2) - r(3)}{2 \cdot (r(1) - r(2))}$  is in fact an element of  $\mathcal{r}_1$ . suppose  $\frac{\pi_{\theta}(1)}{\pi_{\theta}(3)} \geq \frac{r(2) - r(3)}{2 \cdot (r(1) - r(2))}$ . there are two cases.

case (a): if  $\frac{\pi_{\theta}(1)}{\pi_{\theta}(3)} \geq \frac{r(2) - r(3)}{r(1) - r(2)}$ , then we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} r (2) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r = - \pi_ {\theta} (1) \cdot (r (1) - r (2)) + \pi_ {\theta} (3) \cdot (r (2) - r (3)) (136) \\ = \pi_ {\theta} (3) \cdot (r (1) - r (2)) \cdot \left[ - \frac {\pi_ {\theta} (1)}{\pi_ {\theta} (3)} + \frac {r (2) - r (3)}{r (1) - r (2)} \right] (137) \\ \leq 0, \quad \left(\frac {\pi_ {\theta} (1)}{\pi_ {\theta} (3)} \geq \frac {r (2) - r (3)}{r (1) - r (2)}\right) (138) \\ \end{array}
$$

which implies,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta (1)} - \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta (2)} = \pi_ {\theta} (1) \cdot \left(r (1) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) - \pi_ {\theta} (2) \cdot \left(r (2) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) (139) \\ \geq 0 - 0 = 0. \quad \left(r (1) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r > 0\right) (140) \\ \end{array}
$$

note that since  $r(1) > \pi_{\theta}^{\top}r$ , and  $r(3) < \pi_{\theta}^{\top}r$ , we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta (1)} - \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta (3)} = \pi_ {\theta} (1) \cdot \left(r (1) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) - \pi_ {\theta} (3) \cdot \left(r (3) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) (141) \\ \geq 0 - 0 = 0. (142) \\ \end{array}
$$

therefore we have  $\frac{d\pi_{\theta}^{\top}r}{d\theta(1)}\geq \frac{d\pi_{\theta}^{\top}r}{d\theta(2)}$  and  $\frac{d\pi_{\theta}^{\top}r}{d\theta(1)}\geq \frac{d\pi_{\theta}^{\top}r}{d\theta(3)}$  , i.e.,  $\theta \in \mathcal{r}_1$

case (b): if  $\frac{r(2) - r(3)}{2 \cdot (r(1) - r(2))} \leq \frac{\pi_{\theta}(1)}{\pi_{\theta}(3)} < \frac{r(2) - r(3)}{r(1) - r(2)}$ , then we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta (1)} - \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta (2)} = \pi_ {\theta} (1) \cdot \left(r (1) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) - \pi_ {\theta} (2) \cdot \left(r (2) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) (143) \\ = 2 \cdot \pi_ {\theta} (1) \cdot \left(r (1) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) + \pi_ {\theta} (3) \cdot \left(r (3) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) (144) \\ \geq \pi_ {\theta} (3) \cdot \left[ \frac {r (2) - r (3)}{r (1) - r (2)} \cdot \left(r (1) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) + \left(r (3) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) \right] \quad \left(\frac {\pi_ {\theta} (1)}{\pi_ {\theta} (3)} \geq \frac {r (2) - r (3)}{2 \cdot (r (1) - r (2))}\right) (145) \\ \geq \pi_ {\theta} (3) \cdot \left[ \frac {r (2) - r (3)}{r (1) - r (2)} \cdot (r (1) - r (2)) + \left(r (3) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) \right] (146) \\ = \pi_ {\theta} (3) \cdot \left(r (2) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) \geq 0, (147) \\ \end{array}
$$

where the second equation is according to

$$
\left. \pi_ {\theta} (1) \cdot \left(r (1) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) + \pi_ {\theta} (2) \cdot \left(r (2) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) + \pi_ {\theta} (3) \cdot \left(r (3) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) = \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r = 0, \right. \tag {148}
$$

and the second inequality is because of

$$
\begin{array}{l} r (1) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r = (1 - \pi_ {\theta} (1)) \cdot r (1) - (\pi_ {\theta} (2) \cdot r (2) + \pi_ {\theta} (3) \cdot r (3)) (149) \\ = \pi_ {\theta} (2) \cdot (r (1) - r (2)) + \pi_ {\theta} (3) \cdot (r (1) - r (3)) (150) \\ = \left(\pi_ {\theta} (2) + \pi_ {\theta} (3)\right) \cdot \left(r (1) - r (2)\right) + \pi_ {\theta} (3) \cdot \left(r (2) - r (3)\right) (151) \\ > \left(\pi_ {\theta} (2) + \pi_ {\theta} (3)\right) \cdot (r (1) - r (2)) + \pi_ {\theta} (1) \cdot (r (1) - r (2)) \quad \left(\frac {\pi_ {\theta} (1)}{\pi_ {\theta} (3)} <   \frac {r (2) - r (3)}{r (1) - r (2)}\right) (152) \\ = r (1) - r (2), (153) \\ \end{array}
$$

and the last inequality is from

$$
\begin{array}{l} r (2) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r = \pi_ {\theta} (3) \cdot (r (1) - r (2)) \cdot \left[ - \frac {\pi_ {\theta} (1)}{\pi_ {\theta} (3)} + \frac {r (2) - r (3)}{r (1) - r (2)} \right] (154) \\ > 0. \quad \left(\frac {\pi_ {\theta} (1)}{\pi_ {\theta} (3)} <   \frac {r (2) - r (3)}{r (1) - r (2)}\right) (155) \\ \end{array}
$$

now we have  $\frac{d\pi_{\theta}^{\top}r}{d\theta(1)}\geq \frac{d\pi_{\theta}^{\top}r}{d\theta(2)}$  according to eq. (141), we have  $\frac{d\pi_{\theta}^{\top}r}{d\theta(1)}\geq \frac{d\pi_{\theta}^{\top}r}{d\theta(3)}$  . therefore we have  $\theta \in \mathcal{r}_1$

$k$ -action case. suppose for each action  $i \in \{2, 3, \ldots, k-1\}$ ,  $\pi_{\theta}(1) \geq \frac{\sum_{j \neq 1, j \neq i} \pi_{\theta}(j) \cdot (r(i) - r(j))}{2 \cdot (r(1) - r(i))}$ . there are two cases.

case (a): if  $\pi_{\theta}(1) \geq \frac{\sum_{j \neq 1, j \neq i} \pi_{\theta}(j) \cdot (r(i) - r(j))}{r(1) - r(i)}$ , then we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} r (i) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r = - \pi_ {\theta} (1) \cdot (r (1) - r (i)) + \sum_ {j \neq 1, j \neq i} \pi_ {\theta} (j) \cdot (r (i) - r (j)) (156) \\ \leq 0, \quad \left(\pi_ {\theta} (1) \geq \frac {\sum_ {j \neq 1 , j \neq i} \pi_ {\theta} (j) \cdot (r (i) - r (j))}{r (1) - r (i)}\right) (157) \\ \end{array}
$$

which implies, for all  $i\in \{2,3,\ldots k - 1\}$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta (1)} - \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta (i)} = \pi_ {\theta} (1) \cdot \left(r (1) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) - \pi_ {\theta} (i) \cdot \left(r (i) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) (158) \\ \geq 0 - 0 = 0. \quad \left(r (1) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r > 0\right) (159) \\ \end{array}
$$

similar with eq. (141), since  $r(1) > \pi_{\theta}^{\top}r$ , and  $r(k) < \pi_{\theta}^{\top}r$ , we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta (1)} - \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta (k)} = \pi_ {\theta} (1) \cdot \left(r (1) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) - \pi_ {\theta} (k) \cdot \left(r (k) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) (160) \\ \geq 0 - 0 = 0. (161) \\ \end{array}
$$

therefore we have  $\frac{d\pi_{\theta}^{\top}r}{d\theta(1)}\geq \frac{d\pi_{\theta}^{\top}r}{d\theta(i)}$ , for all  $i\in \{2,3,\ldots k\}$ , i.e.,  $\theta \in \mathcal{r}_1$

case (b): if  $\frac{\sum_{j\neq 1,j\neq i}\pi_{\theta}(j)\cdot(r(i) - r(j))}{2\cdot(r(1) - r(i))} \leq \pi_{\theta}(1) < \frac{\sum_{j\neq 1,j\neq i}\pi_{\theta}(j)\cdot(r(i) - r(j))}{r(1) - r(i)}$ , then we have, for all  $i \in \{2,3,\ldots,k-1\}$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta (1)} - \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta (i)} = \pi_ {\theta} (1) \cdot \left(r (1) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) - \pi_ {\theta} (i) \cdot \left(r (i) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) (162) \\ = 2 \cdot \pi_ {\theta} (1) \cdot \left(r (1) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) + \sum_ {j \neq 1, j \neq i} \pi_ {\theta} (j) \cdot \left(r (j) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) (163) \\ \geq \frac {\sum_ {j \neq 1 , j \neq i} \pi_ {\theta} (j) \cdot \left(r (i) - r (j)\right)}{r (1) - r (i)} \cdot \left(r (1) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) + \sum_ {j \neq 1, j \neq i} \pi_ {\theta} (j) \cdot \left(r (j) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) (164) \\ \geq \frac {\sum_ {j \neq 1 , j \neq i} \pi_ {\theta} (j) \cdot (r (i) - r (j))}{r (1) - r (i)} \cdot (r (1) - r (i)) + \sum_ {j \neq 1, j \neq i} \pi_ {\theta} (j) \cdot (r (j) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r) (165) \\ = \sum_ {j \neq 1, j \neq i} \pi_ {\theta} (j) \cdot \left(r (i) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) \geq 0, (166) \\ \end{array}
$$

where the second equation is according to

$$
\pi_ {\theta} (1) \cdot \left(r (1) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) + \pi_ {\theta} (i) \cdot \left(r (i) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) + \sum_ {j \neq 1, j \neq i} \pi_ {\theta} (j) \cdot \left(r (j) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) = \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r = 0, \tag {167}
$$

and the first inequality is by  $r(1) - \pi_{\theta}^{\top}r > 0$  and,

$$
\pi_ {\theta} (1) \geq \frac {\sum_ {j \neq 1 , j \neq i} \pi_ {\theta} (j) \cdot (r (i) - r (j))}{2 \cdot (r (1) - r (i))}, \tag {168}
$$

and the second inequality is because of

$$
\begin{array}{l} r (1) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r = \pi_ {\theta} (i) \cdot (r (1) - r (i)) + \sum_ {j \neq 1, j \neq i} \pi_ {\theta} (j) \cdot (r (1) - r (j)) (169) \\ = \sum_ {j \neq 1} \pi_ {\theta} (j) \cdot (r (1) - r (i)) + \sum_ {j \neq 1, j \neq i} \pi_ {\theta} (j) \cdot (r (i) - r (j)) (170) \\ > \sum_ {j \neq 1} \pi_ {\theta} (j) \cdot (r (1) - r (i)) + \pi_ {\theta} (1) \cdot (r (1) - r (i)) \quad \left(\frac {\sum_ {j \neq 1 , j \neq i} \pi_ {\theta} (j) \cdot (r (i) - r (j))}{r (1) - r (i)} > \pi_ {\theta} (1)\right) (171) \\ = r (1) - r (i), (172) \\ \end{array}
$$

and the last inequality is from  $\frac{\sum_{j\neq 1,j\neq i}\pi_{\theta}(j)\cdot(r(i) - r(j))}{r(1) - r(i)} >\pi_{\theta}(1) > 0$  and,

$$
\begin{array}{l} r (i) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r = - \pi_ {\theta} (1) \cdot (r (1) - r (i)) + \sum_ {j \neq 1, j \neq i} \pi_ {\theta} (j) \cdot (r (i) - r (j)) (173) \\ > 0. \quad \left(\pi_ {\theta} (1) <   \frac {\sum_ {j \neq 1 , j \neq i} \pi_ {\theta} (j) \cdot (r (i) - r (j))}{r (1) - r (i)}\right) (174) \\ \end{array}
$$

now we have  $\frac{d\pi_{\theta}^{\top}r}{d\theta(1)}\geq \frac{d\pi_{\theta}^{\top}r}{d\theta(i)}$  for all  $i\in \{2,3,\ldots k - 1\}$ . according to eq. (160), we have  $\frac{d\pi_{\theta}^{\top}r}{d\theta(1)}\geq \frac{d\pi_{\theta}^{\top}r}{d\theta(k)}$ . therefore we have  $\theta \in \mathcal{r}_1$ .

# a.1.3. proofs for softmax parametrization in mdps

lemma 7 (smoothness).  $v^{\pi_{\theta}}(\rho)$  is  $8 / (1 - \gamma)^{3}$ -smooth.

proof. see agarwal et al. (2019, lemma e.4). our proof is for completeness. denote  $\theta_{\alpha} = \theta + \alpha u$ , where  $\alpha \in \mathbb{r}$  and  $u \in \mathbb{r}^{sa}$ . for any  $s \in s$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {a} \left| \frac {\partial \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s)}{\partial \alpha} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} = \sum_ {a} \left| \left\langle \frac {\partial \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s)}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0}, \frac {\partial \theta_ {\alpha}}{\partial \alpha} \right\rangle (175) \\ = \sum_ {a} \left| \left\langle \frac {\partial \pi_ {\theta} (a | s)}{\partial \theta}, u \right\rangle \right|. (176) \\ \end{array}
$$

since  $\frac{\partial\pi_{\theta}(a|s)}{\partial\theta(s',\cdot)} = 0$  for  $s^{\prime}\neq s$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left. \sum_ {a} \left| \frac {\partial \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s)}{\partial \alpha} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \right| = \sum_ {a} \left| \left\langle \frac {\partial \pi_ {\theta} (a | s)}{\partial \theta (s , \cdot)}, u (s, \cdot) \right\rangle \right| (177) \\ = \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot | u (s, a) - \pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s) ^ {\top} u (s, \cdot) | (178) \\ \leq \max  _ {a} | u (s, a) | + | \pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s) ^ {\top} u (s, \cdot) | \leq 2 \cdot \| u \| _ {2}. (179) \\ \end{array}
$$

similarly,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left. \sum_ {a} \left| \frac {\partial^ {2} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \right| = \sum_ {a} \left| \right.\left\langle \right. \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha}} \left\{\frac {\partial \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s)}{\partial \alpha} \right\}\left. \right| _ {\alpha = 0}, \frac {\partial \theta_ {\alpha}}{\partial \alpha} \left. \right\rangle\left. \right| (180) \\ = \sum_ {a} \left| \right.\left\langle \right. \frac {\partial^ {2} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s)}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha} ^ {2}} \left. \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \frac {\partial \theta_ {\alpha}}{\partial \alpha}, \frac {\partial \theta_ {\alpha}}{\partial \alpha} \left. \right\rangle\left. \right| (181) \\ = \sum_ {a} \left| \left\langle \frac {\partial^ {2} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s)}{\partial \theta^ {2} (s , \cdot)} u (s, \cdot), u (s, \cdot) \right\rangle \right|. (182) \\ \end{array}
$$

let  $s(a,\theta) = \frac{\partial^2\pi_\theta(a|s)}{\partial\theta^2(s,.)}\in \mathbb{r}^{a\times a}$ .  $\forall i,j\in [a]$ , the value of  $s(a,\theta)$  is,

$$
\begin{array}{l} s _ {i, j} = \frac {\partial \left\{\delta_ {i a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) - \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \pi_ {\theta} (i | s) \right\}}{\partial \theta (s , j)} (183) \\ = \delta_ {i a} \cdot \left[ \delta_ {j a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) - \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \pi_ {\theta} (j | s) \right] - \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \left[ \delta_ {i j} \pi_ {\theta} (j | s) - \pi_ {\theta} (i | s) \pi_ {\theta} (j | s) \right] - \pi_ {\theta} (i | s) \cdot \left[ \delta_ {j a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) - \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \pi_ {\theta} (j | s) \right], (184) \\ \end{array}
$$

where the  $\delta$  notation is as defined in eq. (50). then we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \left\langle \frac {\partial^ {2} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s)}{\partial \theta^ {2} (s , \cdot)} u (s, \cdot), u (s, \cdot) \right\rangle \right| = \left| \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {a} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {a} s _ {i, j} u (s, i) u (s, j) \right| (185) \\ = \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \left| u (s, a) ^ {2} - 2 \cdot u (s, a) \cdot \pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s) ^ {\top} u (s, \cdot) - \pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s) ^ {\top} (u (s, \cdot) \odot u (s, \cdot)) + 2 \cdot \left(\pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s) ^ {\top} u (s, \cdot)\right) ^ {2} \right|. (186) \\ \end{array}
$$

therefore we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {a} \left| \frac {\partial^ {2} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \mid \leq \max  _ {a} \left\{u (s, a) ^ {2} + 2 \cdot \left| u (s, a) \cdot \pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s) ^ {\top} u (s, \cdot) \right| \right\} + \pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s) ^ {\top} (u (s, \cdot) \odot u (s, \cdot)) + 2 \cdot \left(\pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s) ^ {\top} u (s, \cdot)\right) ^ {2} (187) \\ \leq \| u (s, \cdot) \| _ {2} ^ {2} + 2 \cdot \| u (s, \cdot) \| _ {2} ^ {2} + \| u (s, \cdot) \| _ {2} ^ {2} + 2 \cdot \| u (s, \cdot) \| _ {2} ^ {2} \leq 6 \cdot \| u \| _ {2} ^ {2}. (188) \\ \end{array}
$$

define  $p(\alpha)\in \mathbb{r}^{s\times s}$  , where  $\forall (s,s^{\prime})$

$$
\left[ p (\alpha) \right] _ {(s, s ^ {\prime})} = \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s) \cdot \mathcal {p} \left(s ^ {\prime} \mid s, a\right). \tag {189}
$$

the derivative w.r.t.  $\alpha$  is

$$
\left[ \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg | _ {(s, s ^ {\prime})} = \sum_ {a} \left[ \frac {\partial \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s)}{\partial \alpha} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg ] \cdot \mathcal {p} \left(s ^ {\prime} | s, a\right). \tag {190}
$$

for any vector  $x\in \mathbb{r}^s$  , we have

$$
\left[ \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} x \bigg | _ {(s)} = \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} \sum_ {a} \left[ \frac {\partial \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s)}{\partial \alpha} \big | _ {\alpha = 0} \right] \cdot \mathcal {p} \left(s ^ {\prime} | s, a\right) \cdot x \left(s ^ {\prime}\right). \tag {191}
$$

the  $\ell_{\infty}$  norm is upper bounded as

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} x \bigg \| _ {\infty} = \max  _ {s} \left| \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} \sum_ {a} \left[ \frac {\partial \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s)}{\partial \alpha} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \right] \cdot \mathcal {p} \left(s ^ {\prime} | s, a\right) \cdot x \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) \bigg | (192) \\ \leq \max  _ {s} \sum_ {a} \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} \mathcal {p} \left(s ^ {\prime} \mid s, a\right) \cdot \left| \frac {\partial \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a \mid s)}{\partial \alpha} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg | \cdot \| x \| _ {\infty} (193) \\ = \max  _ {s} \sum_ {a} \left| \frac {\partial \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s)}{\partial \alpha} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \cdot \| x \| _ {\infty} (194) \\ \leq 2 \cdot \| u \| _ {2} \cdot \| x \| _ {\infty}. \quad (\text {b y e q . (1 7 7)}) (195) \\ \end{array}
$$

similarly, taking second derivative w.r.t.  $\alpha$

$$
\left[ \frac {\partial^ {2} p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \big | _ {(s, s ^ {\prime})} = \sum_ {a} \left[ \frac {\partial^ {2} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \big | _ {\alpha = 0} \right] \cdot \mathcal {p} \left(s ^ {\prime} \mid s, a\right). \tag {196}
$$

the  $\ell_{\infty}$  norm is upper bounded as

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \frac {\partial^ {2} p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} x \| _ {\infty} = \max  _ {s} \left| \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} \sum_ {a} \left[ \frac {\partial^ {2} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \right] \cdot \mathcal {p} \left(s ^ {\prime} | s, a\right) \cdot x \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) (197) \\ \leq \max  _ {s} \sum_ {a} \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} \mathcal {p} \left(s ^ {\prime} | s, a\right) \cdot \left| \frac {\partial^ {2} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg | \cdot \| x \| _ {\infty} (198) \\ = \max  _ {s} \sum_ {a} \left| \frac {\partial^ {2} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \cdot \| x \| _ {\infty} (199) \\ \leq 6 \cdot \| u \| _ {2} ^ {2} \cdot \| x \| _ {\infty}. \quad (\text {b y e q . (1 8 7)}) (200) \\ \end{array}
$$

next, consider the state value function of  $\pi_{\theta_{\alpha}}$

$$
v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}}} (s) = \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s) \cdot r (s, a) + \gamma \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s) \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} \mathcal {p} \left(s ^ {\prime} \mid s, a\right) \cdot v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}}} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right), \tag {201}
$$

which implies,

$$
v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}}} (s) = e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}, \tag {202}
$$

where

$$
m (\alpha) = \left(\mathbf {i d} - \gamma p (\alpha)\right) ^ {- 1}, \tag {203}
$$

and  $r_{\theta_{\alpha}} \in \mathbb{r}^{s}$  for  $s \in s$  is given by

$$
r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s) = \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s) \cdot r (s, a). \tag {204}
$$

since  $[p(\alpha)]_{(s,s')}\geq 0,\forall (s,s')$  , and

$$
m (\alpha) = \left(\mathbf {i d} - \gamma p (\alpha)\right) ^ {- 1} = \sum_ {t = 0} ^ {\infty} \gamma^ {t} [ p (\alpha) ] ^ {t}, \tag {205}
$$

we have  $[m(\alpha)]_{(s,s')}\geq 0,\forall (s,s')$ . denote  $[m(\alpha)]_{i,:}$  as the  $i$ -th row vector of  $m(\alpha)$ . we have

$$
\mathbf {1} = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot (\mathbf {i d} - \gamma p (\alpha)) \mathbf {1} \longrightarrow m (\alpha) \mathbf {1} = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \mathbf {1}, \tag {206}
$$

which implies,  $\forall i$

$$
\left\| [ m (\alpha) ] _ {i,:} \right\| _ {1} = \sum_ {j} [ m (\alpha) ] _ {(i, j)} = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma}. \tag {207}
$$

therefore, for any vector  $x\in \mathbb{r}^s$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| m (\alpha) x \right\| _ {\infty} = \max  _ {i} \left| [ m (\alpha) ] _ {i,:} ^ {\top} x \right| (208) \\ \leq \max  _ {i} \left\| [ m (\alpha) ] _ {i,:} \right\| _ {1} \cdot \| x \| _ {\infty} (209) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \| x \| _ {\infty}. (210) \\ \end{array}
$$

according to assumption 1,  $r(s,a)\in [0,1],\forall (s,a)$  . we have,

$$
\left\| r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} \right\| _ {\infty} = \max  _ {s} | r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s) | = \max  _ {s} \left| \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s) \cdot r (s, a) \right| \leq 1. \tag {211}
$$

since  $\frac{\partial\pi_{\theta}(a|s)}{\partial\theta(s^{\prime},\cdot)} = 0$  for  $s^\prime \neq s$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \frac {\partial r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)}{\partial \alpha} \right| = \left| \left(\frac {\partial r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha}}\right) ^ {\top} \frac {\partial \theta_ {\alpha}}{\partial \alpha} \right| (212) \\ = \left| \left(\frac {\partial \left\{\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s) ^ {\top} r (s , \cdot) \right\}}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha} (s , \cdot)}\right) ^ {\top} u (s, \cdot) \right| (213) \\ = \left| \left(h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)\right) r (s, \cdot)\right) ^ {\top} u (s, \cdot) \right| (214) \\ \leq \left\| h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)\right) r (s, \cdot) \right\| _ {1} \cdot \left\| u (s, \cdot) \right\| _ {\infty}. (215) \\ \end{array}
$$

similarly to eq. (59), the  $\ell_1$  norm is upper bounded as

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)\right) r (s, \cdot) \right\| _ {1} = \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s) \cdot \left| r (s, a) - \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s) ^ {\top} r (s, \cdot) \right| (216) \\ \leq \max  _ {a} | r (s, a) - \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s) ^ {\top} r (s, \cdot) | (217) \\ \leq 1. \quad (\text {s i n c e} r (s, a) \in [ 0, 1 ]) (218) \\ \end{array}
$$

therefore we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \frac {\partial r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha} \right\| _ {\infty} = \max  _ {s} \left| \frac {\partial r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)}{\partial \alpha} \right| (219) \\ \leq \max  _ {s} \| h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)\right) r (s, \cdot) \| _ {1} \cdot \| u (s, \cdot) \| _ {\infty} (220) \\ \leq \| u \| _ {2}. (221) \\ \end{array}
$$

similarly,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \frac {\partial^ {2} r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right\| _ {\infty} = \max  _ {s} \left| \frac {\partial^ {2} r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right| (222) \\ = \max  _ {s} \left| \left(\frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha}} \left\{\frac {\partial r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)}{\partial \alpha} \right\}\right) ^ {\top} \frac {\partial \theta_ {\alpha}}{\partial \alpha} \right| (223) \\ = \max  _ {s} \left| \left(\frac {\partial^ {2} r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha} ^ {2}} \frac {\partial \theta_ {\alpha}}{\partial \alpha}\right) ^ {\top} \frac {\partial \theta_ {\alpha}}{\partial \alpha} \right| (224) \\ = \max  _ {s} \left| u (s, \cdot) ^ {\top} \frac {\partial^ {2} \left\{\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s) ^ {\top} r (s , \cdot) \right\}}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha} (s , \cdot) ^ {2}} u (s, \cdot) \right| (225) \\ \leq 5 / 2 \cdot \| u (s, \cdot) \| _ {2} ^ {2} \leq 3 \cdot \| u \| _ {2} ^ {2}. \quad (\text {b y e q .} (6 2)) (226) \\ \end{array}
$$

taking derivative w.r.t.  $\alpha$  in eq. (202),

$$
\frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}}} (s)}{\partial \alpha} = \gamma \cdot e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} + e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha}. \tag {227}
$$

taking second derivative w.r.t.  $\alpha$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\partial^ {2} v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)}}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} = 2 \gamma^ {2} \cdot e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} + \gamma \cdot e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial^ {2} p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} m (\alpha) r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (228) \\ + 2 \gamma \cdot e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha} + e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial^ {2} r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha^ {2}}. (229) \\ \end{array}
$$

for the last term,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial^ {2} r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg | \leq \| e _ {s} \| _ {1} \cdot \left\| m (\alpha) \frac {\partial^ {2} r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg | \| _ {\infty} (230) \\ \leq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \left\| \frac {\partial^ {2} r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg | _ {\infty} \quad (\text {b y e q . (2 0 8)}) (231) \\ \leq \frac {3}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \| u \| _ {2} ^ {2}. \quad (\text {b y e q . (2 2 2)}) (232) \\ \end{array}
$$

for the second last term,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg | \leq \left\| m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg \| _ {\infty} (233) \\ \leq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \left\| \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg \| _ {\infty} \quad \text {(b y e q . (2 0 8))} (234) \\ \leq \frac {2 \cdot \| u \| _ {2}}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \left\| m (\alpha) \frac {\partial r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg \| _ {\infty} \quad \text {(b y e q . (1 9 2))} (235) \\ \leq \frac {2 \cdot \| u \| _ {2}}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}} \cdot \left\| \frac {\partial r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \| _ {\infty} \quad (\text {b y e q . (2 0 8)}) (236) \\ \leq \frac {2 \cdot \| u \| _ {2}}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}} \cdot \| u \| _ {2} = \frac {2}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}} \cdot \| u \| _ {2} ^ {2}. \quad (\text {b y e q . (2 1 9)}) (237) \\ \end{array}
$$

for the second term,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial^ {2} p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} m (\alpha) r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg | \leq \left\| m (\alpha) \frac {\partial^ {2} p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} m (\alpha) r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg | \| _ {\infty} (238) \\ \leq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \left\| \frac {\partial^ {2} p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} m (\alpha) r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg \| _ {\infty} \quad \text {(b y e q . (2 0 8))} (239) \\ \leq \frac {6 \cdot \| u \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \left\| m (\alpha) r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg | _ {\infty} \quad \text {(b y e q . (1 9 7))} (240) \\ \leq \frac {6 \cdot \| u \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}} \cdot \left\| r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \| _ {\infty} \quad (\text {b y e q . (2 0 8)}) (241) \\ \leq \frac {6}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}} \cdot \| u \| _ {2} ^ {2}. \quad (\text {b y e q . (2 1 1)}) (242) \\ \end{array}
$$

for the first term, according to eq. (192), eqs. (208) and (211),

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg | \leq \left\| m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg \| _ {\infty} (243) \\ \leq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot 2 \cdot \| u \| _ {2} \cdot \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot 2 \cdot \| u \| _ {2} \cdot \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot 1 (244) \\ = \frac {4}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {3}} \cdot \| u \| _ {2} ^ {2}. (245) \\ \end{array}
$$

combining eqs. (230), (233), (238) and (243) with eq. (228),

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left. \left| \frac {\partial^ {2} v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}}} (s)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \right| \leq 2 \gamma^ {2} \cdot \left| e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} + \gamma \cdot \left| e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial^ {2} p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} m (\alpha) r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} (246) \\ + 2 \gamma \cdot \left| e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg | + \left| e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial^ {2} r _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg | (247) \\ \leq \left(2 \gamma^ {2} \cdot \frac {4}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {3}} + \gamma \cdot \frac {6}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}} + 2 \gamma \cdot \frac {2}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}} + \frac {3}{1 - \gamma}\right) \cdot \| u \| _ {2} ^ {2} (248) \\ \leq \frac {8}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {3}} \cdot \| u \| _ {2} ^ {2}, (249) \\ \end{array}
$$

which implies for all  $y\in \mathbb{r}^{sa}$  and  $\theta$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| y ^ {\top} \frac {\partial^ {2} v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)}{\partial \theta^ {2}} y \right| = \left| \left(\frac {y}{\| y \| _ {2}}\right) ^ {\top} \frac {\partial^ {2} v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)}{\partial \theta^ {2}} \left(\frac {y}{\| y \| _ {2}}\right) \right| \cdot \| y \| _ {2} ^ {2} (250) \\ \leq \max  _ {\| u \| _ {2} = 1} \left| \left\langle \frac {\partial^ {2} v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)}{\partial \theta^ {2}} u, u \right\rangle \right| \cdot \| y \| _ {2} ^ {2} (251) \\ = \max  _ {\| u \| _ {2} = 1} \left| \right.\left\langle \right. \frac {\partial^ {2} v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}}} (s)}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha} ^ {2}} \left. \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \frac {\partial \theta_ {\alpha}}{\partial \alpha}, \frac {\partial \theta_ {\alpha}}{\partial \alpha} \left. \right\rangle\left. \right| \cdot \| y \| _ {2} ^ {2} (252) \\ = \max  _ {\| u \| _ {2} = 1} \left| \left\langle \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha}} \left\{\frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}}} (s)}{\partial \alpha} \right\} \right| _ {\alpha = 0}, \frac {\partial \theta_ {\alpha}}{\partial \alpha} \right\rangle \bigg | \cdot \| y \| _ {2} ^ {2} (253) \\ = \max  _ {\| u \| _ {2} = 1} \left| \frac {\partial^ {2} v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}}} (s)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg | \cdot \| y \| _ {2} ^ {2} (254) \\ \leq \frac {8}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {3}} \cdot \| y \| _ {2} ^ {2}. \quad (\text {b y e q . (2 4 6)}) (255) \\ \end{array}
$$

denote  $\theta_{\xi} = \theta + \xi (\theta' - \theta)$ , where  $\xi \in [0,1]$ . according to taylor's theorem,  $\forall s, \forall \theta, \theta'$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| v ^ {\pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}}} (s) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) - \left\langle \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)}{\partial \theta}, \theta^ {\prime} - \theta \right\rangle \right| = \frac {1}{2} \cdot \left| \left(\theta^ {\prime} - \theta\right) ^ {\top} \frac {\partial^ {2} v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\xi}}} (s)}{\partial \theta_ {\xi} ^ {2}} \left(\theta^ {\prime} - \theta\right) \right| (256) \\ \leq \frac {4}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {3}} \cdot \| \theta^ {\prime} - \theta \| _ {2} ^ {2}. \quad (\text {b y e q . (2 5 0)}) (257) \\ \end{array}
$$

since  $v^{\pi_{\theta}}(s)$  is  $8 / (1 - \gamma)^{3}$ -smooth, for any state  $s$ ,  $v^{\pi_{\theta}}(\rho) = \mathbb{e}_{s\sim \rho}[v^{\pi_{\theta}}(s)]$  is also  $8 / (1 - \gamma)^{3}$ -smooth.

lemma 8 (non-uniform łojasiewicz). let  $\pi_{\theta}(\cdot |s) = \mathrm{softmax}(\theta (s,\cdot))$ ,  $s\in s$  and fix an arbitrary optimal policy  $\pi^{*}$ . we have,

$$
\left\| \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta} \right\| _ {2} \geq \frac {1}{\sqrt {s}} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \cdot \min  _ {s} \pi_ {\theta} (a ^ {*} (s) | s) \cdot [ v ^ {*} (\rho) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\rho) ], \tag {258}
$$

where  $a^*(s) = \arg \max_a \pi^*(a|s) (s \in s)$ . furthermore,

$$
\left\| \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta} \right\| _ {2} \geq \frac {1}{\sqrt {s a}} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \cdot \left[ \min  _ {s} \sum_ {\bar {a} (s) \in \bar {a} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (\bar {a} (s) | s) \right] \cdot [ v ^ {*} (\rho) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\rho) ], \tag {259}
$$

where  $\bar{\mathcal{a}}^{\pi}(s) = \{\bar{a} (s)\in \mathcal{a}:q^{\pi}(s,\bar{a} (s)) = \max_{a}q^{\pi}(s,a)\}$  is the greedy action set for state  $s$  given policy  $\pi$ . finally,

$$
\left\| \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta} \right\| _ {2} \geq \frac {1}{\sqrt {s a}} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\theta} ^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \cdot \left[ \min  _ {s} \sum_ {a ^ {\prime} \in \mathcal {a} ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} \left(a ^ {\prime} | s\right) \right] \cdot \left[ v ^ {*} (\rho) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\rho) \right], \tag {260}
$$

where  $\mathcal{a}^*(s)$  is the "optimal action set" under state  $s \in s$ , defined by,

$$
\mathcal {a} ^ {*} (s) := \left\{a ^ {*} (s) \in \mathcal {a}: q ^ {*} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) = \max  _ {a \in \mathcal {a}} q ^ {*} (s, a) \right\}, \tag {261}
$$

and  $\pi_{\theta}^{*}$  is the globally optimal policy induced by  $\pi_{\theta}$ , where for all  $s \in s$

$$
\pi_ {\theta} ^ {*} (a | s) := \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} \frac {\pi_ {\theta} (a | s)}{\sum_ {a ^ {\prime} \in \mathcal {a} ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} \left(a ^ {\prime} | s\right)}, & \text {i f} a \in \mathcal {a} ^ {*} (s), \\ 0, & \text {o t h e r w i s e .} \end{array} \right. \tag {262}
$$

proof. we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta} \right\| _ {2} = \left[ \sum_ {s, a} \left(\frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta (s , a)}\right) ^ {2} \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}} (263) \\ \geq \left[ \sum_ {s} \left(\frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta (s , a ^ {*} (s))}\right) ^ {2} \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}} (264) \\ \geq \frac {1}{\sqrt {s}} \sum_ {s} \left| \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta \left(s , a ^ {*} (s)\right)} \right| \quad \text {(b y c a u c h y - s c h w a r z ,} \| x \| _ {1} = | \langle \mathbf {1}, | x | \rangle | \leq \| \mathbf {1} \| _ {2} \cdot \| x \| _ {2}) (265) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {s}} \sum_ {s} \left| d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \pi_ {\theta} \left(a ^ {*} (s) \mid s\right) \cdot a ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) \right| \quad (\text {b y}) (266) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {s}} \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \pi_ {\theta} \left(a ^ {*} (s) | s\right) \cdot \left| a ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) \right|. \quad \left(\text {b e c a u s e} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \geq 0 \text {a n d} \pi_ {\theta} \left(a ^ {*} (s) | s\right) \geq 0\right) (267) \\ \end{array}
$$

define the distribution mismatch coefficient as  $\left\| \frac{d_{\rho}^{\pi^{*}}}{d_{\mu}^{\pi\theta}} \right\|_{\infty} = \max_{s} \frac{d_{\rho}^{\pi^{*}}(s)}{d_{\mu}^{\pi\theta}(s)}$ . we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta} \right\| _ {2} \geq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {s}} \sum_ {s} \frac {d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)}{d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi^ {*}} (s)} \cdot d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi^ {*}} (s) \cdot \pi_ {\theta} \left(a ^ {*} (s) | s\right) \cdot \left| a ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) \right| (268) \\ \geq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {s}} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \cdot \min  _ {s} \pi_ {\theta} \left(a ^ {*} (s) | s\right) \cdot \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi^ {*}} (s) \cdot \left| a ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) \right| (269) \\ \geq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {s}} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \cdot \min  _ {s} \pi_ {\theta} \left(a ^ {*} (s) | s\right) \cdot \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi^ {*}} (s) \cdot a ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) (270) \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {s}} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \cdot \min  _ {s} \pi_ {\theta} (a ^ {*} (s) | s) \cdot \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi^ {*}} (s) \sum_ {a} \pi^ {*} (a | s) \cdot a ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) (271) \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {s}} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \cdot \min  _ {s} \pi_ {\theta} \left(a ^ {*} (s) | s\right) \cdot \left[ v ^ {*} (\rho) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\rho) \right], (272) \\ \end{array}
$$

where the one but last equality used that  $\pi^{*}$  is deterministic and in state  $s$  chooses  $a^{*}(s)$  with probability one, and the last equality uses the performance difference formula (lemma 19).

to prove the second claim, given a policy  $\pi$ , define the greedy action set for each state  $s$ ,

$$
\bar {\mathcal {a}} ^ {\pi} (s) = \left\{\bar {a} (s) \in \mathcal {a}: q ^ {\pi} (s, \bar {a} (s)) = \max  _ {a} q ^ {\pi} (s, a) \right\}. \tag {273}
$$

by similar arguments that were used in the first part, we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta} \right\| _ {2} \geq \frac {1}{\sqrt {s a}} \sum_ {s, a} \left| \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta (s , a)} \right| \quad \text {(b y c a u c h y - s c h w a r z)} (274) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {s a}} \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \left| a ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) \right| \quad (\text {b y}) (275) \\ \geq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {s a}} \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \sum_ {\bar {a} (s) \in \bar {a} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (\bar {a} (s) | s) \cdot | a ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, \bar {a} (s)) | (276) \\ \geq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {s a}} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \cdot \left[ \min  _ {s} \sum_ {\bar {a} (s) \in \bar {\mathcal {a}} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (\bar {a} (s) | s) \right] \cdot \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi^ {*}} (s) \cdot \left| \max  _ {a} q ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \right|, (277) \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last inequality is because for any  $\bar{a} (s)\in \bar{\mathcal{a}}^{\pi_{\theta}}(s)$  we have

$$
a ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, \bar {a} (s)) = \max  _ {a} q ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s), \tag {278}
$$

which is the same value across all  $\bar{a}(s) \in \bar{\mathcal{a}}^{\pi_{\theta}}(s)$ . then we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta} \right\| _ {2} \geq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {s a}} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \cdot \left[ \min  _ {s} \sum_ {\bar {a} (s) \in \bar {\mathcal {a}} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (\bar {a} (s) | s) \right] \cdot \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi^ {*}} (s) \cdot \left[ \max  _ {a} q ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \right] (279) \\ \geq \frac {1}{\sqrt {s a}} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \cdot \left[ \min  _ {s} \sum_ {\bar {a} (s) \in \bar {a} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (\bar {a} (s) | s) \right] \cdot \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi^ {*}} (s) \cdot \left[ q ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \right] (280) \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {s a}} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \cdot \left[ \min  _ {s} \sum_ {\bar {a} (s) \in \bar {a} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (\bar {a} (s) | s) \right] \cdot \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi^ {*}} (s) \sum_ {a} \pi^ {*} (a | s) \cdot a ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) (281) \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {s a}} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \cdot \left[ \min  _ {s} \sum_ {\bar {a} (s) \in \bar {a} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (\bar {a} (s) | s) \right] \cdot \left[ v ^ {*} (\rho) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\rho) \right], (282) \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last equation is again according to lemma 19.

to prove the third claim, using the similar arguments in the second part, we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta} \right\| _ {2} \geq \frac {1}{\sqrt {s a}} \cdot \sum_ {s, a} \left| \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta (s , a)} \right| \quad \text {(b y c a u c h y - s c h w a r z)} (283) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {s a}} \cdot \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \left| a ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) \right| \quad (\text {b y}) (284) \\ \geq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {s a}} \cdot \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a} ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot | a ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) | \quad (\text {f e w e r t e r m s}) (285) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {s a}} \cdot \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a} ^ {*} (s)} \frac {\pi_ {\theta} (a | s)}{\sum_ {a ^ {\prime} \in \mathcal {a} ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (a ^ {\prime} | s)} \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a ^ {\prime} \in \mathcal {a} ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (a ^ {\prime} | s) \right] \cdot | a ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) | (286) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {s a}} \cdot \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a} ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} ^ {*} (a | s) \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a ^ {\prime} \in \mathcal {a} ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} \left(a ^ {\prime} | s\right) \right] \cdot \left| a ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) \right|. \quad (\text {b y}) \\ \end{array}
$$

taking minimum over all states, we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta} \right\| _ {2} \geq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {s a}} \cdot \left[ \min  _ {s} \sum_ {a ^ {\prime} \in \mathcal {a} ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} \left(a ^ {\prime} | s\right) \right] \cdot \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a} ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} ^ {*} (a | s) \cdot | a ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) | (288) \\ \geq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {s a}} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\theta} ^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \cdot \left[ \min  _ {s} \sum_ {a ^ {\prime} \in \mathcal {a} ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} \left(a ^ {\prime} | s\right) \right] \cdot \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta} ^ {*}} (s) \cdot \sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a} ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} ^ {*} (a | s) \cdot | a ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) | (289) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {s a}} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\theta} ^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \cdot \left[ \min  _ {s} \sum_ {a ^ {\prime} \in \mathcal {a} ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} \left(a ^ {\prime} | s\right) \right] \cdot \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta} ^ {*}} (s) \cdot \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta} ^ {*} (a | s) \cdot | a ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) |. (290) \\ \geq \frac {1}{\sqrt {s a}} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\theta} ^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \cdot \left[ \min  _ {s} \sum_ {a ^ {\prime} \in \mathcal {a} ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} \left(a ^ {\prime} | s\right) \right] \cdot \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta} ^ {*}} (s) \cdot \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta} ^ {*} (a | s) \cdot a ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a). \quad (| x | \geq x) (291) \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {s a}} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\theta} ^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \cdot \left[ \min  _ {s} \sum_ {a ^ {\prime} \in \mathcal {a} ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} \left(a ^ {\prime} | s\right) \right] \cdot \left[ v ^ {\pi_ {\theta} ^ {*}} (\rho) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\rho) \right] \quad (\text {b y}) \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {s a}} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\theta} ^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \cdot \left[ \min  _ {s} \sum_ {a ^ {\prime} \in \mathcal {a} ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} \left(a ^ {\prime} | s\right) \right] \cdot \left[ v ^ {*} (\rho) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\rho) \right], \quad \left(v ^ {\pi_ {\theta} ^ {*}} (\rho) = v ^ {*} (\rho)\right) (293) \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last equation is because of  $\pi_{\theta}^{*}$  is an optimal policy, for all  $s \in s$ ,

$$
\sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a} ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} ^ {*} (a | s) = \frac {\sum_ {a \in \mathcal {a} ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s)}{\sum_ {a ^ {\prime} \in \mathcal {a} ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} \left(a ^ {\prime} | s\right)} = 1, \tag {294}
$$

thus finishing the proofs.

lemma 9. let assumption 2 hold. using algorithm 1, we have  $c\coloneqq \inf_{s\in s,t\geq 1}\pi_{\theta_t}(a^* (s)|s) > 0$

proof. the proof is an extension of the proof for lemma 5. denote  $\delta^{*}(s) = q^{*}(s,a^{*}(s)) - \max_{a\neq a^{*}(s)}q^{*}(s,a) > 0$  as the optimal value gap of state  $s$ , where  $a^* (s)$  is the action that the optimal policy selects under state  $s$ , and  $\delta^{*} = \min_{s\in s}\delta^{*}(s) > 0$  as the optimal value gap of the mdp. for each state  $s\in s$ , define the following sets:

$$
\mathcal {r} _ {1} (s) = \left\{\theta : \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta \left(s , a ^ {*} (s)\right)} \geq \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta (s , a)}, \forall a \neq a ^ {*} \right\}, \tag {295}
$$

$$
\mathcal {r} _ {2} (s) = \left\{\theta : q ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) \geq q ^ {*} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) - \delta^ {*} (s) / 2 \right\}, \tag {296}
$$

$$
\mathcal {r} _ {3} (s) = \left\{\theta_ {t}: v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \geq q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) - \delta^ {*} (s) / 2, \text {f o r a l l} t \geq 1 \right. \tag {297}
$$

$$
\mathcal {n} _ {c} (s) = \left\{\theta : \pi_ {\theta} \left(a ^ {*} (s) \mid s\right) \geq \frac {c (s)}{c (s) + 1} \right\}, \text {w h e r e} c (s) = \frac {a}{(1 - \gamma) \cdot \delta^ {*} (s)} - 1. \tag {298}
$$

similarly to the previous proof, we have the following claims:

claim i.  $\mathcal{r}_1(s)\cap \mathcal{r}_2(s)\cap \mathcal{r}_3(s)$  is a "nice" region, in the sense that, following a gradient update, (i) if  $\theta_t\in \mathcal{r}_1(s)\cap \mathcal{r}_2(s)\cap \mathcal{r}_3(s)$ , then  $\theta_{t + 1}\in \mathcal{r}_1(s)\cap \mathcal{r}_2(s)\cap \mathcal{r}_3(s)$ ; while we also have (ii)  $\pi_{\theta_{t + 1}}(a^{*}(s)|s)\geq \pi_{\theta_{t}}(a^{*}(s)|s)$ .

claim ii.  $\mathcal{n}_c(s)\cap \mathcal{r}_2(s)\cap \mathcal{r}_3(s)\subset \mathcal{r}_1(s)\cap \mathcal{r}_2(s)\cap \mathcal{r}_3(s).$

claim iii. there exists a finite time  $t_0(s) \geq 1$ , such that  $\theta_{t_0(s)} \in \mathcal{n}_c(s) \cap \mathcal{r}_2(s) \cap \mathcal{r}_3(s)$ , and thus  $\theta_{t_0(s)} \in \mathcal{r}_1(s) \cap \mathcal{r}_2(s) \cap \mathcal{r}_3(s)$ , which implies  $\inf_{t \geq 1} \pi_{\theta_t}(a^*(s)|s) = \min_{1 \leq t \leq t_0(s)} \pi_{\theta_t}(a^*(s)|s)$ .

claim iv. define  $t_0 = \max_s t_0(s)$ . then, we have  $\inf_{s\in s,t\geq 1}\pi_{\theta_t}(a^* (s)|s) = \min_{1\leq t\leq t_0}\min_s\pi_{\theta_t}(a^* (s)|s)$ .

clearly, claim iv suffices to prove the lemma since for any  $\theta$ ,  $\min_{s,a} \pi_{\theta}(a|s) > 0$ . in what follows we provide the proofs of these four claims.

claim i. first we prove part (i) of the claim. if  $\theta_t \in \mathcal{r}_1(s) \cap \mathcal{r}_2(s) \cap \mathcal{r}_3(s)$ , then  $\theta_{t+1} \in \mathcal{r}_1(s) \cap \mathcal{r}_2(s) \cap \mathcal{r}_3(s)$ . suppose  $\theta_t \in \mathcal{r}_1(s) \cap \mathcal{r}_2(s) \cap \mathcal{r}_3(s)$ . we have  $\theta_{t+1} \in \mathcal{r}_3(s)$  by the definition of  $\mathcal{r}_3(s)$ . we have,

$$
q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) \geq q ^ {*} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) - \delta^ {*} (s) / 2. \tag {299}
$$

according to smoothness arguments as eq. (345), we have  $v^{\pi_{\theta_{t + 1}}}(s') \geq v^{\pi_{\theta_t}}(s')$ , and

$$
\begin{array}{l} q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}}} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) = q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) + q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}}} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) - q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) (300) \\ = q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) + \gamma \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} \mathcal {p} \left(s ^ {\prime} \mid s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) \cdot \left[ v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}}} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) \right] (301) \\ \geq q ^ {\pi \theta_ {t}} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) + 0 (302) \\ \geq q ^ {*} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) - \delta^ {*} (s) / 2, (303) \\ \end{array}
$$

which means  $\theta_{t + 1}\in \mathcal{r}_2(s)$  . next we prove  $\theta_{t + 1}\in \mathcal{r}_1(s)$  . note that  $\forall a\neq a^{*}(s)$

$$
\begin{array}{l} q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) - q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a) = q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) - q ^ {*} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) + q ^ {*} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) - q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a) (304) \\ \geq - \delta^ {*} (s) / 2 + q ^ {*} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) - q ^ {*} (s, a) + q ^ {*} (s, a) - q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a) (305) \\ \geq - \delta^ {*} (s) / 2 + q ^ {*} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) - \max  _ {a \neq a ^ {*} (s)} q ^ {*} (s, a) + q ^ {*} (s, a) - q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a) (306) \\ = - \delta^ {*} (s) / 2 + \delta^ {*} (s) + \gamma \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} \mathcal {p} \left(s ^ {\prime} \mid s, a\right) \cdot \left[ v ^ {*} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) \right] (307) \\ \geq - \delta^ {*} (s) / 2 + \delta^ {*} (s) + 0 (308) \\ = \delta^ {*} (s) / 2. (309) \\ \end{array}
$$

using similar arguments we also have  $q^{\pi_{\theta_{t + 1}}}(s,a^{*}(s)) - q^{\pi_{\theta_{t + 1}}}(s,a)\geq \delta^{*}(s) / 2$ . according to lemma 1,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t} (s , a)} = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \cdot \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a | s) \cdot a ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a) (310) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \cdot \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a | s) \cdot \left[ q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \right]. (311) \\ \end{array}
$$

furthermore, since  $\frac{\partial v^{\pi\theta_t}(\mu)}{\partial\theta_t(s,a^* (s))}\geq \frac{\partial v^{\pi\theta_t}(\mu)}{\partial\theta_t(s,a)}$  , we have

$$
\pi_ {\theta_ {t}} \left(a ^ {*} (s) | s\right) \cdot \left[ q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \right] \geq \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a | s) \cdot \left[ q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \right]. \tag {312}
$$

similarly to the first part in the proof for lemma 5. there are two cases.

case (a): if  $\pi_{\theta_t}(a^*(s)|s) \geq \pi_{\theta_t}(a|s)$ , then  $\theta_t(s, a^*(s)) \geq \theta_t(s, a)$ . after an update of the parameters,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \theta_ {t + 1} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) = \theta_ {t} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) + \eta \cdot \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t} (s , a ^ {*} (s))} (313) \\ \geq \theta_ {t} (s, a) + \eta \cdot \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t} (s , a)} = \theta_ {t + 1} (s, a), (314) \\ \end{array}
$$

which implies  $\pi_{\theta_{t + 1}}(a^* (s)|s)\geq \pi_{\theta_{t + 1}}(a|s)$ . since  $q^{\pi_{\theta_{t + 1}}}(s,a^{*}(s)) - q^{\pi_{\theta_{t + 1}}}(s,a)\geq \delta^{*}(s) / 2\geq 0,\forall a$  we have  $q^{\pi_{\theta_{t + 1}}}(s,a^{*}(s)) - v^{\pi_{\theta_{t + 1}}}(s) = q^{\pi_{\theta_{t + 1}}}(s,a^{*}(s)) - \sum_{a}\pi_{\theta_{t + 1}}(a|s)\cdot q^{\pi_{\theta_{t + 1}}}(s,a)\geq 0$  and

$$
\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}} \left(a ^ {*} (s) | s\right) \cdot \left[ q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}}} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}}} (s) \right] \geq \pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}} (a | s) \cdot \left[ q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}}} (s, a) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}}} (s) \right], \tag {315}
$$

which is equivalent to  $\frac{\partial v^{\pi_{\theta_{t + 1}}(\mu)}}{\partial\theta_{t + 1}(s,a^{*}(s))}\geq \frac{\partial v^{\pi_{\theta_{t + 1}}(\mu)}}{\partial\theta_{t + 1}(s,a)}$  , i.e.,  $\theta_{t + 1}\in \mathcal{r}_1(s)$

case (b): if  $\pi_{\theta_t}(a^*(s)|s) < \pi_{\theta_t}(a|s)$ , then by  $\frac{\partial v^{\pi_{\theta_t}}(\mu)}{\partial \theta_t(s, a^*(s))} \geq \frac{\partial v^{\pi_{\theta_t}}(\mu)}{\partial \theta_t(s, a)}$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} \left(a ^ {*} (s) | s\right) \cdot \left[ q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \right] \geq \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a | s) \cdot \left[ q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \right] (316) \\ = \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a | s) \cdot \left[ q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) + q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a) - q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) \right], (317) \\ \end{array}
$$

which, after rearranging, is equivalent to

$$
\begin{array}{l} q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) - q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a) \geq \left(1 - \frac {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}} \left(a ^ {*} (s) \mid s\right)}{\pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a \mid s)}\right) \cdot \left[ q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \right] (318) \\ = \left(1 - \exp \left\{\theta_ {t} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) - \theta_ {t} (s, a) \right\}\right) \cdot \left[ q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \right]. (319) \\ \end{array}
$$

since  $\theta_{t + 1}\in \mathcal{r}_3(s)$ , we have,

$$
q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}}} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}}} (s) \leq \delta^ {*} (s) / 2 \leq q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}}} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) - q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}}} (s, a). \tag {320}
$$

on the other hand,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \theta_ {t + 1} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) - \theta_ {t + 1} (s, a) = \theta_ {t} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) + \eta \cdot \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t} \left(s , a ^ {*} (s)\right)} - \theta_ {t} (s, a) - \eta \cdot \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t} (s , a)} (321) \\ \geq \theta_ {t} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) - \theta_ {t} (s, a), (322) \\ \end{array}
$$

which implies

$$
1 - \exp \left\{\theta_ {t + 1} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) - \theta_ {t + 1} (s, a) \right\} \leq 1 - \exp \left\{\theta_ {t} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) - \theta_ {t} (s, a) \right\}. \tag {323}
$$

furthermore, since  $1 - \exp \left\{\theta_{t}(s,a^{*}(s)) - \theta_{t}(s,a)\right\} = 1 - \frac{\pi_{\theta_{t}}(a^{*}(s)|s)}{\pi_{\theta_{t}}(a|s)} >0$  (in this case  $\pi_{\theta_t}(a^* (s)|s) <   \pi_{\theta_t}(a|s))$

$$
\left(1 - \exp \left\{\theta_ {t + 1} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) - \theta_ {t + 1} (s, a) \right\}\right) \cdot \left[ q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}}} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}}} (s) \right] \leq q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}}} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) - q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}}} (s, a), \tag {324}
$$

which after rearranging is equivalent to

$$
\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}} \left(a ^ {*} (s) | s\right) \cdot \left[ q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}}} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}}} (s) \right] \geq \pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}} (a | s) \cdot \left[ q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}}} (s, a) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}}} (s) \right], \tag {325}
$$

which means  $\frac{\partial v^{\pi_{\theta_{t + 1}}(\mu)}}{\partial\theta_{t + 1}(s,a^{*}(s))}\geq \frac{\partial v^{\pi_{\theta_{t + 1}}(\mu)}}{\partial\theta_{t + 1}(s,a)}$  i.e.,  $\theta_{t + 1}\in \mathcal{r}_1(s)$ . now we have (i) if  $\theta_t\in \mathcal{r}_1(s)\cap \mathcal{r}_2(s)\cap \mathcal{r}_3(s)$ , then  $\theta_{t + 1}\in \mathcal{r}_1(s)\cap \mathcal{r}_2(s)\cap \mathcal{r}_3(s)$ .

let us now turn to proving part (ii). we have  $\pi_{\theta_{t + 1}}(a^* (s)|s)\geq \pi_{\theta_t}(a^* (s)|s)$  . if  $\theta_t\in \mathcal{r}_1(s)\cap \mathcal{r}_2(s)\cap \mathcal{r}_3(s)$  , then  $\frac{\partial v^{\pi_{\theta_t}}(\mu)}{\partial\theta_t(s,a^* (s))}\geq \frac{\partial v^{\pi_{\theta_t}}(\mu)}{\partial\theta_t(s,a)},\forall a\neq a^*$  . after an update of the parameters,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}} \left(a ^ {*} (s) | s\right) = \frac {\exp \left\{\theta_ {t + 1} \left(s , a ^ {*} (s)\right) \right\}}{\sum_ {a} \exp \left\{\theta_ {t + 1} (s , a) \right\}} (326) \\ = \frac {\exp \left\{\theta_ {t} \left(s , a ^ {*} (s)\right) + \eta \cdot \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi \theta_ {t}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t} \left(s , a ^ {*} (s)\right)} \right\}}{\sum_ {a} \exp \left\{\theta_ {t} (s , a) + \eta \cdot \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi \theta_ {t}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t} (s , a)} \right\}} (327) \\ \geq \frac {\exp \left\{\theta_ {t} \left(s , a ^ {*} (s)\right) + \eta \cdot \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi \theta_ {t}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t} \left(s , a ^ {*} (s)\right)} \right\}}{\sum_ {a} \exp \left\{\theta_ {t} (s , a) + \eta \cdot \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi \theta_ {t}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t} \left(s , a ^ {*} (s)\right)} \right\}} \quad \left(\text {b e c a u s e} \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi \theta_ {t}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t} \left(s , a ^ {*} (s)\right)} \geq \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi \theta_ {t}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t} (s , a)}\right) (328) \\ = \frac {\exp \left\{\theta_ {t} \left(s , a ^ {*} (s)\right) \right\}}{\sum_ {a} \exp \left\{\theta_ {t} (s , a) \right\}} = \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} \left(a ^ {*} (s) | s\right). (329) \\ \end{array}
$$

claim ii.  $\mathcal{n}_c(s)\cap \mathcal{r}_2(s)\cap \mathcal{r}_3(s)\subset \mathcal{r}_1(s)\cap \mathcal{r}_2(s)\cap \mathcal{r}_3(s)$ . suppose  $\theta \in \mathcal{r}_2(s)\cap \mathcal{r}_3(s)$  and  $\pi_{\theta}(a^{*}(s)|s)\geq \frac{c(s)}{c(s) + 1}$ . there are two cases.

case (a): if  $\pi_{\theta}(a^{*}(s)|s)\geq \max_{a\neq a^{*}(s)}\{\pi_{\theta}(a|s)\}$ , then we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta (s , a ^ {*} (s))} = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \pi_ {\theta} \left(a ^ {*} (s) | s\right) \cdot \left[ q ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \right] (330) \\ > \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \left[ q ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \right] (331) \\ = \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta (s , a)}, (332) \\ \end{array}
$$

where the inequality is since  $q^{\pi_{\theta}}(s, a^{*}(s)) - q^{\pi_{\theta}}(s, a) \geq \delta^{*}(s) / 2 > 0, \forall a \neq a^{*}(s)$ , similarly to eq. (304).

case (b):  $\pi_{\theta}(a^{*}(s)|s) < \max_{a \neq a^{*}(s)} \{\pi_{\theta}(a|s)\}$ , which is not possible. suppose there exists an  $a \neq a^{*}(s)$ , such that  $\pi_{\theta}(a^{*}(s)|s) < \pi_{\theta}(a|s)$ . then we have the following contradiction,

$$
\pi_ {\theta} \left(a ^ {*} (s) | s\right) + \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) > \frac {2 \cdot c (s)}{c (s) + 1} = 2 - \frac {2 \cdot (1 - \gamma) \cdot \delta^ {*} (s)}{a} > 1, \tag {333}
$$

where the last inequality is according to  $a \geq 2$  (there are at least two actions), and  $\delta^{*}(s) \leq 1 / (1 - \gamma)$ .

claim iii. (1) according to the asymptotic convergence results of agarwal et al. (2019, theorem 5.1), which we can use thanks to assumption 2,  $\pi_{\theta_t}(a^*(s)|s) \to 1$ . hence, there exists  $t_1(s) \geq 1$ , such that  $\pi_{\theta_{t_1(s)}}(a^*(s)|s) \geq \frac{c(s)}{c(s) + 1}$ . (2)  $q^{\pi_{\theta_t}}(s,a^*(s)) \to q^*(s,a^*(s))$ , as  $t \to \infty$ . there exists  $t_2(s) \geq 1$ , such that  $q^{\pi_{\theta_{t_2(s)}}}(s,a^*(s)) \geq q^*(s,a^*(s)) - \delta^*(s)/2$ . (3)  $q^{\pi_{\theta_t}}(s,a^*(s)) \to v^*(s)$ , and  $v^{\pi_{\theta_t}}(s) \to v^*(s)$ , as  $t \to \infty$ . there exists  $t_3(s) \geq 1$ , such that  $\forall t \geq t_3(s)$ ,  $q^{\pi_{\theta_t}}(s,a^*(s)) - v^{\pi_{\theta_t}}(s) \leq \delta^*(s)/2$ .

define  $t_0(s) = \max \{t_1(s), t_2(s), t_3(s)\}$ . we have  $\theta_{t_0(s)} \in \mathcal{n}_c(s) \cap \mathcal{r}_2(s) \cap \mathcal{r}_3(s)$ , and thus  $\theta_{t_0(s)} \in \mathcal{r}_1(s) \cap \mathcal{r}_2(s) \cap \mathcal{r}_3(s)$ . according to the first part in our proof, i.e., once  $\theta_t$  is in  $\mathcal{r}_1(s) \cap \mathcal{r}_2(s) \cap \mathcal{r}_3(s)$ , following gradient update  $\theta_{t+1}$  will be in  $\mathcal{r}_1(s) \cap \mathcal{r}_2(s) \cap \mathcal{r}_3(s)$ , and  $\pi_{\theta_t}(a^*(s)|s)$  is increasing in  $\mathcal{r}_1(s) \cap \mathcal{r}_2(s) \cap \mathcal{r}_3(s)$ , we have  $\inf_t \pi_{\theta_t}(a^*(s)|s) = \min_{1 \leq t \leq t_0(s)} \pi_{\theta_t}(a^*(s)|s)$ .  $t_0(s)$  depends on initialization and  $c(s)$ , which only depends on the mdp and state  $s$ .

claim iv. define  $t_0 = \max_s t_0(s)$ . then we have  $\inf_{s\in s,t\geq 1}\pi_{\theta_t}(a^* (s)|s) = \min_{1\leq t\leq t_0}\min_s\pi_{\theta_t}(a^* (s)|s) > 0$

theorem 4. let assumption 2 hold and let  $\{\theta_t\}_{t\geq 1}$  be generated using algorithm 1 with  $\eta = (1 - \gamma)^3 /8$ $c$  the positive constant from lemma 9. then, for all  $t\geq 1$

$$
v ^ {*} (\rho) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\rho) \leq \frac {1 6 s}{c ^ {2} (1 - \gamma) ^ {6} t} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi^ {*}}}{\mu} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {2} \cdot \left\| \frac {1}{\mu} \right\| _ {\infty}. \tag {334}
$$

proof. let us first note that for any  $\theta$  and  $\mu$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) = \underset {s _ {0} \sim \mu} {\mathbb {e}} \left[ d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \right] (335) \\ = \underset {s _ {0} \sim \mu} {\mathbb {e}} \left[ (1 - \gamma) \sum_ {t = 0} ^ {\infty} \gamma^ {t} \pr \left(s _ {t} = s \mid s _ {0}, \pi_ {\theta}, \mathcal {p}\right) \right] (336) \\ \geq \underset {s _ {0} \sim \mu} {\mathbb {e}} [ (1 - \gamma) \pr (s _ {0} = s | s _ {0}) ] (337) \\ = (1 - \gamma) \cdot \mu (s). (338) \\ \end{array}
$$

according to the value sub-optimality lemma of lemma 21,

$$
\begin{array}{l} v ^ {*} (\rho) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\rho) = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \sum_ {a} \left(\pi^ {*} (a | s) - \pi_ {\theta} (a | s)\right) \cdot q ^ {*} (s, a) (339) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \sum_ {a} \left(\pi^ {*} (a | s) - \pi_ {\theta} (a | s)\right) \cdot q ^ {*} (s, a) (340) \\ \leq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \left\| \frac {1}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \sum_ {a} \left(\pi^ {*} (a | s) - \pi_ {\theta} (a | s)\right) \cdot q ^ {*} (s, a) (341) \\ \leq \frac {1}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}} \cdot \left\| \frac {1}{\mu} \right\| _ {\infty} \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \sum_ {a} \left(\pi^ {*} (a | s) - \pi_ {\theta} (a | s)\right) \cdot q ^ {*} (s, a) \quad \left(\text {b y e q . (3 3 5) a n d} \min  _ {s} \mu (s) > 0\right) (342) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \left\| \frac {1}{\mu} \right\| _ {\infty} \cdot \left[ v ^ {*} (\mu) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu) \right], (343) \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first inequality is because of

$$
\sum_ {a} \left(\pi^ {*} (a | s) - \pi_ {\theta} (a | s)\right) \cdot q ^ {*} (s, a) \geq 0, \tag {344}
$$

and the last equation is again by lemma 21. according to lemma 7,  $v^{\pi_{\theta}}(\mu)$  is  $\beta$ -smooth with  $\beta = 8 / (1 - \gamma)^3$ . denote  $\delta_t = v^*(\mu) - v^{\pi_{\theta_t}}(\mu)$ . and note  $\eta = \frac{(1 - \gamma)^3}{8}$ . we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \delta_ {t + 1} - \delta_ {t} = v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}}} (\mu) (345) \\ \leq - \frac {(1 - \gamma) ^ {3}}{1 6} \cdot \left\| \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t}} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \quad \text {(b y l e m m a 1 8)} (346) \\ \leq - \frac {(1 - \gamma) ^ {3}}{1 6 s} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 2} \cdot \min  _ {s} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} \left(a ^ {*} (s) | s\right) ^ {2} \cdot \left[ v ^ {*} (\mu) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu) \right] ^ {2} \quad (347) \\ \leq - \frac {(1 - \gamma) ^ {5}}{1 6 s} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi^ {*}}}{\mu} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 2} \cdot \min  _ {s} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} \left(a ^ {*} (s) | s\right) ^ {2} \cdot \delta_ {t} ^ {2} (348) \\ \leq - \frac {(1 - \gamma) ^ {5}}{1 6 s} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi^ {*}}}{\mu} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 2} \cdot \inf  _ {s \in s, t \geq 1} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} \left(a ^ {*} (s) | s\right) ^ {2} \cdot \delta_ {t} ^ {2}, (349) \\ \end{array}
$$

where the second to last inequality is by  $d_{\mu}^{\pi_{\theta_t}}(s) \geq (1 - \gamma) \cdot \mu(s)$  (cf. eq. (335)). according to lemma 9,  $c = \inf_{s \in s, t \geq 1} \pi_{\theta_t}(a^*(s)|s) > 0$ . using similar induction arguments as in eq. (79),

$$
v ^ {*} (\mu) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu) \leq \frac {1 6 s}{c ^ {2} (1 - \gamma) ^ {5} t} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi^ {*}}}{\mu} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {2}, \tag {350}
$$

which leads to the final result,

$$
v ^ {*} (\rho) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\rho) \leq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \left\| \frac {1}{\mu} \right\| _ {\infty} \cdot \left[ v ^ {*} (\mu) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu) \right] \leq \frac {1 6 s}{c ^ {2} (1 - \gamma) ^ {6} t} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi^ {*}}}{\mu} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {2} \cdot \left\| \frac {1}{\mu} \right\| _ {\infty}, \tag {351}
$$

thus, finishing the proof.

# a.2. proofs for section 4: entropy regularized softmax policy gradient

# a.2.1. preliminaries

lemma 10. entropy regularized policy gradient w.r.t.  $\theta$  is

$$
\frac {\partial \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta (s , a)} = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \tilde {a} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) \tag {352}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\partial \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta (s , \cdot)} = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot h \left(\pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s)\right) \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, \cdot) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s) \right] (353) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot h \left(\pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s)\right) \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, \cdot) - \tau \theta (s, \cdot) \right], \forall s (354) \\ \end{array}
$$

where  $\tilde{a}^{\pi_{\theta}}(s,a)$  is soft advantage function defined as

$$
\tilde {a} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) = \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \tag {355}
$$

$$
\tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) = r (s, a) + \gamma \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} \mathcal {p} \left(s ^ {\prime} \mid s, a\right) \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right). \tag {356}
$$

proof. according to the definition of  $\tilde{v}^{\pi_{\theta}}$

$$
\tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu) = \underset {s \sim \mu} {\mathbb {e}} \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \right]. \tag {357}
$$

taking derivative w.r.t.  $\theta$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\partial \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta} = \underset {s \sim \mu} {\mathbb {e}} \sum_ {a} \frac {\partial \pi_ {\theta} (a | s)}{\partial \theta} \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \right] + \underset {s \sim \mu} {\mathbb {e}} \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \left[ \frac {\partial \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s , a)}{\partial \theta} - \tau \cdot \frac {1}{\pi_ {\theta} (a | s)} \cdot \frac {\partial \pi_ {\theta} (a | s)}{\partial \theta} \right] (358) \\ = \underset {s \sim \mu} {\mathbb {e}} \sum_ {a} \frac {\partial \pi_ {\theta} (a | s)}{\partial \theta} \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \right] + \underset {s \sim \mu} {\mathbb {e}} \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \frac {\partial \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s , a)}{\partial \theta} (359) \\ = \underset {s \sim \mu} {\mathbb {e}} \sum_ {a} \frac {\partial \pi_ {\theta} (a | s)}{\partial \theta} \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \right] + \gamma \cdot \underset {s \sim \mu} {\mathbb {e}} \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} \mathcal {p} \left(s ^ {\prime} | s, a\right) \cdot \frac {\partial \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right)}{\partial \theta} (360) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \sum_ {a} \frac {\partial \pi_ {\theta} (a | s)}{\partial \theta} \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \right], (361) \\ \end{array}
$$

where the second equation is because of

$$
\sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \left[ \frac {1}{\pi_ {\theta} (a | s)} \cdot \frac {\partial \pi_ {\theta} (a | s)}{\partial \theta} \right] = \sum_ {a} \frac {\partial \pi_ {\theta} (a | s)}{\partial \theta} = \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta} \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) = \frac {\partial 1}{\partial \theta} = 0. \tag {362}
$$

using similar arguments as in the proof for lemma 1, i.e., for  $s' \neq s$ ,  $\frac{\partial \pi_{\theta}(a|s)}{\partial \theta(s', \cdot)} = 0$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\partial \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta (s , \cdot)} = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a} \frac {\partial \pi_ {\theta} (a | s)}{\partial \theta (s , \cdot)} \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \right] \right] (363) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \left(\frac {d \pi (\cdot | s)}{d \theta (s , \cdot)}\right) ^ {\top} \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, \cdot) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s) \right] (364) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot h \left(\pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s)\right) \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, \cdot) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s) \right] \quad (\text {b y e q .} (6)) (365) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot h \left(\pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s)\right) \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, \cdot) - \tau \theta (\cdot | s) + \tau \log \sum_ {a} \exp \left\{\theta (s, a) \right\} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right] (366) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot h \left(\pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s)\right) \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, \cdot) - \tau \theta (\cdot | s) \right]. \quad \left(h \left(\pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s)\right) \mathbf {1} = \mathbf {0} \text {i n l e m m a 2 2}\right) (367) \\ \end{array}
$$

for each component  $a$ , we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\partial \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta (s , a)} = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) - \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \right] \right] (368) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \right] (369) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \tilde {a} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a). \\ \end{array}
$$

# a.2.2. proofs for bandits and non-uniform contraction

lemma 11 (non-uniform contraction). using update 2 with  $\tau \eta \leq 1, \forall t \geq 1$ ,

$$
\left\| \zeta_ {t + 1} \right\| _ {2} \leq \left(1 - \tau \eta \cdot \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a)\right) \cdot \| \zeta_ {t} \| _ {2}, \tag {370}
$$

where  $\zeta_t = \tau \theta_t - r - \frac{(\tau \theta_t - r)^\top \mathbf{1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf{1}$ .

proof. update 2 can be written as

$$
\begin{array}{l} \theta_ {t + 1} = \theta_ {t} - \eta \cdot h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) (\tau \log \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} - r) (371) \\ = \theta_ {t} - \eta \cdot h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) \left[ \tau \theta_ {t} - r - \left(\log \sum_ {a} \exp \left\{\theta_ {t} (a) \right\}\right) \cdot \mathbf {1} \right] (372) \\ = \theta_ {t} - \eta \cdot h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) \left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r\right) (373) \\ = \theta_ {t} - \eta \cdot h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) \left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r - \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1}\right), (374) \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last two equations are from  $h(\pi_{\theta_t}) \mathbf{1} = \mathbf{0}$  as shown in lemma 22. for all  $t \geq 1$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \zeta_ {t + 1} = \tau \theta_ {t + 1} - r - \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t + 1} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} (375) \\ = \tau \theta_ {t} - r - \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} + \tau \left(\theta_ {t + 1} - \theta_ {t}\right) + \left(\frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} - \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t + 1} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k}\right) \cdot \mathbf {1} (376) \\ = \tau \theta_ {t} - r - \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} + \tau \left(\theta_ {t + 1} - \theta_ {t}\right) + \frac {\tau \left(\theta_ {t} - \theta_ {t + 1}\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1}. (377) \\ \end{array}
$$

for the last term,

$$
\frac {\tau \left(\theta_ {t} - \theta_ {t + 1}\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} = \frac {\tau}{k} \cdot \left(\eta \cdot h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) \left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r - \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1}\right)\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1} \cdot \mathbf {1} = \mathbf {0}, \tag {378}
$$
