where the last equation is again by  $h(\pi_{\theta_t})^\top \mathbf{1} = h(\pi_{\theta_t})\mathbf{1} = \mathbf{0}$ . using the update rule and combining the above,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \zeta_ {t + 1} = \tau \theta_ {t} - r - \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} + \tau \left(\theta_ {t + 1} - \theta_ {t}\right) (379) \\ = \left(\mathbf {i d} - \tau \eta \cdot h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right)\right) \left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r - \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1}\right) (380) \\ = \left(\mathbf {i d} - \tau \eta \cdot h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right)\right) \zeta_ {t}. (381) \\ \end{array}
$$

according to lemma 23, with  $\tau \eta \leq 1$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \zeta_ {t + 1} \right\| _ {2} = \left\| \left(\mathbf {i d} - \tau \eta \cdot h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right)\right) \zeta_ {t} \right\| _ {2} \tag {382} \\ \leq \left(1 - \tau \eta \cdot \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a)\right) \cdot \| \zeta_ {t} \| _ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

lemma 12. let  $\pi_{\theta_t} = \mathrm{softmax}(\theta_t)$ . using update 2 with  $\tau \eta \leq 1, \forall t \geq 1$

$$
\left\| \zeta_ {t} \right\| _ {2} \leq \frac {2 \left(\tau \left\| \theta_ {1} \right\| _ {\infty} + 1\right) \sqrt {k}}{\exp \left\{\tau \eta \sum_ {s = 1} ^ {t - 1} \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {s}} (a) \right\}}. \tag {383}
$$

proof. according to lemma 11, for all  $t \geq 1$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \zeta_ {t + 1} \right\| _ {2} \leq \left(1 - \tau \eta \cdot \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a)\right) \cdot \| \zeta_ {t} \| _ {2} (384) \\ \leq \frac {1}{\exp \left\{\tau \eta \cdot \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a) \right\}} \cdot \| \zeta_ {t} \| _ {2} (385) \\ \leq \frac {1}{\exp \left\{\tau \eta \cdot \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a) \right\}} \cdot \left(1 - \tau \eta \cdot \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {t - 1}} (a)\right) \cdot \| \zeta_ {t - 1} \| _ {2} (386) \\ \leq \frac {1}{\exp \left\{\tau \eta \sum_ {s = t - 1} ^ {t} \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {s}} (a) \right\}} \cdot \| \zeta_ {t - 1} \| _ {2} (387) \\ \leq \frac {1}{\exp \left\{\tau \eta \sum_ {s = 1} ^ {t} \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {s}} (a) \right\}} \cdot \| \zeta_ {1} \| _ {2}. (388) \\ \end{array}
$$

for the initial logit  $\theta_{1}$

$$
\left\| \zeta_ {1} \right\| _ {2} = \left\| \tau \theta_ {1} - r - \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {1} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {2} \tag {389}
$$

$$
\leq \left\| \tau \theta_ {1} - r \right\| _ {2} + \left\| \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {1} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {2} \quad (\text {b y}) \tag {390}
$$

$$
= \| \tau \theta_ {1} - r \| _ {2} + \frac {\left| (\tau \theta_ {1} - r) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1} \right|}{\sqrt {k}} \tag {391}
$$

$$
\leq \left\| \tau \theta_ {1} - r \right\| _ {2} + \frac {\left\| \tau \theta_ {1} - r \right\| _ {2} \cdot \left\| \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {2}}{\sqrt {k}} \quad (\text {b y c a u c h y - s c h w a r z}) \tag {392}
$$

$$
= 2 \cdot \| \tau \theta_ {1} - r \| _ {2} \tag {393}
$$

$$
\leq 2 \cdot \left(\| \tau \theta_ {1} \| _ {2} + \| r \| _ {2}\right) \tag {394}
$$

$$
\leq 2 \left(\tau \| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} + 1\right) \sqrt {k}, \tag {395}
$$

finishing the proof.

lemma 13. there exists  $c = c(\tau, k, \| \theta_1 \|_\infty) > 0$ , such that for all  $t \geq 1$ ,  $\min_a \pi_{\theta_t}(a) \geq c$ . thus,  $\sum_{s=1}^{t-1} \min_a \pi_{\theta_s}(a) \geq c \cdot (t - 1)$ .

proof. define the constant  $c = c(\tau, k, \| \theta_1 \|_{\infty})$  as

$$
c = \frac {1}{k} \cdot \frac {1}{\exp \left\{1 / \tau \right\}} \cdot \frac {1}{\exp \left\{4 \left(\left\| \theta_ {1} \right\| _ {\infty} + 1 / \tau\right) \sqrt {k} \right\}}. \tag {396}
$$

first, according to eq. (389), we have,

$$
\left\| \zeta_ {1} \right\| _ {2} \leq 2 \left(\tau \left\| \theta_ {1} \right\| _ {\infty} + 1\right) \sqrt {k}. \tag {397}
$$

next, according to lemma 11, with  $\tau \eta \leq 1$

$$
\left\| \zeta_ {t + 1} \right\| _ {2} \leq \left(1 - \tau \eta \cdot \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a)\right) \cdot \left\| \zeta_ {t} \right\| _ {2} \leq 2 (\tau \| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} + 1) \sqrt {k}. \tag {398}
$$

therefore, for all  $t \geq 1$ , we have,

$$
\left\| \zeta_ {t} \right\| _ {2} \leq 2 \left(\tau \left\| \theta_ {1} \right\| _ {\infty} + 1\right) \sqrt {k}. \tag {399}
$$

we now prove  $\min_{a}\pi_{\theta_t}(a)\geq c$  . we have,  $\forall a$

$$
\left| \theta_ {t} (a) - \frac {r (a)}{\tau} - \frac {\left(\theta_ {t} - r / \tau\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \right| = \frac {1}{\tau} \cdot \left| \tau \theta_ {t} (a) - r (a) - \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \right| \tag {400}
$$

$$
\leq \frac {1}{\tau} \cdot \left\| \tau \theta_ {t} - r - \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {2} \tag {401}
$$

$$
= \frac {1}{\tau} \cdot \| \zeta_ {t} \| _ {2} \tag {402}
$$

$$
\leq 2 \left(\left\| \theta_ {1} \right\| _ {\infty} + 1 / \tau\right) \sqrt {k}. \tag {403}
$$

denote  $a_1 = \arg \min_a\theta_t(a)$ , and  $a_2 = \arg \max_a\theta_t(a)$ . according to the above, we have the following results,

$$
\theta_ {t} \left(a _ {1}\right) \geq \frac {r \left(a _ {1}\right)}{\tau} + \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} - 2 \left(\left\| \theta_ {1} \right\| _ {\infty} + 1 / \tau\right) \sqrt {k}, \tag {404}
$$

$$
- \theta_ {t} \left(a _ {2}\right) \geq - \frac {r \left(a _ {2}\right)}{\tau} - \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} - 2 \left(\left\| \theta_ {1} \right\| _ {\infty} + 1 / \tau\right) \sqrt {k}, \tag {405}
$$

which can be used to lower bound the minimum probability as,

$$
\min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a) = \frac {\exp \left\{\theta_ {t} \left(a _ {1}\right) \right\}}{\sum_ {a} \exp \left\{\theta_ {t} (a) \right\}} \geq \frac {\exp \left\{\theta_ {t} \left(a _ {1}\right) \right\}}{\sum_ {a} \exp \left\{\theta_ {t} \left(a _ {2}\right) \right\}} = \frac {1}{k} \cdot \exp \left\{\theta_ {t} \left(a _ {1}\right) - \theta_ {t} \left(a _ {2}\right) \right\}, (\text {s i n c e} \theta_ {t} (a) \leq \theta_ {t} \left(a _ {2}\right), \forall a) \tag {406}
$$

which can be further lower bounded using the above results,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a) \geq \frac {1}{k} \cdot \exp \left\{\theta_ {t} \left(a _ {1}\right) - \theta_ {t} \left(a _ {2}\right) \right\} (407) \\ \geq \frac {1}{k} \cdot \exp \left\{\frac {r \left(a _ {1}\right)}{\tau} + \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} - 2 \left(\| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} + 1 / \tau\right) \sqrt {k} - \frac {r \left(a _ {2}\right)}{\tau} - \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} - 2 \left(\| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} + 1 / \tau\right) \sqrt {k} \right\} (408) \\ = \frac {1}{k} \cdot \exp \left\{\frac {r \left(a _ {1}\right) - r \left(a _ {2}\right)}{\tau} - 4 \left(\left\| \theta_ {1} \right\| _ {\infty} + 1 / \tau\right) \sqrt {k} \right\} (409) \\ \geq \frac {1}{k} \cdot \exp \left\{- \frac {1}{\tau} - 4 \left(\left\| \theta_ {1} \right\| _ {\infty} + 1 / \tau\right) \sqrt {k} \right\} \quad \left(\text {b e c a u s e} r \in [ 0, 1 ] ^ {k} \text {a n d} r \left(a _ {1}\right) - r \left(a _ {2}\right) \geq - 1\right) (410) \\ = \frac {1}{k} \cdot \frac {1}{\exp \{1 / \tau \}} \cdot \frac {1}{\exp \{4 (\| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} + 1 / \tau) \sqrt {k} \}} = c. \\ \end{array}
$$

theorem 5. let  $\pi_{\theta_t} = \mathrm{softmax}(\theta_t)$ . using update 2 with  $\eta \leq 1 / \tau$ , for all  $t \geq 1$ ,

$$
\left(\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) ^ {\top} r \leq \frac {2 \sqrt {k} \left(\| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} + 1 / \tau\right)}{\exp \left\{\tau \eta \cdot c \cdot (t - 1) \right\}}, \tag {411}
$$

$$
\tilde {\delta} _ {t} \leq \frac {2 \left(\tau \| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} + 1\right) ^ {2} k / \tau}{\exp \left\{2 \tau \eta \cdot c \cdot (t - 1) \right\}}, \tag {412}
$$

where  $\tilde{\delta}_t\coloneqq \pi_\tau^{*}^\top (r - \tau \log \pi_\tau^*) - \pi_{\theta_t}^\top (r - \tau \log \pi_{\theta_t})$  and  $c > 0$  is from lemma 13.

proof. according to hölder's inequality,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) ^ {\top} r \leq \left\| \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} \right\| _ {1} \cdot \| r \| _ {\infty} \quad (\text {b y h o l d e r ' s i n e q u a l i t y}) (413) \\ \leq \left\| \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} \right\| _ {1} \quad \left(\text {b e c a u s e} r \in [ 0, 1 ] ^ {k}\right) (414) \\ \leq \left\| \frac {r}{\tau} - \theta_ {t} + \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{\tau k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {\infty} \quad (\text {b y}) (415) \\ = \frac {1}{\tau} \cdot \left\| \tau \theta_ {t} - r - \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {\infty} (416) \\ \leq \frac {1}{\tau} \cdot \left\| \tau \theta_ {t} - r - \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {2} (417) \\ \leq \frac {1}{\tau} \cdot \frac {2 \left(\tau \| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} + 1\right) \sqrt {k}}{\exp \left\{\tau \eta \sum_ {s = 1} ^ {t - 1} \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {s}} (a) \right\}} \quad (\text {b y}) (418) \\ \leq \frac {2 \sqrt {k}}{\tau} \cdot \frac {\tau \| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} + 1}{\exp \left\{\tau \eta \cdot c \cdot (t - 1) \right\}}. \quad (\text {b y}) (419) \\ \end{array}
$$

on the other hand, we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \pi_ {\tau} ^ {* \top} (r - \tau \log \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}) - \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} ^ {\top} (r - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta_ {t}}) = \pi_ {\tau} ^ {* \top} (r - \tau \log \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}) - \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} ^ {\top} (r - \tau \log \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} + \tau \log \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta_ {t}}) (420) \\ = \left(\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) ^ {\top} \left(r - \tau \log \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}\right) + \tau \cdot d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {t}} \| \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}\right) (421) \\ = \left(\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1} \cdot \tau \cdot \log \sum_ {a} \exp \{r (a) / \tau \} + \tau \cdot d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {t}} \| \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}\right) (422) \\ = \tau \cdot d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {t}} \| \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}\right) (423) \\ \leq \frac {\tau}{2} \cdot \left\| \theta_ {t} - \frac {r}{\tau} - \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{\tau k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {2} \quad (\text {b y l e m m a 2 7}) (424) \\ = \frac {1}{2 \tau} \cdot \left\| \tau \theta_ {t} - r - \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {2} (425) \\ \leq \frac {1}{2 \tau} \cdot \left\| \tau \theta_ {t} - r - \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} (426) \\ \leq \frac {1}{2 \tau} \cdot \frac {4 \left(\tau \| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} + 1\right) ^ {2} k}{\exp \left\{2 \tau \eta \sum_ {s = 1} ^ {t - 1} \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {s}} (a) \right\}} \quad (\text {b y}) (427) \\ \leq \frac {1}{\tau} \cdot \frac {2 (\tau \| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} + 1) ^ {2} k}{\exp \left\{2 \tau \eta \cdot c \cdot (t - 1) \right\}}. \quad \text {(b y} (by lemma 13) \\ \end{array}
$$

# a.2.3. proofs for mdps and entropy regularization

lemma 14 (smoothness).  $\mathbb{h}(\rho, \pi_{\theta})$  is  $(4 + 8\log a) / (1 - \gamma)^3$ -smooth, where  $a = |\mathcal{a}|$  is the total number of actions.

proof. denote  $\mathbb{h}^{\pi_{\theta}}(s) = \mathbb{h}(s,\pi_{\theta})$ . also denote  $\theta_{\alpha} = \theta +\alpha u$ , where  $\alpha \in \mathbb{r}$  and  $u\in \mathbb{r}^{sa}$ . according to eq. (16),

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {h} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}}} (s) = \underset { \begin{array}{c} s _ {0} = s, a _ {t} \sim \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s _ {t}), \\ s _ {t + 1} \sim \mathcal {p} (\cdot | s _ {t}, a _ {t}) \end{array} } {\mathbb {e}} \left[ \sum_ {t = 0} ^ {\infty} - \gamma^ {t} \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} \left(a _ {t} \mid s _ {t}\right) \right] (428) \\ = - \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s) \cdot \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s) + \gamma \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s) \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} \mathcal {p} \left(s ^ {\prime} \mid s, a\right) \cdot \mathbb {h} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}}} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right), (429) \\ \end{array}
$$

which implies,

$$
\mathbb {h} ^ {\pi_ {\theta \alpha}} (s) = e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) h _ {\theta \alpha}, \tag {430}
$$

where  $m(\alpha) = (\mathbf{id} - \gamma p(\alpha))^{-1}$  is defined in eq. (203),  $p(\alpha)$  is defined in eq. (189), and  $h_{\theta_\alpha} \in \mathbb{r}^s$  for  $s \in s$  is given by

$$
h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s) = - \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s) \cdot \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s). \tag {431}
$$

according to eq. (431),  $h_{\theta_{\alpha}}(s) \in [0, \log a]$ ,  $\forall s$ . then we have,

$$
\left\| h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} \right\| _ {\infty} = \max  _ {s} \left| h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s) \right| \leq \log a. \tag {432}
$$

for any state  $s\in s$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \frac {\partial h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)}{\partial \alpha} \right| = \left| \left\langle \frac {\partial h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha}}, \frac {\partial \theta_ {\alpha}}{\partial \alpha} \right\rangle \right| (433) \\ = \left| \left\langle \frac {\partial h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha} (\cdot | s)}, u (s, \cdot) \right\rangle \right| (434) \\ = \left| \left(h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)\right) \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)\right) ^ {\top} u (s, \cdot) \right| (435) \\ \leq \left\| h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)\right) \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s) \right\| _ {1} \cdot \| u (s, \cdot) \| _ {\infty}. (436) \\ \end{array}
$$

the  $\ell_1$  norm is upper bounded as

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)\right) \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s) \right\| _ {1} = \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s) \cdot \left| \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s) - \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s) ^ {\top} \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s) \right| (437) \\ \leq \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s) \cdot \left(\left| \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s) \right| + \left| \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s) ^ {\top} \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s) \right|\right) (438) \\ = - 2 \cdot \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s) \cdot \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s) \leq 2 \cdot \log a. (439) \\ \end{array}
$$

therefore we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \frac {\partial h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha} \right\| _ {\infty} = \max  _ {s} \left| \frac {\partial h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)}{\partial \alpha} \right| (440) \\ \leq \max  _ {s} \| h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)\right) \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s) \| _ {1} \cdot \| u (s, \cdot) \| _ {\infty} (441) \\ \leq 2 \cdot \log a \cdot \| u \| _ {2}. (442) \\ \end{array}
$$

the second derivative w.r.t.  $\alpha$  is

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \frac {\partial^ {2} h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right| = \left| \left(\frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha}} \left\{\frac {\partial h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)}{\partial \alpha} \right\}\right) ^ {\top} \frac {\partial \theta_ {\alpha}}{\partial \alpha} \right| (443) \\ = \left| \left(\frac {\partial^ {2} h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha} ^ {2}} \frac {\partial \theta_ {\alpha}}{\partial \alpha}\right) ^ {\top} \frac {\partial \theta_ {\alpha}}{\partial \alpha} \right| (444) \\ = \left| u (s, \cdot) ^ {\top} \frac {\partial^ {2} h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha} ^ {2} (s , \cdot)} u (s, \cdot) \right|. (445) \\ \end{array}
$$

denote the hessian  $t(s,\theta_{\alpha}) = \frac{\partial^{2}h_{\theta_{\alpha}}(s)}{\partial\theta^{2}(s,\cdot)}$  . then,

$$
\begin{array}{l} t (s, \theta_ {\alpha}) = \frac {\partial^ {2} h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha} ^ {2} (s , \cdot)} = \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha} (s , \cdot)} \left\{\frac {\partial h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha} (s , \cdot)} \right\} (446) \\ = \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha} (s , \cdot)} \left\{\left(\frac {\partial \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha} (s , \cdot)}\right) ^ {\top} \frac {\partial h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)}{\partial \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)} \right\} (447) \\ = \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha} (s , \cdot)} \left\{h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)\right) (- \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)) \right\}. (448) \\ \end{array}
$$

note  $t(s,\theta_{\alpha})\in \mathbb{r}^{a\times a}$  , and  $\forall i,j\in \mathcal{a}$  , the value of  $t(s,\theta_{\alpha})$  is,

$$
\begin{array}{l} t _ {i, j} = \frac {d \left\{\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (i | s) \cdot \left(- \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (i | s) - h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)\right) \right\}}{d \theta_ {\alpha} (s , j)} (449) \\ = \frac {d \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (i | s)}{d \theta_ {\alpha} (s , j)} \cdot \left(- \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (i | s) - h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)\right) + \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (i | s) \cdot \frac {d \left\{- \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (i | s) - h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s) \right\}}{d \theta_ {\alpha} (s , j)} (450) \\ = \left(\delta_ {i j} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (j | s) - \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (i | s) \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (j | s)\right) \cdot \left(- \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (i | s) - h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)\right) (451) \\ + \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (i | s) \cdot \left(- \frac {1}{\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (i | s)} \cdot \left(\delta_ {i j} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (j | s) - \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (i | s) \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (j | s)\right) - \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (j | s) \cdot \left(- \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (j | s) - h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)\right)\right) (452) \\ = \delta_ {i j} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (j | s) \cdot \left(- \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (i | s) - h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s) - 1\right) - \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (i | s) \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (j | s) \cdot \left(- \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (i | s) - h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s) - 1\right) (453) \\ - \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (i | s) \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (j | s) \cdot (- \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (j | s) - h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)). (454) \\ \end{array}
$$

for any vector  $y\in \mathbb{r}^a$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| y ^ {\top} t (s, \theta_ {\alpha}) y \right| = \left| \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {a} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {a} t _ {i, j} y (i) y (j) \right| (455) \\ \leq \left| \sum_ {i} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (i | s) \cdot \left(- \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (i | s) - h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s) - 1\right) \cdot y (i) ^ {2} \right| (456) \\ + 2 \cdot \left| \sum_ {i} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (i | s) \cdot y (i) \sum_ {j} \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (j | s) \cdot \left(- \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (j | s) - h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)\right) \cdot y (j) \right| + \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s) ^ {\top} y\right) ^ {2} (457) \\ = \left| \left(h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)\right) (- \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)) - \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)\right) ^ {\top} (y \odot y) \right| (458) \\ + 2 \cdot \left| \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s) ^ {\top} y\right) \cdot \left(h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)\right) (- \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s))\right) ^ {\top} y \right| + \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s) ^ {\top} y\right) ^ {2} (459) \\ \leq \| h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)\right) (- \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)) \| _ {\infty} \cdot \| y \odot y \| _ {1} + \| \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s) \| _ {\infty} \cdot \| y \odot y \| _ {1} (460) \\ + 2 \cdot \| \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s) \| _ {1} \cdot \| y \| _ {\infty} \cdot \| h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)\right) (- \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)) \| _ {1} \cdot \| y \| _ {\infty} + \| \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s) \| _ {2} ^ {2} \cdot \| y \| _ {2} ^ {2}, (461) \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last inequality is by hölder's inequality. note that  $\| y \odot y \|_1 = \| y \|_2^2$ ,  $\| \pi_{\theta_\alpha}(\cdot |s) \|_\infty \leq \| \pi_{\theta_\alpha}(\cdot |s) \|_1$ ,  $\| \pi_{\theta_\alpha}(\cdot |s) \|_2 \leq \| \pi_{\theta_\alpha}(\cdot |s) \|_1 = 1$ , and  $\| y \|_\infty \leq \| y \|_2$ . the  $\ell_\infty$  norm is upper bounded as

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left. \right.\left\| h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)\right) (- \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)) \right\| _ {\infty} = \max  _ {a} \left| \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s) \cdot \left(- \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s) + \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s) ^ {\top} \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)\right)\right| (462) \\ \leq \max  _ {a} - \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s) \cdot \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (a | s) - \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s) ^ {\top} \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s) (463) \\ \leq \frac {1}{e} + \log a. \quad \left(\operatorname {s i n c e} - x \cdot \log x \leq \frac {1}{e} \text {f o r a l l} x \in [ 0, 1 ]\right) (464) \\ \end{array}
$$

therefore we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| y ^ {\top} t (s, \theta_ {\alpha}) y \right| \leq \| h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)\right) (- \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)) \| _ {\infty} \cdot \| y \| _ {2} ^ {2} (465) \\ + \| y \| _ {2} ^ {2} + 2 \cdot \| h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)\right) (- \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)) \| _ {1} \cdot \| y \| _ {2} ^ {2} + \| y \| _ {2} ^ {2} (466) \\ \leq \left(\frac {1}{e} + \log a + 2\right) \cdot \| y \| _ {2} ^ {2} + 2 \cdot \| h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)\right) (- \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (\cdot | s)) \| _ {1} \cdot \| y \| _ {2} ^ {2} \quad \text {(b y e q . (4 6 2))} (467) \\ \leq \left(\frac {1}{e} + \log a + 2 + 2 \cdot \log a\right) \cdot \| y \| _ {2} ^ {2} \quad (\text {b y e q . (4 3 7)}) (468) \\ \leq 3 \cdot (1 + \log a) \cdot \| y \| _ {2} ^ {2}. (469) \\ \end{array}
$$

according to the above results,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \frac {\partial^ {2} h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right\| _ {\infty} = \max  _ {s} \left| \frac {\partial^ {2} h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right| (470) \\ = \max  _ {s} \left| u (s, \cdot) ^ {\top} \frac {\partial^ {2} h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (s)}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha} ^ {2} (s , \cdot)} u (s, \cdot) \right| (471) \\ = \max  _ {s} | u (s, \cdot) ^ {\top} t (s, \theta_ {\alpha}) u (s, \cdot) | (472) \\ \leq 3 \cdot (1 + \log a) \cdot \max  _ {s} \| u (s, \cdot) \| _ {2} ^ {2} (473) \\ \leq 3 \cdot (1 + \log a) \cdot \| u \| _ {2} ^ {2}. (474) \\ \end{array}
$$

taking derivative w.r.t.  $\alpha$  in eq. (430),

$$
\frac {\partial \mathbb {h} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}}} (s)}{\partial \alpha} = \gamma \cdot e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} + e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha}. \tag {475}
$$

taking second derivative w.r.t.  $\alpha$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\partial^ {2} \mathbb {h} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}}} (s)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} = 2 \gamma^ {2} \cdot e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} + \gamma \cdot e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial^ {2} p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} m (\alpha) h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} (476) \\ + 2 \gamma \cdot e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha} + e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial^ {2} h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha^ {2}}. (477) \\ \end{array}
$$

for the last term,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial^ {2} h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg | \leq \| e _ {s} \| _ {1} \cdot \left\| m (\alpha) \frac {\partial^ {2} h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg \| _ {\infty} (478) \\ \leq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \left\| \frac {\partial^ {2} h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg | _ {\infty} \quad (\text {b y e q . (2 0 8)}) (479) \\ \leq \frac {3 \cdot (1 + \log a)}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \| u \| _ {2} ^ {2}. \quad (\text {b y e q .} (4 7 0)) (480) \\ \end{array}
$$

for the second last term,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \leq \left\| m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \| _ {\infty} (481) \\ \leq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \left\| \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg \| _ {\infty} \quad \text {(b y e q . (2 0 8))} (482) \\ \leq \frac {2 \cdot \| u \| _ {2}}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \left\| m (\alpha) \frac {\partial h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg \| _ {\infty} \quad \text {(b y e q . (1 9 2))} (483) \\ \leq \frac {2 \cdot \| u \| _ {2}}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}} \cdot \left\| \frac {\partial h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg \| _ {\infty} \quad (\text {b y e q . (2 0 8)}) (484) \\ \leq \frac {2 \cdot \| u \| _ {2}}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}} \cdot 2 \cdot \log a \cdot \| u \| _ {2} = \frac {4 \cdot \log a}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}} \cdot \| u \| _ {2} ^ {2}. \quad (\text {b y e q . (4 4 0)}) (485) \\ \end{array}
$$

for the second term,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial^ {2} p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} m (\alpha) h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg | \leq \left\| m (\alpha) \frac {\partial^ {2} p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} m (\alpha) h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg \| _ {\infty} (486) \\ \leq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \left\| \frac {\partial^ {2} p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} m (\alpha) h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg \| _ {\infty} \quad \text {(b y e q . (2 0 8))} (487) \\ \leq \frac {6 \cdot \| u \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \left\| m (\alpha) h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \| _ {\infty} \quad \text {(b y e q . (1 9 7))} (488) \\ \leq \frac {6 \cdot \| u \| _ {2} ^ {2}}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}} \cdot \left\| h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg | _ {\infty} \quad (\text {b y e q . (2 0 8)}) (489) \\ \leq \frac {6 \cdot \log a}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}} \cdot \| u \| _ {2} ^ {2}. \quad (\text {b y e q . (4 3 2)}) (490) \\ \end{array}
$$

for the first term, according to eqs. (192) and (208), eq. (432),

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \leq \left\| m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg \| _ {\infty} (491) \\ \leq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot 2 \cdot \| u \| _ {2} \cdot \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot 2 \cdot \| u \| _ {2} \cdot \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \log a (492) \\ = \frac {4 \cdot \log a}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {3}} \cdot \| u \| _ {2} ^ {2}. (493) \\ \end{array}
$$

combining eqs. (478), (481), (486) and (491) with eq. (476),

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left. \left| \frac {\partial^ {2} \mathbb {h} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}}} (s)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \right| \leq 2 \gamma^ {2} \cdot \left| e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} + \gamma \cdot \left| e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial^ {2} p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} m (\alpha) h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} (494) \\ + 2 \gamma \cdot \left| e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial p (\alpha)}{\partial \alpha} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} + \left| e _ {s} ^ {\top} m (\alpha) \frac {\partial^ {2} h _ {\theta_ {\alpha}}}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} (495) \\ \leq \left(2 \gamma^ {2} \cdot \frac {4 \cdot \log a}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {3}} + \gamma \cdot \frac {6 \cdot \log a}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}} + 2 \gamma \cdot \frac {4 \cdot \log a}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}} + \frac {3 \cdot (1 + \log a)}{1 - \gamma}\right) \cdot \| u \| _ {2} ^ {2} (496) \\ \leq \left(\frac {8 \cdot \log a}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {3}} + \frac {3}{1 - \gamma}\right) \cdot \| u \| _ {2} ^ {2} (497) \\ \leq \frac {4 + 8 \cdot \log a}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {3}} \cdot \| u \| _ {2} ^ {2}, (498) \\ \end{array}
$$

which implies for all  $y\in \mathbb{r}^{sa}$  and  $\theta$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| y ^ {\top} \frac {\partial^ {2} \mathbb {h} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)}{\partial \theta^ {2}} y \right| = \left| \left(\frac {y}{\| y \| _ {2}}\right) ^ {\top} \frac {\partial^ {2} \mathbb {h} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)}{\partial \theta^ {2}} \left(\frac {y}{\| y \| _ {2}}\right) \right| \cdot \| y \| _ {2} ^ {2} (499) \\ \leq \max  _ {\| u \| _ {2} = 1} \left| \left\langle \frac {\partial^ {2} \mathbb {h} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)}{\partial \theta^ {2}} u, u \right\rangle \right| \cdot \| y \| _ {2} ^ {2} (500) \\ = \max  _ {\| u \| _ {2} = 1} \left| \left\langle \frac {\partial^ {2} \mathbb {h} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}}} (s)}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha} ^ {2}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \frac {\partial \theta_ {\alpha}}{\partial \alpha}, \frac {\partial \theta_ {\alpha}}{\partial \alpha} \right\rangle \bigg | \cdot \| y \| _ {2} ^ {2} (501) \\ = \max  _ {\| u \| _ {2} = 1} \left| \left\langle \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta_ {\alpha}} \left\{\frac {\partial \mathbb {h} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}}} (s)}{\partial \alpha} \right\} \right| _ {\alpha = 0}, \frac {\partial \theta_ {\alpha}}{\partial \alpha} \right\rangle \bigg | \cdot \| y \| _ {2} ^ {2} (502) \\ = \max  _ {\| u \| _ {2} = 1} \left| \frac {\partial^ {2} \mathbb {h} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\alpha}}} (s)}{\partial \alpha^ {2}} \right| _ {\alpha = 0} \bigg | \cdot \| y \| _ {2} ^ {2} (503) \\ \leq \frac {4 + 8 \cdot \log a}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {3}} \cdot \| y \| _ {2} ^ {2}. \quad (\text {b y e q . (4 9 4)}) (504) \\ \end{array}
$$

denote  $\theta_{\xi} = \theta + \xi (\theta' - \theta)$ , where  $\xi \in [0,1]$ . according to taylor's theorem,  $\forall s, \forall \theta, \theta'$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \mathbb {h} ^ {\pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}}} (s) - \mathbb {h} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) - \left\langle \frac {\partial \mathbb {h} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)}{\partial \theta}, \theta^ {\prime} - \theta \right\rangle \right| = \frac {1}{2} \cdot \left| \left(\theta^ {\prime} - \theta\right) ^ {\top} \frac {\partial^ {2} \mathbb {h} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\xi}}} (s)}{\partial \theta_ {\xi} ^ {2}} (\theta^ {\prime} - \theta) \right| (505) \\ \leq \frac {2 + 4 \cdot \log a}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {3}} \cdot \| \theta^ {\prime} - \theta \| _ {2} ^ {2}. \quad (\text {b y e q . (4 9 9)}) (506) \\ \end{array}
$$

since  $\mathbb{h}^{\pi_{\theta}}(s)$  is  $(4 + 8\log a) / (1 - \gamma)^{3}$ -smooth,  $\forall s, \mathbb{h}(\rho, \pi_{\theta}) = \mathbb{e}_{s \sim \rho}[\mathbb{h}^{\pi_{\theta}}(s)]$  is also  $(4 + 8\log a) / (1 - \gamma)^{3}$ -smooth.

lemma 15 (non-uniform łojasiewicz). suppose  $\mu(s) > 0$  for all states  $s \in s$  and  $\pi_{\theta}(\cdot|s) = \mathrm{softmax}(\theta(s, \cdot))$ . then,

$$
\left\| \frac {\partial \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta} \right\| _ {2} \geq \frac {\sqrt {2 \tau}}{\sqrt {s}} \cdot \min  _ {s} \sqrt {\mu (s)} \cdot \min  _ {s, a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- \frac {1}{2}} \cdot \left[ \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (\rho) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\rho) \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}}. \tag {507}
$$

proof. according to the definition of soft value functions,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (\rho) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\rho) = \underset { \begin{array}{c} s _ {0} \sim \rho , a _ {t} \sim \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (\cdot | s _ {t}), \\ s _ {t + 1} \sim \mathcal {p} (\cdot | s _ {t}, a _ {t}) \end{array} } {\mathbb {e}} \left[ \sum_ {t = 0} ^ {\infty} \gamma^ {t} \left(r \left(s _ {t}, a _ {t}\right) - \tau \log \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} \left(a _ {t} \mid s _ {t}\right)\right) \right] - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\rho) (508) \\ = \underset {s _ {0} \sim \rho , a _ {t} \sim \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (\cdot | s _ {t}), \\ s _ {t + 1} \sim \mathcal {p} (\cdot | s _ {t}, a _ {t})} {\mathbb {e}} \left[ \sum_ {t = 0} ^ {\infty} \gamma^ {t} \left(r \left(s _ {t}, a _ {t}\right) - \tau \log \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} \left(a _ {t} \mid s _ {t}\right) + \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} \left(s _ {t}\right) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} \left(s _ {t}\right)\right) \right] - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\rho) (509) \\ = \underset {s _ {0} \sim \rho , a _ {t} \sim \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (\cdot | s _ {t}), \\ s _ {t + 1} \sim \mathcal {p} (\cdot | s _ {t}, a _ {t})} {\mathbb {e}} \left[ \sum_ {t = 0} ^ {\infty} \gamma^ {t} \left(r \left(s _ {t}, a _ {t}\right) - \tau \log \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} \left(a _ {t} \mid s _ {t}\right) + \gamma \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} \left(s _ {t + 1}\right) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} \left(s _ {t}\right)\right) \right] (510) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (s) \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (a | s) \cdot \left(r (s, a) - \tau \log \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (a | s) + \gamma \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} \mathcal {p} \left(s ^ {\prime} \mid s, a\right) \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)\right) \right] (511) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (s) \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (a | s) \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) - \tau \log \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (a | s) \right] - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \right]. (512) \\ \end{array}
$$

next, define the "soft greedy policy"  $\bar{\pi}_{\theta}(\cdot |s) = \mathrm{softmax}(\tilde{q}^{\pi_{\theta}}(s,\cdot) / \tau),\forall s$  , i.e.,

$$
\bar {\pi} _ {\theta} (a | s) = \frac {\exp \left\{\tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s , a) / \tau \right\}}{\sum_ {a ^ {\prime}} \exp \left\{\tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s , a ^ {\prime}) / \tau \right\}}, \forall a. \tag {513}
$$

we have,  $\forall s$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (a | s) \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) - \tau \log \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (a | s) \right] \leq \max  _ {\pi (\cdot | s)} \sum_ {a} \pi (a | s) \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) - \tau \log \pi (a | s) \right] (514) \\ = \sum_ {a} \bar {\pi} _ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) - \tau \log \bar {\pi} _ {\theta} (a | s) \right] (515) \\ = \tau \log \sum_ {a} \exp \left\{\tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) / \tau \right\}. (516) \\ \end{array}
$$

also note that,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) = \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \right] (517) \\ = \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) - \tau \log \bar {\pi} _ {\theta} (a | s) + \tau \log \bar {\pi} _ {\theta} (a | s) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \right] (518) \\ = \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) - \tau \log \bar {\pi} _ {\theta} (a | s) \right] - \tau d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s) \| \bar {\pi} _ {\theta} (\cdot | s)\right) (519) \\ = \tau \log \sum_ {a} \exp \left\{\tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) / \tau \right\} - \tau \cdot d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s) \| \bar {\pi} _ {\theta} (\cdot | s)\right). (520) \\ \end{array}
$$

combining the above,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (\rho) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\rho) = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (s) \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (a | s) \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) - \tau \log \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (a | s) \right] - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \right] (521) \\ \leq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (s) \cdot \left[ \tau \log \sum_ {a} \exp \left\{\tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) / \tau \right\} - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \right] (522) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (s) \cdot \tau \cdot d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s) \| \bar {\pi} _ {\theta} (\cdot | s)\right) (523) \\ \leq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (s) \cdot \frac {\tau}{2} \cdot \left\| \frac {\tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s , \cdot)}{\tau} - \theta (s, \cdot) - \frac {(\tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s , \cdot) / \tau - \theta (s , \cdot)) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{a} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {2} \quad (\text {b y l e m m a 2 7}) (524) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (s) \cdot \frac {1}{2 \tau} \cdot \left\| \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, \cdot) - \tau \theta (s, \cdot) - \frac {\left(\tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s , \cdot) - \tau \theta (s , \cdot)\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{a} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {2}, (525) \\ \end{array}
$$

where  $a = |\mathcal{a}|$  is the total number of actions. taking square root of soft sub-optimality,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left[ \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (\rho) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\rho) \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \leq \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 - \gamma}} \cdot \left[ \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (s) \cdot \frac {1}{2 \tau} \cdot \left\| \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, \cdot) - \tau \theta (s, \cdot) - \frac {(\tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s , \cdot) - \tau \theta (s , \cdot)) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{a} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {2} \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}} (526) \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 - \gamma}} \cdot \left[ \sum_ {s} \left(\sqrt {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (s)} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \tau}} \cdot \left\| \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, \cdot) - \tau \theta (s, \cdot) - \frac {\left(\tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s , \cdot) - \tau \theta (s , \cdot)\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{a} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {\infty}\right) ^ {2} \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}} (527) \\ \leq \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 - \gamma}} \cdot \sum_ {s} \sqrt {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (s)} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \tau}} \cdot \left\| \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, \cdot) - \tau \theta (s, \cdot) - \frac {\left(\tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s , \cdot) - \tau \theta (s , \cdot)\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{a} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {\infty} \quad (\text {b y} \| x \| _ {2} \leq \| x \| _ {1}) (528) \\ \leq \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 - \gamma}} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \tau}} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu^ {\theta}} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \sum_ {s} \sqrt {d _ {\mu^ {\theta}} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)} \cdot \left\| \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, \cdot) - \tau \theta (s, \cdot) - \frac {(\tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s , \cdot) - \tau \theta (s , \cdot)) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{a} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {\infty}. (529) \\ \end{array}
$$

on the other hand, the entropy regularized policy gradient norm is lower bounded as

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \frac {\partial \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta} \right\| _ {2} = \left[ \sum_ {s, a} \left(\frac {\partial \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta (s , a)}\right) ^ {2} \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}} (530) \\ = \left[ \sum_ {s} \left\| \frac {\partial \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta (s , \cdot)} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}} (531) \\ \geq \frac {1}{\sqrt {s}} \sum_ {s} \left\| \frac {\partial \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta (s , \cdot)} \right\| _ {2}, \quad \text {(b y c a u c h y - s c h w a r z ,} \| x \| _ {1} = | \langle \mathbf {1}, | x | \rangle | \leq \| \mathbf {1} \| _ {2} \cdot \| x \| _ {2}) (532) \\ \end{array}
$$

which is further lower bounded as

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \frac {\partial \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta} \right\| _ {2} \geq \frac {1}{\sqrt {s}} \cdot \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \left\| h \left(\pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s)\right) \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, \cdot) - \tau \theta (s, \cdot) \right] \right\| _ {2} \quad (\text {b y}) \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {s}} \cdot \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \left\| h \left(\pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s)\right) \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, \cdot) - \tau \theta (s, \cdot) - \frac {\left(\tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s , \cdot) - \tau \theta (s , \cdot)\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{a} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right] \right\| _ {2} \quad (bylemma22) \\ \geq \frac {1}{\sqrt {s}} \cdot \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \left\| \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, \cdot) - \tau \theta (s, \cdot) - \frac {\left(\tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s , \cdot) - \tau \theta (s , \cdot)\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{a} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {2} \quad (\text {b y}) (535) \\ \geq \frac {1}{\sqrt {s}} \cdot \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \left\| \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, \cdot) - \tau \theta (s, \cdot) - \frac {\left(\tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s , \cdot) - \tau \theta (s , \cdot)\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{a} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {\infty}. (536) \\ \end{array}
$$

denote  $\zeta_{\theta}(s) = \tilde{q}^{\pi_{\theta}}(s,\cdot) - \tau \theta (s,\cdot) - \frac{(\tilde{q}^{\pi_{\theta}}(s,\cdot) - \tau\theta(s,\cdot))^{\top}\mathbf{1}}{k}\cdot \mathbf{1}$ . we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \frac {\partial \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta} \right\| _ {2} \geq \frac {1}{\sqrt {s}} \cdot \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \| \zeta_ {\theta} (s) \| _ {\infty} (537) \\ \geq \frac {1}{\sqrt {s}} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 - \gamma}} \cdot \min  _ {s} \sqrt {d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)} \cdot \min  _ {s, a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \sqrt {2 \tau} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- \frac {1}{2}} \cdot \left[ \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 - \gamma}} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \tau}} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \sum_ {s} \sqrt {d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)} \cdot \| \zeta_ {\theta} (s) \| _ {\infty} \right] (538) \\ \geq \frac {1}{\sqrt {s}} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 - \gamma}} \cdot \min  _ {s} \sqrt {d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)} \cdot \min  _ {s, a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \sqrt {2 \tau} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- \frac {1}{2}} \cdot \left[ \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (\rho)} - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\rho) \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}} (539) \\ \geq \frac {\sqrt {2 \tau}}{\sqrt {s}} \cdot \min  _ {s} \sqrt {\mu (s)} \cdot \min  _ {s, a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- \frac {1}{2}} \cdot \left[ \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (\rho)} - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\rho) \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}}, (540) \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last inequality is by  $d_{\mu}^{\pi_{\theta}}(s) \geq (1 - \gamma) \cdot \mu(s)$  (cf. eq. (335)).

lemma 16. using algorithm 1 with the entropy regularized objective, we have  $c \coloneqq \inf_{t \geq 1} \min_{s,a} \pi_{\theta_t}(a|s) > 0$ .

proof. the augmented value function  $\tilde{v}^{\pi_{\theta_t}}(\rho)$  is monotonically increasing following gradient update due to smoothness, i.e., lemmas 7 and 14. it follows then that  $\tilde{v}^{\pi_{\theta_t}}(\rho)$  is upper bounded. indeed,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\rho) = \underset { \begin{array}{c} s _ {0} \sim \rho , a _ {t} \sim \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (\cdot | s _ {t}), \\ s _ {t + 1} \sim \mathcal {p} (\cdot | s _ {t}, a _ {t}) \end{array} } {\mathbb {e}} \left[ \sum_ {t = 0} ^ {\infty} \gamma^ {t} (r \left(s _ {t}, a _ {t}\right) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} \left(a _ {t} \mid s _ {t}\right)) \right] (541) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a | s) \cdot \left(r (s, a) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a | s)\right) \right] (542) \\ \leq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \cdot (1 + \tau \log a) \quad \left(\text {b y} r (s, a) \leq 1 \text {a n d} - \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a | s) \cdot \log \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a | s) \leq \log a\right) (543) \\ \leq \frac {1 + \tau \log a}{1 - \gamma}. (544) \\ \end{array}
$$

according to the monotone convergence theorem,  $\tilde{v}^{\pi_{\theta_t}}(\rho)$  converges to a finite value. suppose  $\pi_{\theta_t}(a|s) \to \pi_{\theta_\infty}(a|s)$ . for any state  $s \in s$ , define the following sets,

$$
\mathcal {a} _ {0} (s) = \left\{a: \pi_ {\theta_ {\infty}} (a | s) = 0 \right\}, \tag {545}
$$

$$
\mathcal {a} _ {+} (s) = \left\{a: \pi_ {\theta_ {\infty}} (a | s) > 0 \right\}. \tag {546}
$$

note that  $\mathcal{a} = \mathcal{a}_0(s) \cup \mathcal{a}_+(s)$  since  $\pi_{\infty}(a|s) \geq 0, \forall a \in \mathcal{a}$ . we prove that for any state  $s \in s$ ,  $\mathcal{a}_0(s) = \emptyset$  by contradiction. suppose  $\exists s \in s$ , such that  $\mathcal{a}_0(s)$  is non-empty. for any  $a_0 \in \mathcal{a}_0(s)$ , we have  $\pi_{\theta_t}(a_0|s) \to \pi_{\theta_\infty}(a_0|s) = 0$ , which implies  $-\log \pi_{\theta_t}(a_0|s) \to \infty$ . there exists  $t_0 \geq 1$ , such that  $\forall t \geq t_0$ ,

$$
- \log \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a _ {0} | s) \geq \frac {1 + \tau \log a}{\tau (1 - \gamma)}. \tag {547}
$$

according to lemma 10,  $\forall t\geq t_0$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\partial \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t} (s , a _ {0})} = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \cdot \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} \left(a _ {0} \mid s\right) \cdot \tilde {a} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a _ {0}) (548) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \cdot \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} \left(a _ {0} \mid s\right) \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a _ {0}) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} \left(a _ {0} \mid s\right) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \right] (549) \\ \geq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \cdot \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} \left(a _ {0} \mid s\right) \cdot \left[ 0 - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} \left(a _ {0} \mid s\right) - \frac {1 + \tau \log a}{1 - \gamma} \right] (550) \\ \geq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \cdot \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} \left(a _ {0} | s\right) \cdot \left[ 0 + \tau \cdot \frac {1 + \tau \log a}{\tau (1 - \gamma)} - \frac {1 + \tau \log a}{1 - \gamma} \right] = 0, (551) \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first inequality is by

$$
\tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a _ {0}) = r (s, a _ {0}) + \gamma \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} \mathcal {p} \left(s ^ {\prime} \mid s, a _ {0}\right) \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) \geq 0. \quad \left(\text {b y} r (s, a _ {0}) \geq 0 \text {a n d} \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) \geq 0\right) \tag {552}
$$

this means that  $\theta_t(s, a_0)$  is increasing for any  $t \geq t_0$ , which in turn implies that  $\theta_{\infty}(s, a_0)$  is lower bounded by constant, i.e.,  $\theta_{\infty}(s, a_0) \geq c$  for some constant  $c$ , and thus  $\exp \left\{\theta_{\infty}(a_0 | s)\right\} \geq e^c > 0$ . according to

$$
\pi_ {\theta_ {\infty}} \left(a _ {0} | s\right) = \frac {\exp \left\{\theta_ {\infty} \left(a _ {0} | s\right) \right\}}{\sum_ {a} \exp \left\{\theta_ {\infty} \left(a | s\right) \right\}} = 0, \tag {553}
$$

we have,

$$
\sum_ {a} \exp \left\{\theta_ {\infty} (a | s) \right\} = \infty . \tag {554}
$$

on the other hand, for any  $a_+ \in \mathcal{a}_+(s)$ , according to

$$
\pi_ {\theta_ {\infty}} (a _ {+} | s) = \frac {\exp \left\{\theta_ {\infty} (a _ {+} | s) \right\}}{\sum_ {a} \exp \left\{\theta_ {\infty} (a | s) \right\}} > 0, \tag {555}
$$

we have,

$$
\exp \left\{\theta_ {\infty} \left(a _ {+} \mid s\right) \right\} = \infty , \quad \forall a _ {+} \in \mathcal {a} _ {+} (s) \tag {556}
$$

which implies,

$$
\sum_ {a _ {+} \in \mathcal {a} _ {+} (s)} \theta_ {\infty} \left(a _ {+} | s\right) = \infty . \tag {557}
$$

note that  $\forall t$ , the summation of logit incremental over all actions is zero:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {a} \frac {\partial \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t} (s , a)} = \sum_ {a _ {0} \in \mathcal {a} _ {0} (s)} \frac {\partial \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t} (s , a _ {0})} + \sum_ {a _ {+} \in \mathcal {a} _ {+} (s)} \frac {\partial \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t} (s , a _ {+})} (558) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a | s) \cdot \tilde {a} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s, a) (559) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \cdot \left[ \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \right] = 0. (560) \\ \end{array}
$$

according to eq. (548),  $\forall t\geq t_0$

$$
\sum_ {a _ {0} \in \mathcal {a} _ {0} (s)} \frac {\partial \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t} (s , a _ {0})} \geq 0. \tag {561}
$$

according to eq. (558),  $\forall t\geq t_0$

$$
\sum_ {a _ {+} \in \mathcal {a} _ {+} (s)} \frac {\partial \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t} (s , a _ {+})} = 0 - \sum_ {a _ {0} \in \mathcal {a} _ {0} (s)} \frac {\partial \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t} (s , a _ {0})} \leq 0. \tag {562}
$$

which means  $\sum_{a_{+} \in \mathcal{a}_{+}(s)} \theta_{t}(s, a_{+})$  will decrease for all large enough  $t \geq 1$ . this contradicts with eq. (557), i.e.,  $\sum_{a_{+} \in \mathcal{a}_{+}(s)} \theta_{t}(s, a_{+}) \to \infty$ .

to this point, we have shown that  $\mathcal{a}_0(s) = \emptyset$  for any state  $s\in s$ , i.e.,  $\pi_{\theta_t}(\cdot |s)$  will converge in the interior of probabilistic simplex  $\delta (\mathcal{a})$ . furthermore, at the convergent point  $\pi_{\theta_{\infty}}(\cdot |s)$ , the gradient is zero, otherwise by smoothness the objective can be further improved, which is a contradiction with convergence. according to lemma 10,  $\forall s$ ,

$$
\frac {\partial \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\infty}} (\mu)}}{\partial \theta_ {\infty} (s , \cdot)} = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\infty}}} (s) \cdot h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {\infty}} (\cdot | s)\right) \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\infty}}} (s, \cdot) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\infty}} (\cdot | s) \right] = \mathbf {0}. \tag {563}
$$

we have  $d_{\mu}^{\pi_{\theta \infty}}(s) \geq (1 - \gamma) \cdot \mu(s) > 0$  for all states  $s$  (cf. eq. (335)). therefore we have,  $\forall s$ ,

$$
h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {\infty}} (\cdot | s)\right) \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\infty}}} (s, \cdot) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\infty}} (\cdot | s) \right] = \mathbf {0}. \tag {564}
$$

according to lemma 22,  $h(\pi_{\theta_{\infty}}(\cdot |s))$  has eigenvalue 0 with multiplicity 1, and its corresponding eigenvector is  $c \cdot 1$  for some constant  $c \in \mathbb{r}$ . therefore, the gradient is zero implies that for all states  $s$ ,

$$
\tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\infty}}} (s, \cdot) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta_ {\infty}} (\cdot | s) = c \cdot \mathbf {1}, \tag {565}
$$

which is equivalent to

$$
\pi_ {\theta_ {\infty}} (\cdot | s) = \operatorname {s o f t m a x} \left(\tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {\infty}}} (s, \cdot) / \tau\right), \tag {566}
$$

which, according to nachum et al. (2017, theorem 3), is the softmax optimal policy  $\pi_{\tau}^{*}$ . since  $\tau \in \omega(1) > 0$  and,

$$
0 \leq \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta \infty}} (s, a) \leq \frac {1 + \tau \log a}{1 - \gamma}, \tag {567}
$$

we have  $\pi_{\theta_{\infty}}(a|s) \in \omega(1), \forall (s, a)$ . since  $\pi_{\theta_t}(a|s) \to \pi_{\theta_{\infty}}(a|s)$ , there exists  $t_0 \geq 1$ , such that  $\forall t \geq t_0$

$$
0. 9 \cdot \pi_ {\theta_ {\infty}} (a | s) \leq \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a | s) \leq 1. 1 \cdot \pi_ {\theta_ {\infty}} (a | s), \forall (s, a), \tag {568}
$$

which means  $\inf_{t\geq t_0}\min_{s,a}\pi_{\theta_t}(a|s)\in \omega (1)$ , and thus

$$
\inf  _ {t \geq 1} \min  _ {s, a} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a | s) = \min  \left\{\min  _ {1 \leq t \leq t _ {0}} \min  _ {s, a} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a | s), \inf  _ {t \geq t _ {0}} \min  _ {s, a} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a | s) \right\} = \min  \{\omega (1), \omega (1) \} \in \omega (1).
$$

theorem 6. suppose  $\mu(s) > 0$  for all state  $s$ . using algorithm 1 with the entropy regularized objective and softmax parametrization and  $\eta = (1 - \gamma)^3 / (8 + \tau(4 + 8\log a))$ , there exists a constant  $c > 0$  such that for all  $t \geq 1$ ,

$$
\tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (\rho) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\rho) \leq \left\| \frac {1}{\mu} \right\| _ {\infty} \cdot \frac {1 + \tau \log a}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}} \cdot e ^ {- c (t - 1)}. \tag {569}
$$

proof. according to the soft sub-optimality lemma of lemma 26,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (\rho) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\rho) = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} \left[ d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \cdot \tau \cdot d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (\cdot | s) \| \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (\cdot | s)\right) \right] (570) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s)}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s)} \cdot \left[ d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \cdot \tau \cdot d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (\cdot | s) \| \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (\cdot | s)\right) \right] (571) \\ \leq \frac {1}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}} \sum_ {s} \frac {1}{\mu (s)} \cdot \left[ d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \cdot \tau \cdot d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (\cdot | s) \| \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (\cdot | s)\right) \right] (572) \\ \leq \frac {1}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}} \cdot \left\| \frac {1}{\mu} \right\| _ {\infty} \sum_ {s} \left[ d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \cdot \tau \cdot d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (\cdot | s) \| \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (\cdot | s)\right) \right] (573) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \left\| \frac {1}{\mu} \right\| _ {\infty} \cdot \left[ \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (\mu) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu) \right], (574) \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last equation is again by lemma 26, and the first inequality is according to  $d_{\mu}^{\pi_{\theta_t}}(s) \geq (1 - \gamma) \cdot \mu(s)$  (cf. eq. (335)). according to lemmas 7 and 14,  $v^{\pi_{\theta}}(\mu)$  is  $8 / (1 - \gamma)^{3}$ -smooth, and  $\mathbb{h}(\mu, \pi_{\theta})$  is  $(4 + 8 \log a) / (1 - \gamma)^{3}$ -smooth. therefore,  $\tilde{v}^{\pi_{\theta}}(\mu) = v^{\pi_{\theta}}(\mu) + \tau \cdot \mathbb{h}(\mu, \pi_{\theta})$  is  $\beta$ -smooth with  $\beta = (8 + \tau (4 + 8 \log a)) / (1 - \gamma)^{3}$ . denote  $\tilde{\delta}_t = \tilde{v}^{\pi_\tau^*}(\mu) - \tilde{v}^{\pi_{\theta_t}}(\mu)$ . and note  $\eta = \frac{(1 - \gamma)^3}{8 + \tau(4 + 8 \log a)}$ . we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde {\delta} _ {t + 1} - \tilde {\delta} _ {t} = \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}}} (\mu) (575) \\ \leq - \frac {(1 - \gamma) ^ {3}}{1 6 + \tau (8 + 1 6 \log a)} \cdot \left\| \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t}} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \quad \text {(b y l e m m a 1 8)} (576) \\ \leq - \frac {(1 - \gamma) ^ {3}}{1 6 + \tau (8 + 1 6 \log a)} \cdot \frac {2 \tau}{s} \cdot \min  _ {s} \mu (s) \cdot \min  _ {s, a} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a | s) ^ {2} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \cdot \left[ \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (\mu) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu) \right] \quad (bylemma15) \\ \leq - \frac {(1 - \gamma) ^ {4}}{(8 / \tau + 4 + 8 \log a) \cdot s} \cdot \min  _ {s} \mu (s) \cdot \min  _ {s, a} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a | s) ^ {2} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}}}{\mu} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \cdot \tilde {\delta} _ {t} \quad (\text {b y} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s) \geq (1 - \gamma) \cdot \mu (s)) (578) \\ \leq - \frac {(1 - \gamma) ^ {4}}{(8 / \tau + 4 + 8 \log a) \cdot s} \cdot \min  _ {s} \mu (s) \cdot \inf  _ {t \geq 1} \min  _ {s, a} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a | s) ^ {2} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}}}{\mu} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \cdot \tilde {\delta} _ {t}, (579) \\ \end{array}
$$

according to lemma 16,  $c = \inf_{t\geq 1}\min_{s,a}\pi_{\theta_t}(a|s) > 0$  is independent with  $t$ . we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde {\delta} _ {t} \leq \left[ 1 - \frac {(1 - \gamma) ^ {4}}{(8 / \tau + 4 + 8 \log a) \cdot s} \cdot \min  _ {s} \mu (s) \cdot c ^ {2} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}}}{\mu} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \right] \cdot \tilde {\delta} _ {t - 1} (580) \\ \leq \exp \left\{- \frac {(1 - \gamma) ^ {4}}{(8 / \tau + 4 + 8 \log a) \cdot s} \cdot \min  _ {s} \mu (s) \cdot c ^ {2} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}}}{\mu} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \right\} \cdot \tilde {\delta} _ {t - 1} (581) \\ \leq \exp \left\{- \frac {(1 - \gamma) ^ {4}}{(8 / \tau + 4 + 8 \log a) \cdot s} \cdot \min  _ {s} \mu (s) \cdot c ^ {2} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}}}{\mu} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \cdot (t - 1) \right\} \cdot \tilde {\delta} _ {1} (582) \\ \leq \exp \left\{- \frac {(1 - \gamma) ^ {4}}{(8 / \tau + 4 + 8 \log a) \cdot s} \cdot \min  _ {s} \mu (s) \cdot c ^ {2} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}}}{\mu} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} \cdot (t - 1) \right\} \cdot \frac {1 + \tau \log a}{1 - \gamma}, (583) \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last inequality is according to eq. (541). therefore we have the final result,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (\rho) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\rho) \leq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \left\| \frac {1}{\mu} \right\| _ {\infty} \cdot \left[ \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (\mu) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu) \right] (584) \\ \leq \frac {1}{\exp \left\{c \cdot (t - 1) \right\}} \cdot \frac {1 + \tau \log a}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}} \cdot \left\| \frac {1}{\mu} \right\| _ {\infty}, (585) \\ \end{array}
$$

where

$$
c = \frac {(1 - \gamma) ^ {4}}{(8 / \tau + 4 + 8 \log a) \cdot s} \cdot \min  _ {s} \mu (s) \cdot c ^ {2} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}}}{\mu} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {- 1} > 0, \tag {586}
$$

is independent with  $t$ .
