# a.2.4. proofs for two-stage and decaying entropy regularization

theorem 7 (two-stage). denote  $\delta = r(a^{*}) - \max_{a\neq a^{*}}r(a) > 0$ . using update 2 for  $t_1\in o(e^{1 / \tau}\cdot \log (\frac{\tau + 1}{\delta}))$  iterations and then update 1 for  $t_2\geq 1$  iterations, we have,

$$
\left(\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) ^ {\top} r \leq 5 / \left(c ^ {2} \cdot t _ {2}\right), \tag {587}
$$

where  $t = t_1 + t_2$ , and  $c \in [1 / k, 1)$ .

proof. in particular, using update 2 with  $\eta \leq 1 / \tau$  for the following number of iterations,

$$
\begin{array}{l} t _ {1} = \frac {1}{\tau \eta} \cdot k \cdot \exp \left\{4 \| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} \sqrt {k} \right\} \cdot \exp \left\{\frac {1 + 4 \sqrt {k}}{\tau} \right\} \cdot \log \left(\frac {4 (\tau \| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} + 1) \sqrt {k}}{\delta}\right) + 1 (588) \\ \in o \left(e ^ {1 / \tau} \cdot \log \left(\frac {\tau + 1}{\delta}\right)\right), (589) \\ \end{array}
$$

we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} t _ {1} - 1 \geq \frac {1}{\tau \eta} \cdot k \cdot \exp \left\{4 \| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} \sqrt {k} \right\} \cdot \exp \left\{\frac {1 + 4 \sqrt {k}}{\tau} \right\} \cdot \log \left(\frac {4 (\tau \| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} + 1) \sqrt {k}}{\delta}\right) (590) \\ = \frac {1}{\tau \eta} \cdot k \cdot \exp \left\{1 / \tau \right\} \cdot \exp \left\{4 \left(\| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} + 1 / \tau\right) \sqrt {k} \right\} \cdot \log \left(\frac {4 (\tau \| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} + 1) \sqrt {k}}{\delta}\right) (591) \\ \geq \frac {1}{\tau \eta} \cdot \frac {1}{c} \cdot \log \left(\frac {4 \left(\tau \| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} + 1\right) \sqrt {k}}{\delta}\right). \quad (c \text {i s f r o m l e m m a 1 3}) (592) \\ \end{array}
$$

therefore we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \log \left(\frac {4 \left(\tau \| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} + 1\right) \sqrt {k}}{\delta}\right) \leq \tau \eta \cdot c \cdot \left(t _ {1} - 1\right) (593) \\ \leq \tau \eta \sum_ {s = 1} ^ {t _ {1} - 1} \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {s}} (a) \quad (\text {b y l e m m a 1 3}) (594) \\ \leq \log \left(\frac {2 \left(\tau \| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} + 1\right) \sqrt {k}}{\| \zeta_ {t _ {1}} \| _ {2}}\right), \quad (\text {b y}) (595) \\ \end{array}
$$

which is equivalent to,

$$
\left\| \zeta_ {t _ {1}} \right\| _ {2} = \left\| \tau \theta_ {t _ {1}} - r - \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t _ {1}} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {2} \leq \frac {\delta}{2}. \tag {596}
$$

then we have, for all  $a$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \theta_ {t _ {1}} (a) - \frac {r (a)}{\tau} - \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t _ {1}} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{\tau k} \right| \leq \left\| \theta_ {t _ {1}} - \frac {r}{\tau} - \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t _ {1}} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{\tau k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {2} (597) \\ = \frac {1}{\tau} \cdot \left\| \tau \theta_ {t _ {1}} - r - \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t _ {1}} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {2} \leq \frac {\delta}{2 \tau}, (598) \\ \end{array}
$$

which implies,

$$
\theta_ {t _ {1}} \left(a ^ {*}\right) \geq \frac {r \left(a ^ {*}\right)}{\tau} - \frac {\delta}{2 \tau} + \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t _ {1}} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{\tau k}, \quad \text {a n d} \tag {599}
$$

$$
\theta_ {t _ {1}} (a) \leq \frac {r (a)}{\tau} + \frac {\delta}{2 \tau} + \frac {\left(\tau \theta_ {t _ {1}} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{\tau k}. \quad \text {f o r a l l} a \neq a ^ {*} \tag {600}
$$

then we have, for all  $a \neq a^*$ ,

$$
\theta_ {t _ {1}} \left(a ^ {*}\right) - \theta_ {t _ {1}} (a) \geq \frac {r \left(a ^ {*}\right)}{\tau} - \frac {\delta}{2 \tau} - \left(\frac {r (a)}{\tau} + \frac {\delta}{2 \tau}\right) \tag {601}
$$

$$
= \frac {r \left(a ^ {*}\right)}{\tau} - \frac {r (a)}{\tau} - \frac {\delta}{\tau} \geq 0, \tag {602}
$$

which means  $\pi_{\theta_{t_1}}(a^*) \geq \pi_{\theta_{t_1}}(a)$ . now we turn off the regularization and use update 1 for  $t_2 \geq 1$  iterations. according to similar arguments as in theorem 3, we have,

$$
\left(\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) ^ {\top} r \leq 5 / \left(c ^ {2} \cdot t _ {2}\right), \tag {603}
$$

where  $t = t_1 + t_2$ , and  $c \in [1 / k, 1)$ .

theorem 8 (decaying entropy regularization). using update 3 with  $\tau_{t} = \frac{\alpha\cdot\delta}{\log t}$  for  $t\geq 2$  , where  $\alpha >0$  , and  $\eta_t = 1 / \tau_t$  , we have, for all  $t\geq 1$

$$
\left(\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) ^ {\top} r \leq \frac {k}{t ^ {1 / \alpha}} + \frac {\log t}{\exp \left\{\sum_ {s = 1} ^ {t - 1} \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {s}} (a) \right\}} \cdot \frac {2 \left(\tau_ {1} \| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} + 1\right) \sqrt {k}}{\alpha \cdot \delta}. \tag {604}
$$

proof. denote  $\pi_{\tau_t}^* = \mathrm{softmax}(r / \tau_t)$  as the softmax optimal policy at time  $t$ . we have,

$$
\left(\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) ^ {\top} r = \underbrace {\left(\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\tau_ {t}} ^ {*}\right) ^ {\top} r} _ {\text {‘ d e c a y i n g ”}} + \underbrace {\left(\pi_ {\tau_ {t}} ^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) ^ {\top} r} _ {\text {‘ t r a c k i n g ”}}. \tag {605}
$$

"decaying" part. note  $a^*$  is the optimal action. denote  $\delta(a) = r(a^*) - r(a)$ , and  $\delta = \min_{a \neq a^*} \delta(a)$ . we have,

$$
\left(\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\tau_ {t}} ^ {*}\right) ^ {\top} r = \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\tau_ {t}} ^ {*} (a) \cdot r \left(a ^ {*}\right) - \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\tau_ {t}} ^ {*} (a) \cdot r (a) = \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} \pi_ {\tau_ {t}} ^ {*} (a) \cdot \delta (a) \tag {606}
$$

$$
= \frac {\sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} e ^ {\frac {r (a)}{\tau_ {t}}} \cdot \delta (a)}{\sum_ {a ^ {\prime}} e ^ {\frac {r \left(a ^ {\prime}\right)}{\tau_ {t}}}} \tag {607}
$$

$$
\leq \frac {1}{e ^ {\frac {r (a ^ {*})}{\tau_ {t}}} + \max  _ {a \neq a ^ {*}} e ^ {\frac {r (a)}{\tau_ {t}}}} \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} e ^ {\frac {r (a)}{\tau_ {t}}} \cdot \delta (a) \right] \quad \left(\sum_ {a ^ {\prime}} e ^ {\frac {r (a ^ {\prime})}{\tau_ {t}}} \geq e ^ {\frac {r (a ^ {*})}{\tau_ {t}}} + \max  _ {a \neq a ^ {*}} e ^ {\frac {r (a)}{\tau_ {t}}}\right) \tag {608}
$$

$$
= \frac {1}{e ^ {\frac {r (a ^ {*})}{\tau_ {t}}} + \max  _ {a \neq a ^ {*}} e ^ {\frac {r (a)}{\tau_ {t}}}} \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} \frac {e ^ {\frac {r (a)}{\tau_ {t}}} \cdot \delta (a)}{e ^ {\frac {r (a ^ {*})}{\tau_ {t}}} + e ^ {\frac {r (a)}{\tau_ {t}}}} \cdot \left(e ^ {\frac {r (a ^ {*})}{\tau_ {t}}} + e ^ {\frac {r (a)}{\tau_ {t}}}\right) \right] \tag {609}
$$

$$
\leq \frac {1}{e ^ {\frac {r (a ^ {*})}{\tau_ {t}}} + \max  _ {a \neq a ^ {*}} e ^ {\frac {r (a)}{\tau_ {t}}}} \cdot \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} \frac {e ^ {\frac {r (a)}{\tau_ {t}}} \cdot \delta (a)}{e ^ {\frac {r (a ^ {*})}{\tau_ {t}}} + e ^ {\frac {r (a)}{\tau_ {t}}}} \cdot \left(e ^ {\frac {r (a ^ {*})}{\tau_ {t}}} + \max  _ {a \neq a ^ {*}} e ^ {\frac {r (a)}{\tau_ {t}}}\right) \quad (\text {b y}
$$

$$
= \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} \frac {e ^ {\frac {r (a)}{\tau_ {t}}} \cdot \delta (a)}{e ^ {\frac {r (a ^ {*})}{\tau_ {t}}} + e ^ {\frac {r (a)}{\tau_ {t}}}} = \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} \frac {\delta (a)}{e ^ {\frac {\delta (a)}{\tau_ {t}}} + 1} \leq \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} \frac {1}{e ^ {\frac {\delta}{\tau_ {t}}} + 1} = \frac {k - 1}{1 + e ^ {\frac {\delta}{\tau_ {t}}}} \leq \frac {k}{e ^ {\frac {\delta}{\tau_ {t}}}}. \tag {611}
$$

using the decaying temperature  $\tau_{t} = \frac{\alpha\cdot\delta}{\log t}$ , for  $t\geq 2$ , where  $\alpha >0$ , we have,

$$
\left(\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\tau_ {t}} ^ {*}\right) ^ {\top} r \leq \frac {k}{t ^ {1 / \alpha}}. \tag {612}
$$

"tracking" part. using update 3, we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tau_ {t + 1} \theta_ {t + 1} - r - \frac {\left(\tau_ {t + 1} \theta_ {t + 1} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} = \tau_ {t} \theta_ {t} - r - \frac {\left(\tau_ {t} \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} (613) \\ + \left(\tau_ {t + 1} \theta_ {t + 1} - \tau_ {t} \theta_ {t}\right) + \left(\frac {\left(\tau_ {t} \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} - \frac {\left(\tau_ {t + 1} \theta_ {t + 1} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k}\right) \cdot \mathbf {1} (614) \\ = \tau_ {t} \theta_ {t} - r - \frac {\left(\tau_ {t} \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} + \tau_ {t} \eta_ {t} \cdot h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) \left(r - \tau_ {t} \log \pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) + \frac {\left(\tau_ {t} \theta_ {t} - \tau_ {t + 1} \theta_ {t + 1}\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \quad (\text {b y}) (615) \\ = \left(\mathbf {i} \mathbf {d} - \tau_ {t} \eta_ {t} \cdot h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right)\right) \left(\tau_ {t} \theta_ {t} - r - \frac {\left(\tau_ {t} \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1}\right) \quad \left(h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) \mathbf {1} = h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1} = \mathbf {0}, \text {c f . e q . (3 7 8)}\right) (616) \\ = \left(\mathbf {i} \mathbf {d} - h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right)\right) \left(\tau_ {t} \theta_ {t} - r - \frac {\left(\tau_ {t} \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1}\right) \quad \left(\eta_ {t} = 1 / \tau_ {t}\right). (617) \\ \end{array}
$$

therefore we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \tau_ {t + 1} \theta_ {t + 1} - r - \frac {\left(\tau_ {t + 1} \theta_ {t + 1} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {2} = \left\| (\mathbf {i d} - h \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right)) \left(\tau_ {t} \theta_ {t} - r - \frac {\left(\tau_ {t} \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1}\right) \right\| _ {2} (618) \\ \leq \left(1 - \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a)\right) \cdot \left\| \tau_ {t} \theta_ {t} - r - \frac {\left(\tau_ {t} \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {2} \quad (\text {b y}) (619) \\ \leq \exp \left\{- \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} (a) \right\} \cdot \left\| \tau_ {t} \theta_ {t} - r - \frac {\left(\tau_ {t} \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {2}. (620) \\ \end{array}
$$

then we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left. \left(\pi_ {\tau_ {t}} ^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) ^ {\top} r \leq \left\| \pi_ {\tau_ {t}} ^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} \right\| _ {1} \quad (\text {b y h o l d e r s i n e q u a l i t y , a n d} \| r \| _ {\infty} \leq 1) \right. (621) \\ \leq \left\| \theta_ {t} - \frac {r}{\tau_ {t}} - \frac {\left(\tau_ {t} \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{\tau_ {t} k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {\infty} \quad (\text {b y}) (622) \\ \leq \frac {1}{\tau_ {t}} \cdot \left\| \tau_ {t} \theta_ {t} - r - \frac {\left(\tau_ {t} \theta_ {t} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {2} \quad (\| x \| _ {\infty} \leq \| x \| _ {2}) (623) \\ \leq \frac {1}{\tau_ {t}} \cdot \exp \left\{- \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {t - 1}} (a) \right\} \cdot \left\| \tau_ {t - 1} \theta_ {t - 1} - r - \frac {\left(\tau_ {t - 1} \theta_ {t - 1} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {2} \quad (\text {b y e q . (6 1 8)}) (624) \\ \leq \frac {1}{\tau_ {t}} \cdot \exp \left\{- \sum_ {s = 1} ^ {t - 1} \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {s}} (a) \right\} \cdot \left\| \tau_ {1} \theta_ {1} - r - \frac {\left(\tau_ {1} \theta_ {1} - r\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {2} (625) \\ \leq \frac {1}{\tau_ {t}} \cdot \exp \left\{- \sum_ {s = 1} ^ {t - 1} \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {s}} (a) \right\} \cdot 2 \left(\tau_ {1} \| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} + 1\right) \sqrt {k} \quad (\text {b y e q . (3 8 9)}) (626) \\ = \frac {\log t}{\exp \left\{\sum_ {s = 1} ^ {t - 1} \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta_ {s}} (a) \right\}} \cdot \frac {2 (\tau_ {1} \| \theta_ {1} \| _ {\infty} + 1) \sqrt {k}}{\alpha \cdot \delta}. \\ \end{array}
$$

# a.3. proofs for section 5 (does entropy regularization really help?)

# a.3.1. proofs for the bandit case

lemma 17 (reversed łojasiewicz). take any  $r \in [0,1]^k$ . denote  $\delta = r(a^*) - \max_{a \neq a^*} r(a) > 0$ . then,

$$
\left\| \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta} \right\| _ {2} \leq \frac {\sqrt {2}}{\delta} \cdot \left(\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta}\right) ^ {\top} r. \tag {627}
$$

proof. note  $a^*$  is the optimal action. denote  $\delta(a) = r(a^*) - r(a)$ , and  $\delta = \min_{a \neq a^*} \delta(a)$ .

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta}\right) ^ {\top} r = \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a) \cdot r \left(a ^ {*}\right) - \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a) \cdot r (a) (628) \\ = \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} \pi_ {\theta} (a) \cdot r \left(a ^ {*}\right) - \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} \pi_ {\theta} (a) \cdot r (a) (629) \\ = \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} \pi_ {\theta} (a) \cdot \delta (a) (630) \\ \geq \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} \pi_ {\theta} (a) \cdot \delta . (631) \\ \end{array}
$$

on the other hand,

$$
0 \leq r \left(a ^ {*}\right) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r = \left(\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta}\right) ^ {\top} r = \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} \pi_ {\theta} (a) \cdot \delta (a) \leq \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} \pi_ {\theta} (a) \cdot 1 = \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} \pi_ {\theta} (a). \tag {632}
$$

therefore the  $\ell_2$  norm of gradient can be upper bounded as

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta} \right\| _ {2} = \left(\pi_ {\theta} \left(a ^ {*}\right) ^ {2} \cdot \left[ r \left(a ^ {*}\right) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r \right] ^ {2} + \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} \left[ \pi_ {\theta} (a) ^ {2} \cdot \left(r (a) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r\right) ^ {2} \right]\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} (633) \\ \leq \left(1 ^ {2} \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} \pi_ {\theta} (a) \right] ^ {2} + \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} \left[ \pi_ {\theta} (a) ^ {2} \cdot 1 ^ {2} \right]\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} (634) \\ \leq \left(\left[ \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} \pi_ {\theta} (a) \right] ^ {2} + \left[ \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} \pi_ {\theta} (a) \right] ^ {2}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \quad (\text {b y} \| x \| _ {2} \leq \| x \| _ {1}) (635) \\ = \sqrt {2} \cdot \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} \pi_ {\theta} (a). (636) \\ \end{array}
$$

combining the results, we have

$$
\left\| \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta} \right\| _ {2} \leq \sqrt {2} \cdot \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} \pi_ {\theta} (a) = \frac {\sqrt {2}}{\delta} \cdot \delta \cdot \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*}} \pi_ {\theta} (a) \leq \frac {\sqrt {2}}{\delta} \cdot (\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta}) ^ {\top} r.
$$

theorem 9 (lower bound). take any  $r \in [0,1]^k$ . for large enough  $t \geq 1$ , using update 1 with learning rate  $\eta_t \in (0,1]$

$$
\left(\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) ^ {\top} r \geq \frac {\delta^ {2}}{6 \cdot t}.
$$

proof. denote  $\delta_t = (\pi^* - \pi_{\theta_t})^\top r > 0$ . let  $\theta_{t+1} = \theta_t + \eta_t \cdot \frac{d\pi_{\theta_t}^\top r}{d\theta_t}$ , and  $\pi_{\theta_{t+1}} = \operatorname{softmax}(\theta_{t+1})$  be the next policy after one step gradient update. we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \delta_ {t} - \delta_ {t + 1} = \left(\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}} - \pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) ^ {\top} r - \left\langle \frac {d \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta_ {t}}, \theta_ {t + 1} - \theta_ {t} \right\rangle + \left\langle \frac {d \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta_ {t}}, \theta_ {t + 1} - \theta_ {t} \right\rangle (637) \\ \leq \frac {5}{4} \cdot \| \theta_ {t + 1} - \theta_ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {2} + \left\langle \frac {d \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta_ {t}}, \theta_ {t + 1} - \theta_ {t} \right\rangle \quad (\text {b y}) (638) \\ = \left(\frac {5 \eta_ {t} ^ {2}}{4} + \eta_ {t}\right) \cdot \left\| \frac {d \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta_ {t}} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \quad \left(\text {b y} \theta_ {t + 1} = \theta_ {t} + \eta_ {t} \cdot \frac {d \pi_ {\theta_ {t}} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta_ {t}}\right) (639) \\ \leq \frac {9}{2} \cdot \frac {1}{\delta^ {2}} \cdot \delta_ {t} ^ {2}. \quad (\text {b y} \eta_ {t} \in (0, 1 ] \text {a n d b y l e m m a 1 7}) (640) \\ \end{array}
$$

according to convergence result theorem 2 we have  $\delta_t > 0$ ,  $\delta_t \to 0$  as  $t \to \infty$ . we prove that for all large enough  $t \geq 1$ ,  $\delta_t \leq \frac{10}{9} \cdot \delta_{t+1}$  by contradiction. suppose  $\delta_t > \frac{10}{9} \cdot \delta_{t+1}$ .

$$
\begin{array}{l} \delta_ {t + 1} \geq \delta_ {t} - \frac {9}{2} \cdot \frac {1}{\delta^ {2}} \cdot \delta_ {t} ^ {2} (641) \\ > \frac {1 0}{9} \cdot \delta_ {t + 1} - \frac {9}{2} \cdot \frac {1}{\delta^ {2}} \cdot \left(\frac {1 0}{9} \cdot \delta_ {t + 1}\right) ^ {2} \quad \left(\text {s i n c e} f (x) = x - a x ^ {2} \text {i s i n c r e a s i n g f o r a l l} x <   \frac {1}{2 a} \text {a n d} a > 0\right) (642) \\ = \frac {1 0}{9} \cdot \delta_ {t + 1} - \frac {5 0}{9} \cdot \frac {1}{\delta^ {2}} \cdot \delta_ {t + 1} ^ {2}, (643) \\ \end{array}
$$

which implies  $\delta_{t + 1} > \frac{\delta^2}{50}$  for large enough  $t\geq 1$ . this is a contradiction with  $\delta_t\to 0$  as  $t\to \infty$ . now we have  $\delta_t\leq \frac{10}{9}\cdot \delta_{t + 1}$ . divide both sides of  $\delta_t - \delta_{t + 1}\leq \frac{9}{2}\cdot \frac{1}{\delta^2}\cdot \delta_t^2$  by  $\delta_t\cdot \delta_{t + 1}$ ,

$$
\frac {1}{\delta_ {t + 1}} - \frac {1}{\delta_ {t}} \leq \frac {9}{2} \cdot \frac {1}{\delta^ {2}} \cdot \frac {\delta_ {t}}{\delta_ {t + 1}} \leq \frac {9}{2} \cdot \frac {1}{\delta^ {2}} \cdot \frac {1 0}{9} = \frac {5}{\delta^ {2}}. \tag {644}
$$

summing up from  $t_{1}$  (some large enough time) to  $t_{1} + t$ , we have

$$
\frac {1}{\delta_ {t _ {1} + t}} - \frac {1}{\delta_ {t _ {1}}} \leq \frac {5}{\delta^ {2}} \cdot (t - 1) \leq \frac {5}{\delta^ {2}} \cdot t. \tag {645}
$$

since  $t_{1}$  is a finite time,  $\delta_{t_1} \geq 1 / c$  for some constant  $c > 0$ . rearranging, we have

$$
\left(\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta_ {t _ {1} + t}}\right) ^ {\top} r = \delta_ {t _ {1} + t} \geq \frac {1}{\frac {1}{\delta_ {t _ {1}}} + \frac {5}{\delta^ {2}} \cdot t} \geq \frac {1}{c + \frac {5}{\delta^ {2}} \cdot t} \geq \frac {1}{c + \frac {5}{\delta^ {2}} \cdot \left(t _ {1} + t\right)}. \tag {646}
$$

by abusing notation  $t\coloneqq t_1 + t$  and  $c\leq \frac{t}{\delta^2}$  , we have

$$
\left(\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta_ {t}}\right) ^ {\top} r \geq \frac {1}{c + \frac {5}{\delta^ {2}} \cdot t} \geq \frac {1}{\frac {t}{\delta^ {2}} + \frac {5}{\delta^ {2}} \cdot t} = \frac {\delta^ {2}}{6 \cdot t}, \tag {647}
$$

for all large enough  $t \geq 1$ .

# a.3.2. proofs for general mdps

theorem 10 (lower bound). take any mdp. for large enough  $t\geq 1$  , using algorithm 1 with  $\eta_t\in (0,1]$

$$
v ^ {*} (\mu) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu) \geq \frac {(1 - \gamma) ^ {5} \cdot \left(\delta^ {*}\right) ^ {2}}{1 2 \cdot t}, \tag {648}
$$

where  $\delta^{*} = \min_{s\in s,a\neq a^{*}(s)}\{q^{*}(s,a^{*}(s)) - q^{*}(s,a)\} >0$  is the optimal value gap of the mdp, and  $a^{\ast}(s) = \arg \max_{a}\pi^{*}(a|s)$  is the action that the optimal policy selects under state  $s$ .

proof. suppose algorithm 1 can converge faster than  $o(1 / t)$  for general mdps, then it can converge faster than  $o(1 / t)$  for any one-state mdps, which are special cases of general mdps. this is a contradiction with theorem 9.

the above one-sentence argument implies a  $\omega(1/t)$  rate lower bound. to calculate the constant in the lower bound, we need results similar to lemma 17. according to the reversed lojasiewicz inequality of lemma 28,

$$
\left\| \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t}} \right\| _ {2} \leq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \frac {\sqrt {2}}{\delta^ {*}} \cdot \delta_ {t}, \tag {649}
$$

where  $\delta_t = v^*(\mu) - v^{\pi_{\theta_t}}(\mu) > 0$ . let  $\theta_{t+1} = \theta_t + \eta_t \cdot \frac{\partial v^{\pi_{\theta_t}}(\mu)}{\partial \theta_t}$ , and  $\pi_{\theta_{t+1}}(\cdot|s) = \text{softmax}(\theta_{t+1}(s, \cdot))$ ,  $\forall s \in s$  be the next policy after one step gradient update. using similar calculations as in eq. (637),

$$
\begin{array}{l} \delta_ {t} - \delta_ {t + 1} = v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}}} (\mu) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu) - \left\langle \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t}}, \theta_ {t + 1} - \theta_ {t} \right\rangle + \left\langle \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t}}, \theta_ {t + 1} - \theta_ {t} \right\rangle (650) \\ \leq \frac {4}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {3}} \cdot \| \theta_ {t + 1} - \theta_ {t} \| _ {2} ^ {2} + \left\langle \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t}}, \theta_ {t + 1} - \theta_ {t} \right\rangle \quad \text {(b y} (651) \\ = \left(\frac {4 \eta_ {t} ^ {2}}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {3}} + \eta_ {t}\right) \cdot \left\| \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t}} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \quad \left(\text {b y} \theta_ {t + 1} = \theta_ {t} + \eta_ {t} \cdot \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta_ {t}}\right) (652) \\ \leq \frac {1 0}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {5}} \cdot \frac {1}{(\delta^ {*}) ^ {2}} \cdot \delta_ {t} ^ {2}. \quad (\text {b y} \eta_ {t} \in (0, 1 ] \text {a n d b y l e m m a} 2 8) (653) \\ \end{array}
$$

according to theorem 4, we have  $\delta_t > 0$ ,  $\delta_t \to 0$  as  $t \to \infty$ . using similar arguments as in eq. (641), we can show that for all large enough  $t \geq 1$ ,  $\delta_t \leq \frac{11}{10} \cdot \delta_{t+1}$ . divide both sides of  $\delta_t - \delta_{t+1} \leq \frac{10}{(1-\gamma)^5} \cdot \frac{1}{(\delta^*)^2} \cdot \delta_t^2$  by  $\delta_t \cdot \delta_{t+1}$ ,

$$
\frac {1}{\delta_ {t + 1}} - \frac {1}{\delta_ {t}} \leq \frac {1 0}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {5}} \cdot \frac {1}{(\delta^ {*}) ^ {2}} \cdot \frac {\delta_ {t}}{\delta_ {t + 1}} \leq \frac {1 0}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {5}} \cdot \frac {1}{(\delta^ {*}) ^ {2}} \cdot \frac {1 1}{1 0} = \frac {1 1}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {5} \cdot (\delta^ {*}) ^ {2}}. \tag {654}
$$

using similar calculations as in the proof of theorem 9, we have,

$$
v ^ {*} (\mu) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (\mu) = \delta_ {t} \geq \frac {(1 - \gamma) ^ {5} \cdot \left(\delta^ {*}\right) ^ {2}}{1 2 \cdot t}, \tag {655}
$$

for all large enough  $t \geq 1$ .

# a.3.3. proofs for the non-uniform lojasiewicz degree

proposition 4. let  $r \in [0,1]^k$  be arbitrary and consider  $\theta \mapsto \mathbb{e}_{a \sim \pi_\theta}[r(a)]$ . the non-uniform łojasiewicz degree of this map with constant  $c(\theta) = \pi_\theta(a^*)$  is zero.

proof. we prove by contradiction. suppose the łojasiewicz degree of  $\mathbb{e}_{a\sim \pi_{\theta}}[r(a)]$  can be larger than 0. then there exists  $\xi >0$ , such that,

$$
\left\| \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta} \right\| _ {2} \geq c (\theta) \cdot \left[ \left(\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta}\right) ^ {\top} r \right] ^ {1 - \xi}. \tag {656}
$$

consider the following example,  $r = (0.6, 0.4, 0.2)^{\top}$ ,  $\pi_{\theta} = (1 - 3\epsilon, 2\epsilon, \epsilon)^{\top}$  with small number  $\epsilon > 0$ .

$$
\left(\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta}\right) ^ {\top} r = r \left(a ^ {*}\right) - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r = 0. 6 - (0. 6 - 0. 8 \epsilon) = 0. 8 \cdot \epsilon . \tag {657}
$$

according to the reversed łojasiewicz inequality of lemma 17,

$$
\left\| \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta} \right\| _ {2} \leq \frac {\sqrt {2}}{\delta} \cdot (\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta}) ^ {\top} r = \frac {\sqrt {2}}{2} \cdot (\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta}) ^ {\top} r \leq \frac {1 . 5}{2} \cdot (\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta}) ^ {\top} r = 0. 6 \cdot \epsilon . \tag {658}
$$

also note that  $\pi_{\theta}(a^{*}) = 1 - 3\epsilon > 1/4$ . then for  $\xi \in (0,1]$ , we have

$$
\left\| \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta} \right\| _ {2} \leq 0. 6 \cdot \epsilon = \frac {1}{4} \cdot 3 \cdot 0. 8 \cdot \epsilon <   \pi_ {\theta} (a ^ {*}) \cdot 3 \cdot 0. 8 \cdot \epsilon = c (\theta) \cdot 3 \cdot 0. 8 \cdot \epsilon . \tag {659}
$$

next, since  $\epsilon > 0$  can be very small,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \frac {d \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} r}{d \theta} \right\| _ {2} <   c (\theta) \cdot 3 \cdot 0. 8 \cdot \epsilon = c (\theta) \cdot 3 \cdot (0. 8 \cdot \epsilon) ^ {\xi} \cdot (0. 8 \cdot \epsilon) ^ {1 - \xi} (660) \\ <   c (\theta) \cdot (0. 8 \cdot \epsilon) ^ {1 - \xi} = c (\theta) \cdot \left[ \left(\pi^ {*} - \pi_ {\theta}\right) ^ {\top} r \right] ^ {1 - \xi}, (661) \\ \end{array}
$$

where the second inequality is by  $(0.8\cdot \epsilon)^{\xi} < 1 / 3$  for small  $\epsilon >0$  since  $\xi >0$ . this is a contradiction with the assumption. therefore the łojasiewicz degree  $\xi$  cannot be larger than 0.

proposition 5. fix  $\tau >0$ . with  $c(\theta) = \sqrt{2\tau}\cdot \min_{a}\pi_{\theta}(a)$ , the łojasiewicz degree of  $\theta \mapsto \mathbb{e}_{a\sim \pi_{\theta}}[r(a) - \tau \log \pi_{\theta}(a)]$  is at least  $1 / 2$ .

proof. denote  $\delta_{\theta} = \mathbb{e}_{a\sim \pi_{\tau}^{*}}[r(a) - \tau \log \pi_{\tau}^{*}(a)] - \mathbb{e}_{a\sim \pi_{\theta}}[r(a) - \tau \log \pi_{\theta}(a)]$  as the soft sub-optimality. we have,

$$
\delta_ {\theta} = \underset {a \sim \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} {\mathbb {e}} [ r (a) - \tau \log \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (a) ] - \underset {a \sim \pi_ {\theta}} {\mathbb {e}} [ r (a) - \tau \log \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (a) ] - \underset {a \sim \pi_ {\theta}} {\mathbb {e}} [ \tau \log \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (a) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta} (a) ] \tag {662}
$$

$$
= \tau \log \sum_ {a} \exp \left\{r (a) / \tau \right\} - \tau \log \sum_ {a} \exp \left\{r (a) / \tau \right\} + \tau \cdot d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi_ {\theta} \| \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}\right) \quad (\text {s i n c e} \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} = \operatorname {s o f t m a x} (r / \tau)) \tag {663}
$$

$$
= \tau \cdot d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi_ {\theta} \| \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}\right) \tag {664}
$$

$$
\leq \frac {\tau}{2} \cdot \left\| \frac {r}{\tau} - \theta - \frac {(r / \tau - \theta) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {2} \quad (\text {b y}) \tag {665}
$$

$$
= \frac {1}{2 \tau} \cdot \left\| r - \tau \theta - \frac {\left(r - \tau \theta\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {2}. \tag {666}
$$

next, the entropy regularized policy gradient w.r.t.  $\theta$  is

$$
\frac {d \left\{\pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} \left(r - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta}\right) \right\}}{d \theta} = h \left(\pi_ {\theta}\right) \left(r - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta}\right) \tag {667}
$$

$$
= h \left(\pi_ {\theta}\right) \left(r - \tau \theta + \tau \log \sum_ {a} \exp \{\theta (a) \} \cdot \mathbf {1}\right) \tag {668}
$$

$$
= h \left(\pi_ {\theta}\right) (r - \tau \theta) \tag {669}
$$

$$
= h \left(\pi_ {\theta}\right) \left(r - \tau \theta - \frac {(r - \tau \theta) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1}\right), \tag {670}
$$

where the last two equations are by  $h(\pi_{\theta}) \mathbf{1} = \mathbf{0}$  as shown in lemma 22. then we have,

$$
\left\| \frac {d \left\{\pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} (r - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta}) \right\}}{d \theta} \right\| _ {2} = \left\| h \left(\pi_ {\theta}\right) \left(r - \tau \theta - \frac {(r - \tau \theta) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1}\right) \right\| _ {2} \tag {671}
$$

$$
\geq \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a) \cdot \left\| r - \tau \theta - \frac {(r - \tau \theta) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {2} \quad (\text {b y}) \tag {672}
$$

$$
\geq \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a) \cdot \left\| r - \tau \theta - \frac {(r - \tau \theta) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {\infty} \tag {673}
$$

$$
\geq \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a) \cdot \sqrt {2 \tau} \cdot \sqrt {\delta_ {\theta}} \quad (\text {b y e q .} (6 6 2)) \tag {674}
$$

$$
= \sqrt {2 \tau} \cdot \min  _ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a) \cdot \left(\mathbb {e} _ {a \sim \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} [ r (a) - \tau \log \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (a) ] - \mathbb {e} _ {a \sim \pi_ {\theta}} [ r (a) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta} (a) ]\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}}, \tag {675}
$$

which means the łojasiewicz degree of  $\mathbb{e}_{a\sim \pi_{\theta}}[r(a) - \tau \log \pi_{\theta}(a)]$  is  $1 / 2$  and  $c(\theta) = \sqrt{2\tau}\cdot \min_a\pi_\theta (a)$ .

# b. miscellaneous extra supporting results

lemma 18 (ascent lemma for smooth function). let  $f: \mathbb{r}^d \to \mathbb{r}$  be a  $\beta$ -smooth function,  $\theta \in \mathbb{r}^d$  and  $\theta' = \theta + \frac{1}{\beta} \cdot \frac{\partial f(\theta)}{\partial \theta}$ . we have,

$$
f (\theta) - f \left(\theta^ {\prime}\right) \leq - \frac {1}{2 \beta} \cdot \left\| \frac {\partial f (\theta)}{\partial \theta} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2}. \tag {676}
$$

proof. according to the definition of smoothness, we have,

$$
\left| f \left(\theta^ {\prime}\right) - f (\theta) - \left\langle \frac {\partial f (\theta)}{\partial \theta}, \theta^ {\prime} - \theta \right\rangle \right| \leq \frac {\beta}{2} \cdot \| \theta^ {\prime} - \theta \| _ {2} ^ {2}, \tag {677}
$$

which implies,

$$
\begin{array}{l} f (\theta) - f \left(\theta^ {\prime}\right) \leq - \left\langle \frac {\partial f (\theta)}{\partial \theta}, \theta^ {\prime} - \theta \right\rangle + \frac {\beta}{2} \cdot \| \theta^ {\prime} - \theta \| _ {2} ^ {2} (678) \\ = - \frac {1}{\beta} \cdot \left\| \frac {\partial f (\theta)}{\partial \theta} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {\beta}{2} \cdot \frac {1}{\beta^ {2}} \cdot \left\| \frac {\partial f (\theta)}{\partial \theta} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} \quad \left(\theta^ {\prime} = \theta + \frac {1}{\beta} \cdot \frac {\partial f (\theta)}{\partial \theta}\right) (679) \\ = - \frac {1}{2 \beta} \cdot \left\| \frac {\partial f (\theta)}{\partial \theta} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

lemma 19 (first performance difference lemma (kakade & langford, 2002)). for any policies  $\pi$  and  $\pi'$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} v ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} (\rho) - v ^ {\pi} (\rho) = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} (s) \sum_ {a} \left(\pi^ {\prime} (a | s) - \pi (a | s)\right) \cdot q ^ {\pi} (s, a) (680) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} (s) \sum_ {a} \pi^ {\prime} (a | s) \cdot a ^ {\pi} (s, a). (681) \\ \end{array}
$$

proof. according to the definition of value function,

$$
\begin{array}{l} v ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} (s) - v ^ {\pi} (s) = \sum_ {a} \pi^ {\prime} (a | s) \cdot q ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} (s, a) - \sum_ {a} \pi (a | s) \cdot q ^ {\pi} (s, a) (682) \\ = \sum_ {a} \pi^ {\prime} (a | s) \cdot \left(q ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} (s, a) - q ^ {\pi} (s, a)\right) + \sum_ {a} \left(\pi^ {\prime} (a | s) - \pi (a | s)\right) \cdot q ^ {\pi} (s, a) (683) \\ = \sum_ {a} \left(\pi^ {\prime} (a | s) - \pi (a | s)\right) \cdot q ^ {\pi} (s, a) + \gamma \sum_ {a} \pi^ {\prime} (a | s) \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} \mathcal {p} \left(s ^ {\prime} | s, a\right) \cdot \left[ v ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) - v ^ {\pi} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) \right] (684) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} d _ {s} ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) \sum_ {a ^ {\prime}} \left(\pi^ {\prime} \left(a ^ {\prime} \mid s ^ {\prime}\right) - \pi \left(a ^ {\prime} \mid s ^ {\prime}\right)\right) \cdot q ^ {\pi} \left(s ^ {\prime}, a ^ {\prime}\right) (685) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} d _ {s} ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) \sum_ {a ^ {\prime}} \pi^ {\prime} \left(a ^ {\prime} \mid s ^ {\prime}\right) \cdot \left(q ^ {\pi} \left(s ^ {\prime}, a ^ {\prime}\right) - v ^ {\pi} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right)\right) (686) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} d _ {s} ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} (s ^ {\prime}) \sum_ {a ^ {\prime}} \pi^ {\prime} \left(a ^ {\prime} \mid s ^ {\prime}\right) \cdot a ^ {\pi} \left(s ^ {\prime}, a ^ {\prime}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

lemma 20 (second performance difference lemma). for any policies  $\pi$  and  $\pi'$ ,

$$
v ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} (\rho) - v ^ {\pi} (\rho) = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi} (s) \sum_ {a} \left(\pi^ {\prime} (a | s) - \pi (a | s)\right) \cdot q ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} (s, a). \tag {687}
$$

proof. according to the definition of value function,

$$
\begin{array}{l} v ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} (s) - v ^ {\pi} (s) = \sum_ {a} \pi^ {\prime} (a | s) \cdot q ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} (s, a) - \sum_ {a} \pi (a | s) \cdot q ^ {\pi} (s, a) (688) \\ = \sum_ {a} \left(\pi^ {\prime} (a | s) - \pi (a | s)\right) \cdot q ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} (s, a) + \sum_ {a} \pi (a | s) \cdot \left(q ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} (s, a) - q ^ {\pi} (s, a)\right) (689) \\ = \sum_ {a} \left(\pi^ {\prime} (a | s) - \pi (a | s)\right) \cdot q ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} (s, a) + \gamma \sum_ {a} \pi (a | s) \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} \mathcal {p} \left(s ^ {\prime} | s, a\right) \cdot \left[ v ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) - v ^ {\pi} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) \right] (690) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} d _ {s} ^ {\pi} (s ^ {\prime}) \sum_ {a ^ {\prime}} \left(\pi^ {\prime} \left(a ^ {\prime} \mid s ^ {\prime}\right) - \pi \left(a ^ {\prime} \mid s ^ {\prime}\right)\right) \cdot q ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} \left(s ^ {\prime}, a ^ {\prime}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

lemma 21 (value sub-optimality lemma). for any policy  $\pi$ ,

$$
v ^ {*} (\rho) - v ^ {\pi} (\rho) = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi} (s) \sum_ {a} \left(\pi^ {*} (a | s) - \pi (a | s)\right) \cdot q ^ {*} (s, a). \tag {691}
$$

proof. according to the second performance difference lemma of lemma 20, the result immediately holds.

lemma 22 (spectrum of h matrix). let  $\pi \in \delta (\mathcal{a})$ . denote  $h(\pi) = diag(\pi) - \pi \pi^{\top}$ . let

$$
\pi (1) \leq \pi (2) \leq \dots \leq \pi (k). \tag {692}
$$

denote the eigenvalues of  $h(\pi)$  as

$$
\lambda_ {1} \leq \lambda_ {2} \leq \dots \leq \lambda_ {k}. \tag {693}
$$

then we have,

$$
\lambda_ {1} = 0, \tag {694}
$$

$$
\pi (i - 1) \leq \lambda_ {i} \leq \pi (i), i = 2, 3, \dots , k. \tag {695}
$$

proof. according to golub (1973, section 5),

$$
\pi (1) - \pi^ {\top} \pi \leq \lambda_ {1} \leq \pi (1), \tag {696}
$$

$$
\pi (i - 1) \leq \lambda_ {i} \leq \pi (i), i = 2, 3, \dots , k. \tag {697}
$$

we show  $\lambda_1 = 0$ . note

$$
h (\pi) \mathbf {1} = \left(\operatorname {d i a g} (\pi) - \pi \pi^ {\top}\right) \mathbf {1} = \pi - \pi = 0 \cdot \mathbf {1}. \tag {698}
$$

thus  $\mathbf{1}$  is an eigenvector of  $h(\pi)$  which corresponds to eigenvalue 0. furthermore, for any vector  $x\in \mathbb{r}^k$

$$
x ^ {\top} h (\pi) x = \underset {a \sim \pi} {\mathbb {e}} [ x (a) ^ {2} ] - \left(\underset {a \sim \pi} {\mathbb {e}} [ x (a) ]\right) ^ {2} = \operatorname {v a r} _ {a \sim \pi} [ x (a) ] \geq 0, \tag {699}
$$

which means all the eigenvalues of  $h(\pi)$  are non-negative.

lemma 23. let  $\pi \in \delta (\mathcal{a})$ . denote  $h(\pi) = \mathrm{diag}(\pi) - \pi \pi^{\top}$ . for any vector  $x\in \mathbb{r}^{k}$ ,

$$
\left\| \left(\mathbf {i d} - h (\pi)\right) \left(x - \frac {x ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1}\right) \right\| _ {2} \leq \left(1 - \min  _ {a} \pi (a)\right) \cdot \left\| x - \frac {x ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {2}, \tag {700}
$$

$$
\left\| h (\pi) \left(x - \frac {x ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1}\right) \right\| _ {2} \geq \min  _ {a} \pi (a) \cdot \left\| x - \frac {x ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {2}. \tag {701}
$$

proof.  $x$  can be written as linear combination of eigenvectors of  $h(\pi)$

$$
x = a _ {1} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {k}} + a _ {2} v _ {2} + \dots + a _ {k} v _ {k} \tag {702}
$$

$$
= \frac {x ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} + a _ {2} v _ {2} + \dots + a _ {k} v _ {k}. \tag {703}
$$

since  $h(\pi)$  is symmetric,  $\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{k}}, v_2, \ldots, v_k\right\}$  are orthonormal. the last equation is because the representation is unique, and

$$
a _ {1} = x ^ {\top} \frac {\mathbf {1}}{\sqrt {k}} = \frac {x ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{\sqrt {k}}. \tag {704}
$$

denote

$$
x ^ {\prime} = x - \frac {x ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} = a _ {2} v _ {2} + \dots + a _ {k} v _ {k}. \tag {705}
$$

we have

$$
\left\| x ^ {\prime} \right\| _ {2} ^ {2} = a _ {2} ^ {2} + \dots + a _ {k} ^ {2}. \tag {706}
$$

on the other hand,

$$
(\mathbf {i d} - h (\pi)) x ^ {\prime} = a _ {2} \left(1 - \lambda_ {2}\right) v _ {2} + \dots + a _ {k} \left(1 - \lambda_ {k}\right) v _ {k}. \tag {707}
$$

therefore

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| (\mathbf {i d} - h (\pi)) x ^ {\prime} \right\| _ {2} = \left(a _ {2} ^ {2} (1 - \lambda_ {2}) ^ {2} + \dots + a _ {k} ^ {2} (1 - \lambda_ {k}) ^ {2}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} (708) \\ \leq \left(\left(a _ {2} ^ {2} + \dots + a _ {k} ^ {2}\right) \cdot \left(1 - \lambda_ {2}\right) ^ {2}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} (709) \\ = \left(1 - \lambda_ {2}\right) \cdot \| x ^ {\prime} \| _ {2} (710) \\ \leq \left(1 - \min  _ {a} \pi (a)\right) \cdot \| x ^ {\prime} \| _ {2}, (711) \\ \end{array}
$$

where the first inequality is by  $0 \leq \pi(1) \leq \lambda_2 \leq \dots \leq \lambda_k \leq \pi(k) \leq 1$ , and the last inequality is according to  $\lambda_2 \geq \pi(1) = \min_a \pi(a)$ , and both are shown in lemma 22. similarly,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| h (\pi) x ^ {\prime} \right\| _ {2} = \left(a _ {2} ^ {2} \lambda_ {2} ^ {2} + \dots + a _ {k} ^ {2} \lambda_ {k} ^ {2}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} (712) \\ \geq \left(\left(a _ {2} ^ {2} + \dots + a _ {k} ^ {2}\right) \cdot \lambda_ {2} ^ {2}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} (713) \\ = \lambda_ {2} \cdot \| x ^ {\prime} \| _ {2} (714) \\ \geq \min  _ {a} \pi (a) \cdot \| x ^ {\prime} \| _ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

lemma 24. let  $\pi_{\theta} = \mathrm{softmax}(\theta)$  and  $\pi_{\theta'} = \mathrm{softmax}(\theta')$ . then for any constant  $c \in \mathbb{r}$ ,

$$
\left\| \pi_ {\theta} - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\prime} \right\| _ {1} \leq \left\| \theta^ {\prime} - \theta - c \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {\infty}. \tag {715}
$$

proof. this results improves the results of  $\| \pi_{\theta} - \pi_{\theta^{\prime}}\|_{\infty}\leq 2\cdot \| \theta -\theta^{\prime}\|_{\infty}$  in xiao et al. (2019, lemma 5). according to the  $\ell_1$  norm strong convexity of negative entropy over probabilistic simplex, i.e., for any policies  $\pi ,\pi^{\prime}$

$$
\pi^ {\top} \log \pi \geq \pi^ {\prime} ^ {\top} \log \pi^ {\prime} + (\pi - \pi^ {\prime}) ^ {\top} \log \pi^ {\prime} + \frac {1}{2} \cdot \| \pi^ {\prime} - \pi \| _ {1} ^ {2}, \tag {716}
$$

we have (letting  $\pi = \pi_{\theta}$ , and  $\pi' = \pi_{\theta'}$ ),

$$
d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi_ {\theta} \| \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}}\right) = \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} \log \pi_ {\theta} - \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}} ^ {\top} \log \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}} - \left(\pi_ {\theta} - \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}}\right) ^ {\top} \log \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}} \geq \frac {1}{2} \cdot \| \pi_ {\theta} - \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}} ^ {\prime} \| _ {1} ^ {2}, \tag {717}
$$

which is the pinsker's inequality. then we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \pi_ {\theta} - \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\prime} \right\| _ {1} \leq \sqrt {2 \cdot d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi_ {\theta} \mid \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}}\right)} (718) \\ \leq \sqrt {2 \cdot \frac {1}{2} \cdot \| \theta^ {\prime} - \theta - c \cdot \mathbf {1} \| _ {\infty} ^ {2}} \quad (\text {b y}) (719) \\ = \left\| \theta^ {\prime} - \theta - c \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {\infty}. \\ \end{array}
$$

lemma 25 (soft performance difference lemma). for any policies  $\pi$  and  $\pi'$ ,

$$
\tilde {v} ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} (\rho) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi} (\rho) = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi} (s) \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a} \left(\pi^ {\prime} (a | s) - \pi (a | s)\right) \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} (s, a) - \tau \log \pi^ {\prime} (a | s) \right] + \tau \cdot d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi (\cdot | s) \| \pi^ {\prime} (\cdot | s)\right) \right]. \tag {720}
$$

proof. according to the definition of soft value function,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde {v} ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} (s) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi} (s) = \sum_ {a} \pi^ {\prime} (a | s) \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} (s, a) - \tau \log \pi^ {\prime} (a | s) \right] - \sum_ {a} \pi (a | s) \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi} (s, a) - \tau \log \pi (a | s) \right] (721) \\ = \sum_ {a} \left(\pi^ {\prime} (a | s) - \pi (a | s)\right) \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} (s, a) - \tau \log \pi^ {\prime} (a | s) \right] + \sum_ {a} \pi (a | s) \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} (s, a) - \tau \log \pi^ {\prime} (a | s) - \tilde {q} ^ {\pi} (s, a) + \tau \log \pi (a | s) \right] (722) \\ = \sum_ {a} \left(\pi^ {\prime} (a | s) - \pi (a | s)\right) \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} (s, a) - \tau \log \pi^ {\prime} (a | s) \right] + \tau d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi (\cdot | s) \| \pi^ {\prime} (\cdot | s)\right) + \gamma \sum_ {a} \pi (a | s) \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} \mathcal {p} \left(s ^ {\prime} | s, a\right) \cdot \left[ \tilde {v} ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) \right] (723) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} d _ {s} ^ {\pi} (s ^ {\prime}) \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a ^ {\prime}} \left(\pi^ {\prime} (a ^ {\prime} | s ^ {\prime}) - \pi (a ^ {\prime} | s ^ {\prime})\right) \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi^ {\prime}} (s ^ {\prime}, a ^ {\prime}) - \tau \log \pi^ {\prime} (a ^ {\prime} | s ^ {\prime}) \right] + \tau \cdot d _ {\mathrm {k l}} (\pi (\cdot | s ^ {\prime}) \| \pi^ {\prime} (\cdot | s ^ {\prime})) \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

lemma 26 (soft sub-optimality lemma). for any policy  $\pi$

$$
\tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (\rho) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi} (\rho) = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} \left[ d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi} (s) \cdot \tau \cdot d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi (\cdot | s) \| \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (\cdot | s)\right) \right]. \tag {724}
$$

proof. according to nachum et al. (2017, theorem 1),  $\forall (s,a)$

$$
\tau \log \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (a | s) = \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (s, a) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (s). \tag {725}
$$

according to the soft performance difference lemma of lemma 25,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (s) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi} (s) = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} d _ {s} ^ {\pi} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a ^ {\prime}} \left(\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} \left(a ^ {\prime} \mid s ^ {\prime}\right) - \pi \left(a ^ {\prime} \mid s ^ {\prime}\right)\right) \cdot \left[ \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} \left(s ^ {\prime}, a ^ {\prime}\right) - \tau \log \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} \left(a ^ {\prime} \mid s ^ {\prime}\right) \right] + \tau \cdot d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi \left(\cdot \mid s ^ {\prime}\right) \| \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (\cdot \mid s ^ {\prime})\right) \right] (726) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} d _ {s} ^ {\pi} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a ^ {\prime}} \left(\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} \left(a ^ {\prime} \mid s ^ {\prime}\right) - \pi \left(a ^ {\prime} \mid s ^ {\prime}\right)\right) \cdot \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) + \tau \cdot d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi (\cdot \mid s ^ {\prime}) \| \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (\cdot \mid s ^ {\prime})\right) \right] \quad (\text {b y e q . (7 2 5)}) (727) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} d _ {s} ^ {\pi} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) \cdot \left[ (1 - 1) \cdot \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) + \tau \cdot d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi (\cdot | s ^ {\prime}) \| \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} (\cdot | s ^ {\prime})\right) \right] (728) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} \left[ d _ {s} ^ {\pi} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) \cdot \tau \cdot d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi \left(\cdot \mid s ^ {\prime}\right) \| \pi_ {\tau} ^ {*} \left(\cdot \mid s ^ {\prime}\right)\right) \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

lemma 27 (kl-logit inequality). let  $\pi_{\theta} = \mathrm{softmax}(\theta)$  and  $\pi_{\theta'} = \mathrm{softmax}(\theta')$ . then for any constant  $c \in \mathbb{r}$ ,

$$
d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi_ {\theta} \| \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}}\right) \leq \frac {1}{2} \cdot \| \theta^ {\prime} - \theta - c \cdot \mathbf {1} \| _ {\infty} ^ {2}. \tag {729}
$$

in particular, let  $c = \frac{(\theta' - \theta)^{\top} \mathbf{1}}{k}$ , we have

$$
d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi_ {\theta} \| \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}}\right) \leq \frac {1}{2} \cdot \left\| \theta^ {\prime} - \theta - \frac {\left(\theta^ {\prime} - \theta\right) ^ {\top} \mathbf {1}}{k} \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {2}. \tag {730}
$$

proof. according to the  $\ell_1$  norm strong convexity of negative entropy over probabilistic simplex, i.e., for any policies  $\pi$ ,  $\pi'$

$$
\pi^ {\prime} ^ {\top} \log \pi^ {\prime} \geq \pi^ {\top} \log \pi + (\pi^ {\prime} - \pi) ^ {\top} \log \pi + \frac {1}{2} \cdot \| \pi - \pi^ {\prime} \| _ {1} ^ {2}, \tag {731}
$$

we have (letting  $\pi = \pi_{\theta}$ , and  $\pi' = \pi_{\theta'}$ ),

$$
\begin{array}{l} d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi_ {\theta} \| \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}}\right) = \pi_ {\theta} ^ {\top} \log \pi_ {\theta} - \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}} ^ {\top} \log \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}} - \left(\pi_ {\theta} - \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}}\right) ^ {\top} \log \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}} (732) \\ \leq \left(\pi_ {\theta} - \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}}\right) ^ {\top} \log \pi_ {\theta} - \frac {1}{2} \cdot \left\| \pi_ {\theta} - \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}} \right\| _ {1} ^ {2} - \left(\pi_ {\theta} - \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}}\right) ^ {\top} \log \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}} (733) \\ = \left(\pi_ {\theta} - \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}}\right) ^ {\top} \left(\log \pi_ {\theta} - \log \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}}\right) - \frac {1}{2} \cdot \| \pi_ {\theta} - \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}} \| _ {1} ^ {2} (734) \\ = \left(\pi_ {\theta} - \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}}\right) ^ {\top} \left[ \theta - \theta^ {\prime} - \left(\log \sum_ {a} \exp \{\theta (a) \} - \log \sum_ {a} \exp \left\{\theta^ {\prime} (a) \right\}\right) \cdot \mathbf {1} \right] - \frac {1}{2} \cdot \| \pi_ {\theta} - \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}} \| _ {1} ^ {2} (735) \\ = \left(\pi_ {\theta} - \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}}\right) ^ {\top} \left(\theta - \theta^ {\prime}\right) - \frac {1}{2} \cdot \| \pi_ {\theta} - \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}} \| _ {1} ^ {2} (736) \\ = \left(\pi_ {\theta} - \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}}\right) ^ {\top} \left(\theta - \theta^ {\prime} - c \cdot \mathbf {1}\right) - \frac {1}{2} \cdot \| \pi_ {\theta} - \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}} \| _ {1} ^ {2} \quad \left(\text {s i n c e} \left(\pi_ {\theta} - \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}}\right) ^ {\top} c \cdot \mathbf {1} = 0 \text {h o l d s} \forall c \in \mathbb {r}\right) (737) \\ \leq \left\| \theta - \theta^ {\prime} - c \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {\infty} \cdot \left\| \pi_ {\theta} - \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}} \right\| _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} \cdot \left\| \pi_ {\theta} - \pi_ {\theta^ {\prime}} \right\| _ {1} ^ {2} \quad (\text {b y h o l d e r s i n e q u a l i t y}) (738) \\ \leq \frac {1}{2} \cdot \| \theta - \theta^ {\prime} - c \cdot \mathbf {1} \| _ {\infty} ^ {2}, (739) \\ \end{array}
$$

where the last inequality is according to  $ax - bx^2 \leq \frac{a^2}{4b}, \forall a, b > 0$ .

![](images/83e61f70a67346d0f206bf70fa1cf5c3d63e5aca10935ab3fafea9eee1f3d7a1.jpg)

lemma 28 (reversed łojasiewicz). denote  $\delta^{*}(s) = q^{*}(s, a^{*}(s)) - \max_{a \neq a^{*}(s)} q^{*}(s, a) > 0$  as the optimal value gap of state  $s$ , where  $a^{*}(s)$  is the action that the optimal policy selects under state  $s$ , and  $\delta^{*} = \min_{s \in s} \delta^{*}(s) > 0$  as the optimal value gap of the mdp. then we have,

$$
\left\| \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta} \right\| _ {2} \leq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \frac {\sqrt {2}}{\delta^ {*}} \cdot \left[ v ^ {*} (\mu) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu) \right]. \tag {740}
$$

proof. denote  $\delta^{*}(s,a) = q^{*}(s,a^{*}(s)) - q^{*}(s,a)$ , and  $\delta^{*}(s) = \min_{a\neq a^{*}(s)}\delta^{*}(s,a)$ . we have,

$$
\begin{array}{l} v ^ {*} (\mu) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu) = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \sum_ {a} \left(\pi^ {*} (a | s) - \pi_ {\theta} (a | s)\right) \cdot q ^ {*} (s, a) \quad \text {(b y} (741) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot q ^ {*} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) - \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot q ^ {*} (s, a) \right] (742) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot q ^ {*} (s, a ^ {*} (s)) - \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot q ^ {*} (s, a) \right] (743) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \delta^ {*} (s, a) \right] (744) \\ \geq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \right] \cdot \delta^ {*} (s). (745) \\ \end{array}
$$

since  $q^{\pi_{\theta}}(s,a)\in [0,1 / (1 - \gamma)]$ , and  $v^{\pi_{\theta}}(s)\in [0,1 / (1 - \gamma)]$ , we have  $|a^{\pi_{\theta}}(s,a)|\in [0,1 / (1 - \gamma)]$ . also,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| a ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) \right| = \left| q ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) - \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot q ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) \right| (746) \\ = \left| \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \left[ q ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) - q ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) \right] \right| (747) \\ \leq \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \cdot \left| q ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) - q ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) \right| \quad (\text {b y}) (748) \\ \leq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s). \quad \left(\text {b e c a u s e} q ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) \in [ 0, 1 / (1 - \gamma) ]\right) (749) \\ \end{array}
$$

therefore the  $\ell_2$  norm of gradient can be upper bounded as

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta} \right\| _ {2} = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \left[ \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) ^ {2} \sum_ {a} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) ^ {2} \cdot a ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) ^ {2} \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}} (750) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \left[ \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) ^ {2} \cdot \left(\pi_ {\theta} \left(a ^ {*} (s) | s\right) ^ {2} \cdot a ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} \left(s, a ^ {*} (s)\right) ^ {2} + \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) ^ {2} \cdot a ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, a) ^ {2}\right) \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}} (751) \\ \leq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \left[ \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) ^ {2} \cdot \left(1 \cdot \frac {1}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}} \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \right] ^ {2} + \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) ^ {2} \cdot \frac {1}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}}\right) \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}} (752) \\ \leq \frac {1}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}} \cdot \left[ \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) ^ {2} \cdot 2 \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \right] ^ {2} \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \quad (\text {b y} \| x \| _ {2} \leq \| x \| _ {1}) (753) \\ \leq \frac {1}{(1 - \gamma) ^ {2}} \cdot \sqrt {2} \cdot \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \right]. \quad (\text {b y} \| x \| _ {2} \leq \| x \| _ {1}) (754) \\ \end{array}
$$

combining the results, we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \frac {\partial v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu)}{\partial \theta} \right\| _ {2} \leq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \sqrt {2} \cdot \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \right] (755) \\ = \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \frac {\sqrt {2}}{\delta^ {*}} \cdot \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \right] \cdot \delta^ {*} (756) \\ \leq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \frac {\sqrt {2}}{\delta^ {*}} \cdot \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \sum_ {s} d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \left[ \sum_ {a \neq a ^ {*} (s)} \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) \right] \cdot \delta^ {*} (s) \quad (\text {b y} \delta^ {*} \leq \delta^ {*} (s) h o l d s f o r a l l s) (757) \\ \leq \frac {1}{1 - \gamma} \cdot \frac {\sqrt {2}}{\delta^ {*}} \cdot \left[ v ^ {*} (\mu) - v ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\mu) \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

# c. sub-optimality guarantees for other entropy-based rl methods

some interesting insight worth mentioning in the proof of lemma 15 is that the intermediate results provide sub-optimality guarantees for existing entropy regularized rl methods. in particular, eqs. (513) and (523) provides policy improvement guarantee for soft actor-critic (haarnoja et al., 2018, sac), and eqs. (524) and (529) provide sub-optimality guarantees for patch consistency learning (nachum et al., 2017, pcl).

remark 6 (soft policy improvement inequality). in haarnoja et al. (2018, eq. (4) and lemma 2), the policy is updated by

$$
\pi_ {\theta_ {t + 1}} = \underset {\pi_ {\theta}} {\arg \min } d _ {\mathrm {k l}} \left(\pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s) \| \frac {\exp \left\{q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s , \cdot) \right\}}{\sum_ {a} \exp \left\{q ^ {\pi_ {\theta_ {t}}} (s , a) \right\}}\right), \tag {758}
$$

which is exactly the kl divergence in eq. (523), with  $\bar{\pi}_{\theta}(\cdot |s)$  defined in eq. (513). the soft policy improvement inequality of eq. (523) guarantees that if the soft policy improvement is small, then the sub-optimality is small.

remark 7 (path inconsistency inequality). in nachum et al. (2017, theorems 1 and 3), it is shown that

- (i) soft optimal policy  $\pi_{\tau}^{*}$  satisfies the consistency conditions eqs. (25) and (26);  
- (ii) for any policy  $\pi$  that satisfies the consistency conditions, i.e., if  $\forall s, a$ ,

$$
\pi (a | s) = \exp \left\{\left(\tilde {q} ^ {\pi} (s, a) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi} (s)\right) / \tau \right\}, \quad a n d \quad \tilde {v} ^ {\pi} (s) = \tau \log \sum_ {a} \exp \left\{\tilde {q} ^ {\pi} (s, a) / \tau \right\}, \tag {759}
$$

then  $\pi = \pi_{\tau}^{*}$ , and  $\tilde{v}^{\pi} = \tilde{v}^{\pi_{\tau}}$

however, nachum et al. (2017) does not show if the consistency is violated during learning, how the violation is related to the sub-optimality. to see why lemma 15 provides insight, define the following "path inconsistency",

$$
r (s, a) + \gamma \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} \mathcal {p} \left(s ^ {\prime} \mid s, a\right) \tilde {v} ^ {\pi} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi} (s) - \tau \log \pi (a \mid s) = \tilde {q} ^ {\pi} (s, a) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi} (s) - \tau \log \pi (a \mid s), \tag {760}
$$

which captures the violation of consistency conditions during learning. note that for softmax policy  $\pi_{\theta}(\cdot |s) =$  softmax  $(\theta (s,\cdot))$  , the r.h.s. of eq. (760) can be written in vector form as

$$
\tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, \cdot) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \mathbf {1} - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s) = \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, \cdot) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \mathbf {1} - \tau \theta (s, \cdot) + \tau \log \sum_ {a} \exp \left\{\theta (s, a) \right\} \cdot \mathbf {1}. \tag {761}
$$

denote  $c_{\theta}(s) = \frac{\tilde{v}^{\pi_{\theta}}(s)}{\tau} - \log \sum_{a} \exp \{\theta(s, a)\}$ , and using lemma 27 in the proof of lemma 15, in particular, eq. (524),

$$
d _ {\mathrm {k l}} (\pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s) \| \bar {\pi} _ {\theta} (\cdot | s)) \leq \frac {1}{2} \cdot \left\| \frac {\tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s , \cdot)}{\tau} - \theta (s, \cdot) - c _ {\theta} (s) \cdot \mathbf {1} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {2} = \frac {1}{2 \tau^ {2}} \cdot \left\| \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, \cdot) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \mathbf {1} - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s) \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {2}.
$$

using the above results in eq. (529),

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left[ \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}} (\rho) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (\rho) \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \leq \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 - \gamma}} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \tau}} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \sum_ {s} \sqrt {d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)} \cdot \left\| \tilde {q} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s, \cdot) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \cdot \mathbf {1} - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta} (\cdot | s) \right\| _ {\infty} (762) \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 - \gamma}} \cdot \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \tau}} \cdot \left\| \frac {d _ {\rho} ^ {\pi_ {\tau} ^ {*}}}{d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}}} \right\| _ {\infty} ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \sum_ {s} \sqrt {d _ {\mu} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s)} \cdot \max  _ {a} \left| r (s, a) + \gamma \sum_ {s ^ {\prime}} \mathcal {p} \left(s ^ {\prime} \mid s, a\right) \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} \left(s ^ {\prime}\right) - \tau \log \pi_ {\theta} (a | s) - \tilde {v} ^ {\pi_ {\theta}} (s) \right|, (763) \\ \end{array}
$$

where (square of)  $\left|r(s,a) + \gamma \sum_{s'} \mathcal{p}(s'|s,a) \tilde{v}^{\pi_\theta}(s') - \tau \log \pi_\theta(a|s) - \tilde{v}^{\pi_\theta}(s)\right|$  is exactly the (one-step) path inconsistency objective used in pcl (nachum et al., 2017, eq. (14)). therefore, minimizing path inconsistency guarantees small sub-optimality. the path inconsistency inequality of eq. (762) implies path consistency of nachum et al. (2017).

# d. simulation results

to verify the convergence rates in the main paper, we conducted experiments on one-state mdps, which have  $k$  actions, with randomly generated reward  $r \in [0,1]^k$ , and randomly initialized policy  $\pi_{\theta_1}$ .

# d.1. softmax policy gradient

$k = 20$ ,  $r \in [0,1]^k$  is randomly generated, and  $\pi_{\theta_1}$  is randomly initialized. softmax policy gradient, i.e., update 1 is used with learning rate  $\eta = 2/5$  and  $t = 3 \times 10^5$ . as shown in fig. 2(a), the sub-optimality  $\delta_t = (\pi^* - \pi_{\theta_t})^\top r$  approaches 0. subfigures (b) and (c) show  $\log \delta_t$  as a function of  $\log t$ . as  $\log t$  increases, the slope is approaching  $-1$ , indicating that  $\log \delta_t = -\log t + c$ , which is equivalent to  $\delta_t = c' / t$ . subfigure (d) shows  $\pi_{\theta_t}(a^*)$  as a function of  $t$ .

# d.2. entropy regularized softmax policy gradient

$k = 20$ ,  $r \in [0,1]^k$  and  $\pi_{\theta_1}$  are the same as above. entropy regularized softmax policy gradient, i.e., update 2 is used with temperature  $\tau = 0.2$ , learning rate  $\eta = 2/5$  and  $t = 5 \times 10^4$ . as shown in fig. 3(a), the soft sub-optimality  $\tilde{\delta}_t = \pi_\tau^{*^\top} (r - \tau \log \pi_\tau^*) - \pi_{\theta_t}^\top (r - \tau \log \pi_{\theta_t})$  approaches 0. subfigure (b) shows  $\log \tilde{\delta}_t$  as a function of  $t$ . as  $t$  increases, the curve approaches a straight line, indicating that  $\log \tilde{\delta}_t = -c_1 \cdot t + c_2$ , which is equivalent to  $\tilde{\delta}_t = c_2' / \exp \{c_1' \cdot t\}$ . subfigure (c) shows  $\zeta_t$  as defined in lemma 11 as a function of  $t$ , which verifies lemma 12. subfigure (d) shows  $\min_a \pi_{\theta_t}(a)$  as a function of  $t$ . as  $t$  increases,  $\min_a \pi_{\theta_t}(a)$  approaches constant values, which verifies lemma 13.

(a)  $\hat{\delta}_t = \pi_\tau^{\top}(r - \tau \log \pi_\tau^*) - \pi_{\theta_t}^\top (r - \tau \log \pi_{\theta_t})$

# d.3. "bad" initializations for softmax policy gradient (pg)

as illustrated in fig. 1, "bad" initializations lead to attraction toward sub-optimal corners and slowly escaping for softmax policy gradient. fig. 4 shows one example with  $k = 5$ . softmax policy gradient takes about  $8 \times 10^{6}$  iterations around a sub-optimal corner. while with entropy regularization ( $\tau = 0.2$ ), the convergence is significantly faster.

# d.4. decaying entropy regularization

we run entropy regularized policy gradient with decaying temperature  $\tau_{t} = \frac{\alpha\cdot\delta}{\log t}$  for  $t\geq 2$  , i.e., update 3. fig. 5 shows one example with  $k = 10$  and different  $\alpha$  values. the actual rate is  $o\left(\frac{1}{t^{-\mathrm{slope}}}\right)$  , and the partial rate in theorem 8 is  $o\left(\frac{1}{t^{1 / \alpha}}\right)$
